=== src\api\localClient.js ===
import Dexie from "dexie";

const BASE_TABLES = [
  "Campaign", "Act", "Scene", "NPC", "Location", "Clue", "RollTable", "GameSession", "Note",
];

const RELATION_TABLES = [...BASE_TABLES, "NPCRelation"];
const VERSION_3_TABLES = [...RELATION_TABLES, "GameSheetModel", "GameCharacter", "SkillCatalog", "EquipmentCatalog"];
export const TABLES = [...VERSION_3_TABLES, "SessionPoll"];

export const LOCAL_USER = { id: "local-user", email: "local@arcanes", full_name: "MJ", role: "admin" };

export const db = new Dexie("arcanes_scenario");
// La version 1 doit rester dÃ©clarÃ©e : elle permet de mettre Ã  niveau les bases existantes.
db.version(1).stores(Object.fromEntries(BASE_TABLES.map((t) => [t, "id, campaign_id"])));
db.version(2).stores(Object.fromEntries(RELATION_TABLES.map((t) => [t, "id, campaign_id"])));
db.version(3).stores(Object.fromEntries(VERSION_3_TABLES.map((t) => [t, "id, campaign_id"])));
db.version(4).stores(Object.fromEntries(TABLES.map((t) => [t, "id, campaign_id"])));

// Demande au navigateur de ne pas effacer les donnÃ©es automatiquement (au mieux).
try {
  navigator.storage?.persist?.();
} catch {
  /* ignorÃ© */
}

const uid = () =>
  globalThis.crypto?.randomUUID?.() ||
  `${Date.now().toString(36)}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)}`;

const matchValue = (actual, expected) => {
  if (expected && typeof expected === "object" && !Array.isArray(expected)) {
    return Object.entries(expected).every(([op, v]) => {
      switch (op) {
        case "$gte": return actual != null && String(actual) >= String(v);
        case "$gt": return actual != null && String(actual) > String(v);
        case "$lte": return actual != null && String(actual) <= String(v);
        case "$lt": return actual != null && String(actual) < String(v);
        case "$ne": return actual !== v;
        case "$in": return Array.isArray(v) && v.includes(actual);
        case "$regex": {
          if (actual == null) return false;
          try {
            return new RegExp(v, expected.$options || "").test(String(actual));
          } catch {
            return String(actual).toLowerCase().includes(String(v).toLowerCase());
          }
        }
        default: return true;
      }
    });
  }
  if (expected === false) return actual === false || actual == null;
  return actual === expected;
};

const matches = (row, query) => Object.entries(query || {}).every(([k, v]) => matchValue(row[k], v));

const compare = (a, b) => {
  if (a == null && b == null) return 0;
  if (a == null) return 1;
  if (b == null) return -1;
  if (typeof a === "number" && typeof b === "number") return a - b;
  return String(a).localeCompare(String(b), "fr", { numeric: true });
};

const sortRows = (rows, sort) => {
  if (!sort || typeof sort !== "string") return rows;
  const desc = sort.startsWith("-");
  const field = desc ? sort.slice(1) : sort;
  return [...rows].sort((x, y) => (desc ? -1 : 1) * compare(x[field], y[field]));
};

const makeEntity = (name) => {
  const table = db.table(name);

  const select = async (query = {}) => {
    const rows =
      typeof query.campaign_id === "string"
        ? await table.where("campaign_id").equals(query.campaign_id).toArray()
        : await table.toArray();
    return rows.filter((row) => matches(row, query));
  };

  const notFound = (id) =>
    Object.assign(new Error(`Entity ${name} with ID ${id} not found`), { status: 404 });

  return {
    async filter(query = {}, options = {}) {
      const opts = typeof options === "string" ? { sort: options } : options || {};
      let items = sortRows(await select(query), opts.sort);
      if (opts.limit) items = items.slice(0, opts.limit);
      return { items };
    },
    async list(sort, limit) {
      let items = sortRows(await table.toArray(), sort);
      if (limit) items = items.slice(0, limit);
      return items;
    },
    async get(id) {
      const row = await table.get(id);
      if (!row) throw notFound(id);
      return row;
    },
    async create(data) {
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const row = {
        ...data,
        id: uid(),
        created_date: now,
        updated_date: now,
        created_by_id: LOCAL_USER.id,
        created_by: LOCAL_USER.email,
      };
      await table.add(row);
      return row;
    },
    async update(id, data) {
      const existing = await table.get(id);
      if (!existing) throw notFound(id);
      const row = { ...existing, ...data, id, updated_date: new Date().toISOString() };
      await table.put(row);
      return row;
    },
    async delete(id) {
      await table.delete(id);
      return { success: true };
    },
    async deleteMany(query = {}) {
      const rows = await select(query);
      await table.bulkDelete(rows.map((r) => r.id));
      return { deleted: rows.length };
    },
    async aggregate({ query = {}, groupBy } = {}) {
      const rows = await select(query);
      const counts = new Map();
      rows.forEach((row) => counts.set(row[groupBy], (counts.get(row[groupBy]) || 0) + 1));
      return { rows: [...counts].map(([key, count]) => ({ [groupBy]: key, count })) };
    },
  };
};

const readAsDataUrl = (file) =>
  new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => resolve(reader.result);
    reader.onerror = () => reject(reader.error);
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });

export const api = {
  entities: Object.fromEntries(TABLES.map((t) => [t, makeEntity(t)])),
  integrations: {
    Core: {
      // Le fichier est stockÃ© dans la base locale sous forme de donnÃ©es (data URL).
      UploadFile: async ({ file }) => ({ file_url: await readAsDataUrl(file) }),
    },
  },
  auth: {
    me: async () => LOCAL_USER,
    isAuthenticated: async () => true,
    logout: () => { },
    redirectToLogin: () => { },
  },
};

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import {
  eachDayOfInterval,
  endOfMonth,
  endOfWeek,
  format,
  isSameMonth,
  isToday,
  startOfMonth,
  startOfWeek,
} from "date-fns";
import { sessionStatusMeta } from "@/lib/sessionMeta";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

const WEEK_DAYS = ["Lun", "Mar", "Mer", "Jeu", "Ven", "Sam", "Dim"];

export default function MonthGrid({
  month,
  sessionsByDate,
  selectedDate,
  onSelectDay,
  onSelectSession,
  onAddSession,
}) {
  const start = startOfWeek(startOfMonth(month), { weekStartsOn: 1 });
  const end = endOfWeek(endOfMonth(month), { weekStartsOn: 1 });
  const days = eachDayOfInterval({ start, end });

  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border bg-card/50">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-7 border-b border-border">
        {WEEK_DAYS.map((day) => (
          <div
            key={day}
            className="px-1 py-2 text-center font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground"
          >
            {day}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-7">
        {days.map((day, index) => {
          const key = format(day, "yyyy-MM-dd");
          const daySessions = sessionsByDate[key] || [];
          const outside = !isSameMonth(day, month);
          const lastColumn = (index + 1) % 7 === 0;
          const expanded = selectedDate === key;

          return (
            <div
              key={key}
              data-expandable-card={`calendar-day:${key}`}
              aria-expanded={expanded}
              role="button"
              tabIndex={0}
              onClick={(event) => {
                event.stopPropagation();
                onSelectDay(day);
              }}
              onKeyDown={(e) => {
                if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  onSelectDay(day);
                }
              }}
              className={cn(
                "min-h-[74px] cursor-pointer border-b border-border p-1.5 text-left align-top transition-[background-color,box-shadow] hover:bg-secondary/40 sm:min-h-[116px]",
                !lastColumn && "border-r",
                outside && "bg-background/40",
                expanded && "relative z-10 rounded-lg bg-card shadow-lg ring-2 ring-primary/60"
              )}
              aria-expanded={expanded}
              aria-label={`${format(day, "d MMMM yyyy")} : ${daySessions.length} session${daySessions.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span
                  className={cn(
                    "font-mono text-sm",
                    isToday(day)
                      ? "flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary text-primary-foreground sm:h-5 sm:w-5"
                      : outside
                        ? "text-muted-foreground/50"
                        : "text-muted-foreground"
                  )}
                >
                  {format(day, "d")}
                </span>
                {daySessions.length > 0 && (
                  <span className="font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    {daySessions.length}
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="mt-1 space-y-1">
                {(expanded ? daySessions : daySessions.slice(0, 3)).map((session) => {
                  const meta = sessionStatusMeta(session.status);
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={session.id}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={(e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        onSelectSession(session);
                      }}
                      className={cn(
                        "min-touch flex w-full items-center gap-1.5 rounded border px-1.5 py-1 text-left text-xs transition-opacity hover:opacity-80",
                        meta.badge
                      )}
                    >
                      <span className={cn("h-1.5 w-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full", meta.dot)} />
                      {session.time && (
                        <span className="shrink-0 font-mono text-xs">{session.time}</span>
                      )}
                      <span className="hidden truncate sm:inline">{session.title}</span>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
                {daySessions.length > 3 && (
                  <span className="block px-1 font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    +{daySessions.length - 3}
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
              {expanded && (
                <div className="mt-2 space-y-2 border-t border-border pt-2">
                  {daySessions.length === 0 ? (
                    <p className="px-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Aucune session prÃ©vue ce jour.</p>
                  ) : (
                    daySessions.map((session) => {
                      const meta = sessionStatusMeta(session.status);
                      return (
                        <button
                          key={`detail-${session.id}`}
                          type="button"
                          onClick={(event) => {
                            event.stopPropagation();
                            onSelectSession(session);
                          }}
                          className="block w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-background/80 p-2 text-left hover:border-primary/50"
                        >
                          <span className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-1">
                            <span className="font-display text-xs font-semibold text-foreground">
                              {session.time ? `${session.time} Â· ` : ""}{session.title}
                            </span>
                            <span className={cn("tag border text-[10px]", meta.badge)}>{meta.label}</span>
                          </span>
                          {session.location && <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-muted-foreground">{session.location}</span>}
                          {session.notes && <span className="mt-1 block whitespace-pre-wrap text-xs text-muted-foreground">{session.notes}</span>}
                        </button>
                      );
                    })
                  )}
                  <Button
                    type="button"
                    size="sm"
                    variant="ghost"
                    className="h-8 w-full justify-start text-xs"
                    onClick={(event) => {
                      event.stopPropagation();
                      onAddSession(day);
                    }}
                  >
                    + Planifier une session
                  </Button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function SessionDialog({
  open,

  form,
  setForm,
  scenes,
  saving,
  onSave,
  isEditing,
  onDelete,
}) {
  const footer = (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
      <div>
        {isEditing && onDelete && (
          <Button
            variant="ghost"

            className="text-destructive hover:text-destructive"

        )}
      </div>

          Annuler
        </Button>
        <Button onClick={onSave} disabled={saving || !form.title.trim() || !form.date}>
          {saving ? "Enregistrementâ€¦" : "Enregistrer"}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <ResponsiveDialog

      onOpenChange={onOpenChange}

      footer={footer}
    >

            />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label>Heure</Label>
            <Input
              type="time"
              value={form.time || ""}
              onChange={(e) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, time: e.target.value }))}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">

            <Label>Lieu</Label>
            <Input
              value={form.location || ""}
              onChange={(e) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, location: e.target.value }))}
              placeholder="Ex. Chez LÃ©a, en ligneâ€¦"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-2">

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { CalendarPlus, Copy, Mail, MessageCircle, Plus, RefreshCw, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import ResponsiveDialog from "@/components/common/ResponsiveDialog";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/use-toast";
import { api } from "@/api/localClient";
import { formatPollDateInput, toPollIsoDate } from "@/lib/pollDates";
import {
  getPollServiceUrl,
  normalizePollServiceUrl,
  pollRequest,
  refreshSessionPoll,
  savePollServiceUrl,
} from "@/lib/sessionPolls";

const newSlot = () => ({ date: "", time: "20:00" });
const AVAILABILITY_LABELS = {
  available: "Disponible",
  maybe: "Peut-Ãªtre",
  unavailable: "Indisponible",
};

const displaySlot = (slot, timezone) => {
  const wallTime = new Date(`${slot.date}T${slot.time}:00Z`);
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("fr-FR", {
    dateStyle: "medium",
    timeStyle: "short",
    timeZone: "UTC",
  }).format(wallTime) + (timezone ? ` (${timezone})` : "");
};

const normalizePlayer = (name) =>
  String(name || "").normalize("NFKC").trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ").toLocaleLowerCase("fr-FR");

export default function SessionPollDialog({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  campaignId,
  onPlanify,
  onPollChange,
}) {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [serviceUrl, setServiceUrl] = useState(getPollServiceUrl);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [comment, setComment] = useState("");
  const [playerNames, setPlayerNames] = useState("");
  const [slots, setSlots] = useState([newSlot(), newSlot()]);
  const [expiresOn, setExpiresOn] = useState("");
  const [polls, setPolls] = useState([]);
  const [busyPoll, setBusyPoll] = useState("");
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);

  const parsedPlayers = useMemo(
    () => playerNames.split(/\r?\n/).map((name) => name.normalize("NFKC").trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ")).filter(Boolean),
    [playerNames]
  );

  const saveServiceUrl = () => {
    const normalized = normalizePollServiceUrl(serviceUrl);
    savePollServiceUrl(normalized);
    setServiceUrl(normalized);
    return normalized;
  };

  const refreshPoll = async (poll) => {
    setBusyPoll(poll.id);
    try {
      const baseUrl = poll.service_url || saveServiceUrl();
      const updated = await refreshSessionPoll(poll, baseUrl);
      setPolls((current) => current.map((item) => item.id === poll.id ? updated : item));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Actualisation du sondage impossible :", error);
      toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "RÃ©sultats indisponibles", description: error.message });
    } finally {
      setBusyPoll("");
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open || !campaignId) return;
    let alive = true;
    api.entities.SessionPoll.filter({ campaign_id: campaignId }, { sort: "-created_date" })
      .then(async (result) => {
        if (!alive) return;
        setPolls(result.items);
        const baseUrl = getPollServiceUrl();
        for (const poll of result.items) {
          const pollUrl = poll.service_url || baseUrl;
          if (!pollUrl) continue;
          try {
            const updated = await refreshSessionPoll(poll, pollUrl);
            if (alive) setPolls((current) => current.map((item) => item.id === poll.id ? updated : item));
          } catch (error) {
            console.error(`Actualisation du sondage Â« ${poll.title} Â» impossible :`, error);
            if (alive) {
              setPolls((current) => current.map((item) =>
                item.id === poll.id ? { ...item, sync_error: error.message } : item
              ));
            }
          }
        }
      })
      .catch((error) => {
        console.error("Chargement des sondages locaux impossible :", error);
        toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Sondages indisponibles", description: "Les sondages locaux n'ont pas pu Ãªtre chargÃ©s." });
      });
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [open, campaignId, toast]);

  const createPoll = async () => {
    if (!campaignId) return;
    const normalizedPlayers = parsedPlayers.map(normalizePlayer);
    if (!title.trim() || title.trim().length > 120) {
      toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Titre invalide", description: "Le titre doit contenir de 1 Ã  120 caractÃ¨res." });
      return;
    }
    if (parsedPlayers.length === 0 || parsedPlayers.length > 40 ||
        new Set(normalizedPlayers).size !== normalizedPlayers.length) {
      toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Liste de joueurs invalide", description: "Indiquez de 1 Ã  40 noms uniques, un par ligne." });
      return;
    }
    if (slots.length < 2 || slots.some((slot) => !toPollIsoDate(slot.date) || !slot.time)) {
      toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Dates incomplÃ¨tes ou invalides", description: "Renseignez au moins deux dates au format jj/mm/aaaa et leurs heures." });
      return;
    }
    if (!toPollIsoDate(expiresOn)) {
      toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Date d'expiration invalide", description: "Choisissez une date valide au format jj/mm/aaaa jusqu'Ã  laquelle le sondage et ses rÃ©sultats resteront accessibles." });
      return;
    }
    setCreating(true);
    try {
      const baseUrl = saveServiceUrl();
      const normalizedSlots = slots.map((slot) => ({ ...slot, date: toPollIsoDate(slot.date) }));
      const uniqueSlots = new Set(normalizedSlots.map((slot) => `${slot.date}T${slot.time}`));
      if (uniqueSlots.size !== slots.length) throw new Error("Les propositions de date doivent Ãªtre uniques.");
      const created = await pollRequest(baseUrl, "/api/polls", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          title: title.trim(),
          comment: comment.trim(),
          players: parsedPlayers,
          slots: normalizedSlots,
          timezone: Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone || "UTC",
          expiresOn: toPollIsoDate(expiresOn),
        }),
      });
      const saved = await api.entities.SessionPoll.create({
        campaign_id: campaignId,
        poll_id: created.id,
        title: created.title,
        comment: created.comment,
        public_url: created.publicUrl,
        service_url: baseUrl,
        owner_token: created.ownerToken,
        timezone: created.timezone,
        expected_players: parsedPlayers,
        slots: [],
        votes: [],
        state: "open",
        expires_at: created.expiresAt,
      });
      setPolls((current) => [saved, ...current]);
      onPollChange?.(saved);
      setTitle("");
      setComment("");
      setPlayerNames("");
      setSlots([newSlot(), newSlot()]);
      setExpiresOn("");
      toast({ title: "Sondage crÃ©Ã©", description: "Le lien est prÃªt Ã  Ãªtre partagÃ© avec les joueurs." });
      await refreshPoll(saved);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("CrÃ©ation du sondage impossible :", error);
      toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Sondage non crÃ©Ã©", description: error.message || "VÃ©rifiez la connexion et la configuration Cloudflare." });
    } finally {
      setCreating(false);
    }
  };

  const copyLink = async (poll, forDiscord = false) => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(poll.public_url);
      toast({
        title: forDiscord ? "Lien copiÃ© pour Discord" : "Lien copiÃ©",
        description: forDiscord ? "Collez-le dans votre conversation Discord." : undefined,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Copie du lien impossible :", error);
      toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Copie impossible", description: "SÃ©lectionnez et copiez le lien affichÃ©." });
    }
  };

  const closePoll = async (poll) => {
    setBusyPoll(poll.id);
    try {
      await pollRequest(poll.service_url, `/api/polls/${poll.poll_id}/close`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${poll.owner_token}` },
        body: "{}",
      });
      const updated = await refreshSessionPoll({ ...poll, state: "closed" }, poll.service_url);
      setPolls((current) => current.map((item) => item.id === poll.id ? updated : item));
      onPollChange?.(updated);
      toast({ title: "Sondage clÃ´turÃ©", description: "Les joueurs ne peuvent plus modifier leur rÃ©ponse." });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("ClÃ´ture du sondage impossible :", error);
      toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "ClÃ´ture impossible", description: error.message });
    } finally {
      setBusyPoll("");
    }
  };

  const removePoll = async (poll) => {
    if (!window.confirm(`Supprimer le sondage Â« ${poll.title} Â» et ses rÃ©ponses en ligne ?`)) return;
    setBusyPoll(poll.id);
    try {
      await pollRequest(poll.service_url, `/api/polls/${poll.poll_id}`, {
        method: "DELETE",
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${poll.owner_token}` },
      });
      await api.entities.SessionPoll.delete(poll.id);
      setPolls((current) => current.filter((item) => item.id !== poll.id));
      onPollChange?.(null, poll.id);
      toast({ title: "Sondage supprimÃ©" });
    } catch (error) {
      if (error.status === 404) {
        try {
          await api.entities.SessionPoll.delete(poll.id);
          setPolls((current) => current.filter((item) => item.id !== poll.id));
          onPollChange?.(null, poll.id);
          toast({ title: "RÃ©fÃ©rence locale supprimÃ©e", description: "Le sondage distant n'existe plus ou a expirÃ©." });
        } catch (localError) {
          console.error("Suppression de la rÃ©fÃ©rence locale impossible :", localError);
          toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Suppression impossible", description: "La rÃ©fÃ©rence locale au sondage n'a pas pu Ãªtre supprimÃ©e." });
        }
        return;
      }
      console.error("Suppression du sondage impossible :", error);
      toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Suppression impossible", description: error.message });
    } finally {
      setBusyPoll("");
    }
  };

  const footer = (
    <div className="flex justify-end">
      <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}>Fermer</Button>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <ResponsiveDialog
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={onOpenChange}
      title="Sondages des joueurs"
      description="Les campagnes restent sur cet appareil. Seuls le sondage, les dates proposÃ©es, les noms et les disponibilitÃ©s sont envoyÃ©s Ã  Cloudflare."
      footer={footer}
    >
      <div className="max-h-[70vh] space-y-6 overflow-y-auto py-1">
        <section className="space-y-3 rounded-lg border border-border p-3">
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="poll-service-url">Adresse du site Cloudflare Pages</Label>
            <Input
              id="poll-service-url"
              type="url"
              placeholder="https://votre-projet.pages.dev"
              value={serviceUrl}
              onChange={(event) => setServiceUrl(event.target.value)}
              onBlur={() => {
                if (!serviceUrl.trim()) return;
                try {
                  const normalized = normalizePollServiceUrl(serviceUrl);
                  savePollServiceUrl(normalized);
                  setServiceUrl(normalized);
                } catch {
                  // La validation dÃ©taillÃ©e est affichÃ©e lors de l'enregistrement ou de la crÃ©ation.
                }
              }}
            />
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              Cette adresse est mÃ©morisÃ©e localement et peut Ãªtre remplacÃ©e par celle de votre dÃ©ploiement.
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="space-y-4 rounded-lg border border-border p-3">
          <h3 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-foreground">CrÃ©er un sondage</h3>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="poll-title-input">Nom de la session</Label>

            <Textarea

import React from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { CalendarPlus } from "lucide-react";
import { format, parseISO } from "date-fns";
import { fr } from "date-fns/locale";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { dateKey, sessionStatusMeta } from "@/lib/sessionMeta";

export default function UpcomingSessions({ sessions, onOpen, onAdd }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-card/50 p-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h2 className="font-display text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">Prochaines sessions</h2>
        <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={onAdd} className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-primary">
          <CalendarPlus className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" /> Planifier
        </Button>
      </div>

      {sessions.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="mt-3 rounded-lg border border-dashed border-border p-4 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          Aucune session Ã  venir. Cliquez sur une date du calendrier pour en planifier une.
        </p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="mt-3 space-y-2">
          {sessions.map((session) => {
            const meta = sessionStatusMeta(session.status);
            let label = "";
            try {
              label = format(parseISO(dateKey(session.date)), "EEE d MMM", { locale: fr });
            } catch {
              label = session.date;
            }
            return (
              <li key={session.id}>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onOpen(session)}
                  className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-border bg-background/60 p-3 text-left transition-colors hover:border-primary/40"
                >
                  <span className={cn("h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full", meta.dot)} />
                  <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <span className="block truncate font-display text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">
                      {session.title}
                    </span>
                    <span className="mt-0.5 block font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">
                      {label}
                      {session.time ? ` Â· ${session.time}` : ""}
                      {session.location ? ` Â· ${session.location}` : ""}
                    </span>
                  </span>
                </button>
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
=== src\components\codex\AttachmentsField.jsx ===
import React, { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { ExternalLink, FileText, Loader2, Paperclip, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { api } from "@/api/localClient";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/use-toast";
import { openAttachment } from "@/lib/openAttachment";

const MAX_MB = 10;

export const isImage = (file) => (file?.type || "").startsWith("image/");

export default function AttachmentsField({ value = [], onChange }) {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const inputRef = useRef(null);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);

  const handleFiles = async (event) => {
    const files = Array.from(event.target.files || []);
    event.target.value = "";
    if (files.length === 0) return;

    setUploading(true);
    const added = [];
    for (const file of files) {
      const isPdf = file.type === "application/pdf";
      if (!isPdf && !file.type.startsWith("image/")) {
        toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Format non pris en charge", description: `${file.name} : images et PDF uniquement.` });
        continue;
      }
      if (file.size > MAX_MB * 1024 * 1024) {
        toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Fichier trop lourd", description: `${file.name} dÃ©passe ${MAX_MB} Mo.` });
        continue;
      }
      try {
        const res = await api.integrations.Core.UploadFile({ file });
        added.push({ name: file.name, url: res.file_url, type: file.type });
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Ã‰chec du tÃ©lÃ©versement :", error);
        toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "TÃ©lÃ©versement impossible", description: file.name });
      }
    }
    if (added.length > 0) onChange([...(value || []), ...added]);
    setUploading(false);
  };

  const remove = (index) => onChange((value || []).filter((_, i) => i !== index));

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2 rounded-lg border border-border p-3">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <Label>Cartes et visuels ({(value || []).length})</Label>
        <Button type="button" size="sm" variant="outline" disabled={uploading} onClick={() => inputRef.current?.click()}>
          {uploading ? <Loader2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Paperclip className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />}
          {uploading ? "Envoiâ€¦" : "Ajouter un fichier"}
        </Button>
      </div>
      <input ref={inputRef} type="file" accept="image/*,application/pdf" multiple className="hidden" onChange={handleFiles} />
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Images ou PDF, {MAX_MB} Mo maximum par fichier.</p>

      {(value || []).length > 0 && (
        <ul className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 pt-1 sm:grid-cols-3">
          {value.map((file, index) => (
            <li key={`${file.url}-${index}`} className="group relative overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-border bg-secondary/40">
              <button type="button" onClick={() => openAttachment(file)} className="block w-full text-left" title={file.name}>
                {isImage(file) ? (
                  <img src={file.url} alt={file.name} className="h-24 w-full object-cover" loading="lazy" />
                ) : (
                  <div className="flex h-24 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 text-muted-foreground">
                    <FileText className="h-6 w-6" />
                    <span className="flex items-center text-xs">Ouvrir le PDF <ExternalLink className="ml-1 h-3 w-3" /></span>
                  </div>
                )}
                <span className="block truncate px-2 py-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{file.name}</span>
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-label={`Retirer ${file.name}`}
                onClick={() => remove(index)}
                className="absolute right-1 top-1 grid h-7 w-7 place-items-center rounded-md bg-background/80 text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive"
              >
                <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

            <Copy className="mr-1.5 h-3 w-3" /> Dupliquer
          </button>
        )}
        <button type="button" aria-label={`Supprimer ${entry.name}`} onClick={() => onDelete(kind, entry)} className="tag min-touch border border-border text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:border-destructive/40 hover:text-destructive">
          <Trash2 className="mr-1.5 h-3 w-3" /> Supprimer
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "@/api/localClient";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
import {
  Select,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import ResponsiveDialog from "@/components/common/ResponsiveDialog";
import AttachmentsField from "@/components/codex/AttachmentsField";
import NpcRelations from "@/components/codex/NpcRelations";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/use-toast";
import { KIND_META, defaultEntry, duplicateDraft } from "@/lib/codex";
import { NPC_ATTITUDE } from "@/lib/sceneMeta";
import { copyLocationSubtree, descendantIds, parentOptions } from "@/lib/locationTree";
import { copyNpcRelations, loadRelations } from "@/lib/npcRelations";

function CopyOption({ checked, onChange, title, hint }) {
  return (
    <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-3 rounded-lg border border-border p-3">
      <input
        type="checkbox"
        className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 accent-[hsl(var(--primary))]"
        checked={checked}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.checked)}
      />
      <span>
        <span className="block text-sm text-foreground">{title}</span>
        <span className="block text-xs text-muted-foreground">{hint}</span>
      </span>
    </label>
  );
}

export default function CodexEntryDialog({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  kind,
  campaignId,
  entry,
  onSaved,
  defaultParentId = "",
  duplicateOf = null,
  locations,
}) {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [form, setForm] = useState(defaultEntry(kind));
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [fetchedLocations, setFetchedLocations] = useState([]);
  const [copyRelations, setCopyRelations] = useState(true);
  const [copyChildren, setCopyChildren] = useState(true);
  const [sourceRelationCount, setSourceRelationCount] = useState(0);
  const allLocations = locations ?? fetchedLocations;
  const meta = KIND_META[kind] || KIND_META.npc;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    if (entry) setForm({ ...entry });
    else if (duplicateOf) setForm(duplicateDraft(kind, duplicateOf));
    else setForm({ ...defaultEntry(kind), parent_id: kind === "location" ? defaultParentId : "" });
    setCopyRelations(true);
    setCopyChildren(true);
  }, [open, kind, entry, defaultParentId, duplicateOf]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (locations || !open || (kind !== "location" && kind !== "npc") || !campaignId) return;
    let alive = true;
    api.entities.Location.filter({ campaign_id: campaignId }, { sort: "name", limit: 1000 }).then((res) => {
      if (alive) setFetchedLocations(res.items);
    });
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [locations, open, kind, campaignId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open || !duplicateOf || kind !== "npc" || !campaignId) {
      setSourceRelationCount(0);
      return;
    }
    let alive = true;
    loadRelations(campaignId).then((rows) => {
      if (alive) setSourceRelationCount(rows.filter((r) => r.from_id === duplicateOf.id).length);
    });
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [open, duplicateOf, kind, campaignId]);

  const locationChoices = useMemo(
    () => parentOptions(allLocations, kind === "location" ? entry?.id : null),
    [allLocations, kind, entry?.id]
  );

  const childCount = useMemo(
    () => (duplicateOf && kind === "location" ? descendantIds(allLocations, duplicateOf.id).length : 0),
    [duplicateOf, kind, allLocations]
  );

  const set = (key, value) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, [key]: value }));

  const save = async () => {
    if (!form.name?.trim() || !campaignId) return;
    setSaving(true);
    const payload = {
      campaign_id: campaignId,
      name: form.name.trim(),
      comment: form.comment?.trim() || "",
      attachments: form.attachments || [],
    };
    if (kind === "npc") {
      payload.role = form.role || "";
      payload.description = form.description || "";
      payload.attitude = form.attitude || "neutre";
      payload.location_id = form.location_id || "";
    } else if (kind === "location") {
      payload.type = form.type || "";
      payload.description = form.description || "";
      payload.parent_id = form.parent_id || "";
    } else {
      payload.description = form.description || "";
      payload.clue_type = form.clue_type === "info" ? "info" : "indice";
      payload.discovered = payload.clue_type === "indice" && !!form.discovered;
      payload.known = payload.clue_type === "info" && !!form.known;
      payload.used = payload.clue_type === "info" && !!form.used;
    }
    try {
      let created = null;
      if (entry?.id) await api.entities[meta.entity].update(entry.id, payload);
      else created = await api.entities[meta.entity].create(payload);

      if (created && duplicateOf) {
        try {
          if (kind === "npc" && copyRelations) await copyNpcRelations(campaignId, duplicateOf.id, created.id);
          if (kind === "location" && copyChildren)
            await copyLocationSubtree(allLocations, duplicateOf.id, created.id);
        } catch (copyError) {
          console.error("Copie incomplÃ¨te :", copyError);
          toast({
            variant: "destructive",
            title: "Copie incomplÃ¨te",
            description: "L'Ã©lÃ©ment a Ã©tÃ© crÃ©Ã©, mais ses relations ou sous-lieux n'ont pas tous Ã©tÃ© copiÃ©s.",
          });
        }
      }
      onOpenChange(false);
      onSaved();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Ã‰chec enregistrement :", error);
      toast({
        variant: "destructive",
        title: "Enregistrement impossible",
        description: "L'Ã©lÃ©ment n'a pas Ã©tÃ© enregistrÃ©.",
      });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const footer = (
    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
      <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}>
        Annuler
      </Button>
      <Button onClick={save} disabled={saving || !form.name?.trim()}>
        {saving ? "Enregistrementâ€¦" : duplicateOf ? "CrÃ©er la copie" : "Enregistrer"}
      </Button>
    </div>
  );

  const title = entry?.id
    ? `Modifier â€” ${meta.singular}`
    : duplicateOf
      ? `Dupliquer â€” ${meta.singular}`
      : `Nouveau ${meta.singular}`;

          : undefined
      }

      <div className="space-y-4 py-1">
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label>{meta.nameLabel}</Label>
          <Input value={form.name || ""} onChange={(e) => set("name", e.target.value)} />
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label>Commentaire (facultatif)</Label>
          <Input
            value={form.comment || ""}
            onChange={(e) => set("comment", e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Ex. nÂ°2, version corrompue, archÃ©type de gardeâ€¦"
          />
        </div>

          <CopyOption
            checked={copyRelations}
            onChange={setCopyRelations}
            title={`Copier aussi les relations (${sourceRelationCount})`}
            hint="Le PNJ dupliquÃ© aura les mÃªmes relations que l'original avec les autres PNJ. Son lieu d'attribution est conservÃ©."
          />

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "@/api/localClient";
import { Search, Plus, Link2, Link2Off, Pencil, ChevronDown, ChevronUp } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { ScrollArea } from "@/components/ui/scroll-area";
import { CODEX_TABS } from "@/lib/codex";
import { NPC_ATTITUDE } from "@/lib/sceneMeta";
import { isImage } from "@/components/codex/AttachmentsField";
import { openAttachment } from "@/lib/openAttachment";

export default function CodexSidebar({
  campaignId,
  activeScene,
  refreshKey,
  onToggleLink,
  onToggleClueFlag,
  onAdd,
  onEdit,
}) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("npc");
  const [term, setTerm] = useState("");
  const [linkedOnly, setLinkedOnly] = useState(false);
  const [items, setItems] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [expandedId, setExpandedId] = useState(null);

  const kind = CODEX_TABS.find((t) => t.key === tab) || CODEX_TABS[0];
  const linkedIds = activeScene ? activeScene[kind.linkKey] || [] : [];
  const linkedKey = linkedIds.join(",");

  useEffect(() => {
    setExpandedId(null);
  }, [tab, activeScene?.id]);

  const toggleClueFlag = async (item, field) => {
    const updated = { ...item, [field]: !item[field] };
    setItems((current) => current.map((entry) => entry.id === item.id ? updated : entry));
    const saved = await onToggleClueFlag(item, field, updated[field]);
    if (!saved) {
      setItems((current) => current.map((entry) => entry.id === item.id ? item : entry));
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!campaignId) {
      setItems([]);
      return;
    }
    let alive = true;
    const ids = activeScene ? activeScene[kind.linkKey] || [] : [];
    const run = async () => {
      setLoading(true);
      const query = { campaign_id: campaignId };
      const q = term.trim();
      if (q) query.name = { $regex: q, $options: "i" };
      if (linkedOnly) {
        if (ids.length === 0) {
          if (alive) {
            setItems([]);
            setLoading(false);
          }
          return;
        }
        query.id = { $in: ids };
      }
      const res = await api.entities[kind.entity].filter(query, { sort: "name", limit: 100 });
      if (!alive) return;
      setItems(res.items);
      setLoading(false);
    };
    const timer = setTimeout(run, term.trim() ? 250 : 0);
    return () => {
      alive = false;
      clearTimeout(timer);
    };
  }, [campaignId, tab, term, linkedOnly, refreshKey, linkedKey, kind.entity, kind.linkKey, activeScene]);

  return (
    <aside className="glass-panel flex max-h-[calc(100vh-3rem)] min-h-[420px] flex-col rounded-xl lg:sticky lg:top-6">
      <div className="border-b border-border p-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <h2 className="font-display text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">Codex du MJ</h2>
          <Button
            size="sm"
            variant="ghost"
            onClick={() => onAdd(tab)}
            disabled={!campaignId}
            className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-primary"
          >
            <Plus className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" /> Ajouter
          </Button>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground">
          {activeScene ? `ScÃ¨ne : ${activeScene.title}` : "Aucune scÃ¨ne sÃ©lectionnÃ©e"}
        </p>

        <div className="mt-3 flex gap-1 rounded-lg border border-border p-1">
          {CODEX_TABS.map((t) => (
            <button
              key={t.key}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setTab(t.key)}
              className={cn(
                "flex-1 rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-wider transition-colors",
                tab === t.key ? "bg-primary/15 text-primary" : "text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
              )}
            >
              {t.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="relative mt-3">
          <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground" />
          <Input
            value={term}
            onChange={(e) => setTerm(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Rechercherâ€¦"
            className="pl-9"
          />
        </div>

        {activeScene && (
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label={linkedOnly ? "Afficher tout le codex" : "N'afficher que les Ã©lÃ©ments liÃ©s Ã  la scÃ¨ne"}
            onClick={() => setLinkedOnly((v) => !v)}
            className={cn(
              "tag min-touch mt-3 border transition-colors",
              linkedOnly ? "border-primary/50 bg-primary/15 text-primary" : "border-border text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
            )}
          >
            {linkedOnly ? <Link2 className="mr-1.5 h-3 w-3" /> : <Link2Off className="mr-1.5 h-3 w-3" />}
            LiÃ©es Ã  la scÃ¨ne ({linkedIds.length})
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      <ScrollArea className="flex-1">
        <div className="space-y-2 p-3">
          {!campaignId && (
            <p className="p-4 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              CrÃ©ez une campagne pour constituer votre codex.
            </p>
          )}
          {campaignId && loading && (
            <p className="p-4 text-center font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground">
              Chargementâ€¦
            </p>
          )}
          {campaignId && !loading && items.length === 0 && (
            <p className="p-4 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              {linkedOnly ? "Aucun Ã©lÃ©ment liÃ© Ã  cette scÃ¨ne." : "Aucun Ã©lÃ©ment pour l'instant."}

                key={item.id}
                className={cn(
                  "rounded-lg border bg-background/60 p-3 transition-colors",
                  linked ? "border-primary/50" : "border-border"
                )}
              >
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    aria-label={`${expandedId === item.id ? "RÃ©duire" : "DÃ©velopper"} ${item.name}`}
                    aria-expanded={expandedId === item.id}
                    onClick={() => setExpandedId((current) => current === item.id ? null : item.id)}
                    className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-left"
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 font-display text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">
                      {expandedId === item.id
                        ? <ChevronUp className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
                        : <ChevronDown className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />}
                      <span className={cn("min-w-0", expandedId === item.id ? "break-words" : "truncate")}>
                        {item.name}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="mt-0.5 truncate font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">
                      {tab === "npc" && (item.role || "PNJ")}
                      {tab === "location" && (item.type || "Lieu")}
                      {tab === "clue" && (item.clue_type === "info" ? "Info" : "Indice")}
                    </div>
                  </button>
                  {tab === "npc" && <span className={cn("tag shrink-0 border", attitude.badge)}>{attitude.label}</span>}

                  <div className="mt-3 space-y-3 border-t border-border pt-3">

                    {item.description && <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm text-muted-foreground">{item.description}</p>}

                      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">

                            key={`${file.url}-${index}`}
                            onClick={() => openAttachment(file)}
                            title={file.name}
                            className="grid h-12 w-12 place-items-center overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-border bg-secondary/40 text-muted-foreground"
                          >
                            {isImage(file)

                  >
                    <Pencil className="mr-1 h-3 w-3" /> Modifier
                  </button>

              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </ScrollArea>
    </aside>
  );

=== src\components\codex\LocationTree.jsx ===
import React, { useMemo } from "react";
import { ChevronDown, ChevronRight, Plus } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import CodexCard from "@/components/codex/CodexCard";
import { childrenMap } from "@/lib/locationTree";

export default function LocationTree({ locations, collapsed, onToggle, onEdit, onDelete, onDuplicate, onAddChild }) {
  const map = useMemo(() => childrenMap(locations), [locations]);
  const byId = useMemo(() => new Map(locations.map((l) => [l.id, l])), [locations]);

  // Chemin des ancÃªtres, sans le lieu lui-mÃªme (ex. "France â€º Lyon").
  const ancestorsLabel = (location) => {
    const names = [];
    const seen = new Set([location.id]);
    let current = byId.get(location.parent_id);
    while (current && !seen.has(current.id)) {
      seen.add(current.id);
      names.unshift(current.name);
      current = byId.get(current.parent_id);
    }
    return names.join(" â€º ");
  };

  const renderNode = (location, depth, seen) => {
    const kids = (map.get(location.id) || []).filter((k) => !seen.has(k.id));
    const isCollapsed = collapsed.has(location.id);
    const path = ancestorsLabel(location);

    const body = (
      <div className="min-w-0">
        {path && (
          <p className="mb-1 truncate px-1 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">
            {path}
          </p>
        )}
        <CodexCard kind="location" entry={location} linked={false} onEdit={onEdit} onDelete={onDelete} onDuplicate={onDuplicate} />
        <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-3 px-1">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onAddChild(location)}
            className="inline-flex items-center font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground hover:text-primary"
          >
            <Plus className="mr-1 h-3 w-3" /> Sous-lieu
          </button>
          {kids.length > 0 && (
            <span className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">
              {kids.length} sous-lieu{kids.length > 1 ? "x" : ""}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    );

    if (kids.length === 0) return <div key={location.id}>{body}</div>;

    const nextSeen = new Set(seen).add(location.id);
    return (
      <div
        key={location.id}
        className={cn(
          "rounded-xl border p-3",
          depth % 2 === 0 ? "border-primary/30 bg-primary/[0.04]" : "border-border bg-secondary/30"
        )}
      >
        <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label={isCollapsed ? `DÃ©plier ${location.name}` : `Replier ${location.name}`}
            aria-expanded={!isCollapsed}
            onClick={() => onToggle(location.id)}
            className="mt-4 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground hover:text-primary"
          >
            {isCollapsed ? <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4" /> : <ChevronDown className="h-4 w-4" />}
          </button>
          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">{body}</div>
        </div>
        {!isCollapsed && (
          <div className="ml-2 mt-3 space-y-3 border-l-2 border-primary/30 pl-2 sm:pl-4">
            {kids.map((kid) => renderNode(kid, depth + 1, nextSeen))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  };

  const roots = map.get("") || [];
  return <div className="max-w-3xl space-y-4">{roots.map((root) => renderNode(root, 0, new Set()))}</div>;
}

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

import { api } from "@/api/localClient";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import {
  Select,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/use-toast";
import {
  CUSTOM_TYPE,
  RELATION_OPTIONS,
  RELATION_TYPES,
  groupIndex,
  groupLabel,
  inverseOf,
  relationLabel,
} from "@/lib/relationMeta";

  createRelation,
  deleteRelation,
  loadRelations,
  mirrorOf,
  pairStatus,

  validateRelation,

const EMPTY = {
  type: "parent_de",
  targetId: "",
  label: "",
  inverseLabel: "",
  notes: "",
  manual: false,
  ack: false,
};

export default function NpcRelations({ npc, campaignId }) {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [npcs, setNpcs] = useState([]);
  const [relations, setRelations] = useState([]);
  const [editor, setEditor] = useState(null); // null | { row: null | relation }
  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [confirmId, setConfirmId] = useState("");

  const reload = useCallback(async () => {
    const [n, r] = await Promise.all([
      api.entities.NPC.filter({ campaign_id: campaignId }, { sort: "name", limit: 1000 }),
      loadRelations(campaignId),
    ]);
    setNpcs(n.items);
    setRelations(r);
  }, [campaignId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    reload();
  }, [reload]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setEditor(null);
    setConfirmId("");
    setError("");
  }, [npc.id]);

  const names = useMemo(() => new Map(npcs.map((n) => [n.id, n.name])), [npcs]);
  const others = useMemo(() => npcs.filter((n) => n.id !== npc.id), [npcs, npc.id]);
  const mine = useMemo(
    () =>
      relations
        .filter((r) => r.from_id === npc.id)
        .sort(
          (a, b) =>
            groupIndex(a.type) - groupIndex(b.type) ||
            String(names.get(a.to_id) || "").localeCompare(String(names.get(b.to_id) || ""), "fr")
        ),
    [relations, npc.id, names]
  );

  const setField = (key, value) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, [key]: value }));

  const openNew = () => {
    setForm(EMPTY);
    setError("");
    setEditor({ row: null });
  };

  const openEdit = (row) => {
    const mirror = mirrorOf(row, relations);
    setForm({
      type: row.type,
      targetId: row.to_id,
      label: row.custom_label || "",
      inverseLabel: mirror?.custom_label || "",
      notes: row.notes || "",
      manual: false,
      ack: false,
    });
    setError("");
    setConfirmId("");
    setEditor({ row });
  };

  const save = async () => {
    const row = editor.row;
    const problem = validateRelation(relations, {
      fromId: npc.id,
      toId: form.targetId,
      type: form.type,
      ignorePairId: row?.pair_id,
    });
    if (problem) {
      setError(problem);
      return;
    }
    if (form.type === CUSTOM_TYPE && (!form.label.trim() || (!form.manual && !form.inverseLabel.trim()))) {
      setError("Renseignez le libellÃ© de la relation et celui de la relation inverse.");
      return;
    }
    setBusy(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const data = {
        toId: form.targetId,
        type: form.type,
        label: form.label.trim(),
        inverseLabel: form.inverseLabel.trim(),
        notes: form.notes.trim(),
        manual: form.manual,
      };
      if (row) await updateRelation(row, relations, data);
      else await createRelation(campaignId, { fromId: npc.id, ...data, oneSided: form.manual });
      await reload();
      setEditor(null);
      toast({ title: row ? "Relation mise Ã  jour" : "Relation ajoutÃ©e" });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Ã‰chec relation :", err);
      toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Enregistrement impossible", description: "La relation n'a pas Ã©tÃ© enregistrÃ©e." });
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const remove = async (row) => {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await deleteRelation(row, relations, true);
      await reload();
      setConfirmId("");
      toast({ title: "Relation supprimÃ©e" });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Suppression relation :", err);
      toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Suppression impossible" });
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const repair = async (row) => {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await repairRelation(row, relations);
      await reload();
      toast({ title: "Relation rÃ©parÃ©e", description: "La relation inverse a Ã©tÃ© rÃ©alignÃ©e." });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("RÃ©paration relation :", err);
      toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "RÃ©paration impossible" });
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const targetName = names.get(form.targetId) || "â€¦";
  const inversePreview =
    form.type === CUSTOM_TYPE
      ? form.inverseLabel || "â€¦"
      : RELATION_TYPES[inverseOf(form.type)]?.label || "";

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3 rounded-lg border border-border p-3">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <Label>Relations ({mine.length})</Label>
        {!editor && (
          <Button type="button" size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={openNew} disabled={others.length === 0}>
            <Plus className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" /> Ajouter
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        Les relations sont enregistrÃ©es immÃ©diatement. La relation inverse est mise Ã  jour automatiquement.
      </p>
      {others.length === 0 && (
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">CrÃ©ez d'autres PNJ pour pouvoir les relier.</p>
      )}

      {mine.length > 0 && (
        <ul className="space-y-2">
          {mine.map((row) => {
            const status = pairStatus(row, relations);
            return (
              <li key={row.id} className="rounded-lg border border-border bg-secondary/30 p-2.5">
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <p className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">
                      {groupLabel(row.type)}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-sm text-foreground">
                      <span className="text-muted-foreground">{relationLabel(row)}</span>{" "}
                      <strong className="font-display">{names.get(row.to_id) || "PNJ introuvable"}</strong>
                    </p>
                    {row.notes && <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{row.notes}</p>}
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex shrink-0 gap-1">
                    <Button

                      variant="ghost"

                      aria-label="Modifier la relation"
                      onClick={() => openEdit(row)}
                      disabled={!!editor || busy}

                      <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </Button>
                    <Button
                      type="button"
                      size="icon"
                      variant="ghost"
                      className="h-8 w-8 text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive"
                      aria-label="Supprimer la relation"
                      onClick={() => setConfirmId(confirmId === row.id ? "" : row.id)}
                      disabled={!!editor || busy}
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                </div>

export default function CampaignSelect({
  campaigns,

import React from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

import {
  Dialog,

  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,

  Drawer,

  DrawerHeader,
  DrawerTitle,
  DrawerDescription,
  DrawerFooter,
} from "@/components/ui/drawer";

export default function ResponsiveDialog({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  title,
  description,
  children,
  footer,
}) {
  const isMobile = useIsMobile();

  if (isMobile) {
    return (
      <Drawer open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
        <DrawerContent className="max-h-[92vh]">
          <DrawerHeader className="text-left">
            <DrawerTitle className="font-display">{title}</DrawerTitle>
            {description && <DrawerDescription>{description}</DrawerDescription>}
          </DrawerHeader>
          <div className="touch-scroll overflow-y-auto px-4 pb-2">{children}</div>
          {footer && <DrawerFooter>{footer}</DrawerFooter>}
        </DrawerContent>
      </Drawer>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent className={cn("flex max-h-[88vh] max-w-2xl flex-col overflow-hidden")}>
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle className="font-display">{title}</DialogTitle>
          {description && <DialogDescription>{description}</DialogDescription>}
        </DialogHeader>
        <div className="touch-scroll overflow-y-auto pr-1">{children}</div>
        {footer && <DialogFooter>{footer}</DialogFooter>}
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );

=== src\components\game\GameCharacterEditor.jsx ===
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Plus, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import ResponsiveDialog from "@/components/common/ResponsiveDialog";

const EMPTY = { name: "", notes: "", characteristics: {}, skills: [], equipment: [] };
const normalize = (value) => String(value || "").trim().toLocaleLowerCase("fr");

const blankCharacteristic = () => ({ value: "", modifier: "", test: "", max: "" });

export default function GameCharacterEditor({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  model,
  character,
  skillCatalog,
  equipmentCatalog,
  saving,
  onSave,
}) {
  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY);
  const [saveError, setSaveError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    setSaveError("");
    setForm(character ? {
      ...EMPTY,
      ...character,
      characteristics: structuredClone(character.characteristics || {}),
      skills: structuredClone(character.skills || []),
      equipment: structuredClone(character.equipment || []),
    } : EMPTY);
  }, [open, character]);

  const skillNames = useMemo(
    () => [...new Set(skillCatalog.map((item) => item.name).filter(Boolean))],
    [skillCatalog]
  );
  const equipmentNames = useMemo(
    () => [...new Set(equipmentCatalog.map((item) => item.name).filter(Boolean))],
    [equipmentCatalog]
  );
  const equipmentTypes = useMemo(
    () => [...new Set(equipmentCatalog.map((item) => item.type).filter(Boolean))],
    [equipmentCatalog]
  );

  const setCharacteristic = (name, field, value) => {
    setForm((current) => ({
      ...current,
      characteristics: {
        ...current.characteristics,
        [name]: { ...blankCharacteristic(), ...current.characteristics[name], [field]: value },
      },
    }));
  };

  const setSkill = (index, field, value) =>
    setForm((current) => ({
      ...current,
      skills: current.skills.map((skill, i) => i === index ? { ...skill, [field]: value } : skill),
    }));

  const findKnownSkill = (name) => skillCatalog.find((item) => normalize(item.name) === normalize(name));
  const findKnownEquipment = (name) => equipmentCatalog.find((item) => normalize(item.name) === normalize(name));

  const fillKnownSkill = (index) => {
    const skill = form.skills[index];
    const known = findKnownSkill(skill?.name);
    if (known && String(skill.roll_modifier || "") === String(known.roll_modifier || "")) return;
    if (!known || !window.confirm(`La compÃ©tence Â« ${known.name} Â» existe dÃ©jÃ  dans le catalogue. Reprendre ses champs ?`)) return;
    setSkill(index, "roll_modifier", known.roll_modifier || "");
  };

  const fieldNamesForType = (type) => [...new Set(
    equipmentCatalog
      .filter((item) => normalize(item.type) === normalize(type))
      .flatMap((item) => item.field_names || (item.fields || []).map((field) => field.name))
      .filter(Boolean)
  )];

  const setEquipment = (index, update) =>
    setForm((current) => ({
      ...current,
      equipment: current.equipment.map((item, i) =>
        i === index ? { ...item, ...(typeof update === "function" ? update(item) : update) } : item
      ),
    }));

  const fillKnownEquipment = (index) => {
    const item = form.equipment[index];
    const known = findKnownEquipment(item?.name);
    if (
      known &&
      normalize(item.type) === normalize(known.type) &&
      JSON.stringify(item.fields || []) === JSON.stringify(known.fields || [])
    ) return;
    if (!known || !window.confirm(`Lâ€™Ã©quipement Â« ${known.name} Â» existe dÃ©jÃ  dans le catalogue. Reprendre ses champs ?`)) return;
    setEquipment(index, {
      type: known.type || "",
      fields: structuredClone(known.fields || []),
    });
  };

  const prepareEquipmentFields = (index) => {
    const item = form.equipment[index];
    if (!item?.type) return;
    const names = fieldNamesForType(item.type);
    if (!names.length) return;
    setEquipment(index, (current) => ({
      fields: names.map((name) => ({
        name,
        value: normalize(current.type) === normalize(item.type)
          ? current.fields?.find((field) => field.name === name)?.value || ""
          : "",
      })),
    }));
  };

  const setEquipmentField = (equipmentIndex, fieldIndex, key, value) => {
    setEquipment(equipmentIndex, (item) => ({
      fields: item.fields.map((field, index) =>
        index === fieldIndex ? { ...field, [key]: value } : field
      ),
    }));
  };

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!form.name.trim()) return;
    setSaveError("");
    try {
      const saved = await onSave({
        ...form,
        name: form.name.trim(),
        characteristics: Object.fromEntries(
          Object.entries(form.characteristics || {}).map(([name, values]) => [
            name,
            Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(values).map(([key, value]) => [key, String(value ?? "")])),
          ])
        ),
        skills: form.skills.filter((skill) => skill.name?.trim()).map((skill) => ({
          ...skill,
          name: skill.name.trim(),
          roll_modifier: String(skill.roll_modifier ?? ""),
        })),
        equipment: form.equipment.filter((item) => item.name?.trim()).map((item) => ({
          ...item,
          name: item.name.trim(),
          type: item.type?.trim() || "",
          fields: (item.fields || []).filter((field) => field.name?.trim()).map((field) => ({
            name: field.name.trim(),
            value: String(field.value ?? ""),
          })),
        })),
      });
      if (!saved) setSaveError("La fiche nâ€™a pas Ã©tÃ© enregistrÃ©e.");
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Ã‰chec enregistrement fiche :", error);
      setSaveError("La fiche nâ€™a pas Ã©tÃ© enregistrÃ©e. VÃ©rifiez votre connexion au stockage local et rÃ©essayez.");
    }
  };

  const footer = (
    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
      <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}>Annuler</Button>
      <Button type="submit" form="game-character-form" disabled={saving || !form.name.trim()}>
        {saving ? "Enregistrementâ€¦" : "Enregistrer la fiche"}
      </Button>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <ResponsiveDialog
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={onOpenChange}
      title={character?.id ? "Modifier la fiche personnage" : "Nouvelle fiche personnage"}
      description="Les seuils Â« Test Â» sont renseignÃ©s manuellement. Ils sont conservÃ©s Ã  la duplication."
      footer={footer}
    >
      <form id="game-character-form" onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-5 py-1">
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="game-character-name">Nom du personnage</Label>
          <Input id="game-character-name" autoFocus value={form.name} onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, name: event.target.value }))} />
        </div>

        {model.characteristics.length > 0 && (
          <section className="space-y-3">
            <h3 className="font-display font-semibold">CaractÃ©ristiques</h3>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {model.characteristics.map((item) => {
                const values = form.characteristics[item.name] || blankCharacteristic();
                return (
                  <div key={item.name} className="rounded-lg border border-border p-3">
                    <h4 className="mb-2 font-medium">{item.name}</h4>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4">
                      <label className="space-y-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                        Valeur
                        <Input aria-label={`${item.name} valeur`} value={values.value || ""} onChange={(event) => setCharacteristic(item.name, "value", event.target.value)} />
                      </label>
                      {item.modifier && (
                        <label className="space-y-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                          Modificateur
                          <Input aria-label={`${item.name} modificateur`} value={values.modifier || ""} onChange={(event) => setCharacteristic(item.name, "modifier", event.target.value)} />
                        </label>
                      )}
                      {item.test && (
                        <label className="space-y-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                          Test (seuil)
                          <Input aria-label={`${item.name} seuil de test`} value={values.test || ""} onChange={(event) => setCharacteristic(item.name, "test", event.target.value)} />
                        </label>
                      )}
                      {item.max && (
                        <label className="space-y-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                          Maximum
                          <Input aria-label={`${item.name} maximum`} value={values.max || ""} onChange={(event) => setCharacteristic(item.name, "max", event.target.value)} />
                        </label>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );

        )}

            </Button>
          </div>

                    list={`skill-catalog-${index}`}

                      list={`equipment-types-${index}`}

=== src\components\game\GameSheetModelEditor.jsx ===
import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Plus, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
import ResponsiveDialog from "@/components/common/ResponsiveDialog";

const EMPTY_MODEL = { characteristics: [], skillRollModifier: false };

export default function GameSheetModelEditor({ open, onOpenChange, model, onSave, saving }) {
  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY_MODEL);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) {
      setForm({
        characteristics: (model?.characteristics || []).map((item) => ({ ...item })),
        skillRollModifier: !!model?.skillRollModifier,
      });
    }
  }, [open, model]);

  const updateCharacteristic = (index, key, value) => {
    setForm((current) => ({
      ...current,
      characteristics: current.characteristics.map((item, i) =>
        i === index ? { ...item, [key]: value } : item
      ),
    }));
  };

  const removeCharacteristic = (index) => {
    setForm((current) => ({
      ...current,
      characteristics: current.characteristics.filter((_, i) => i !== index),
    }));
  };

  const duplicateNames = new Set(
    form.characteristics
      .map((item) => item.name.trim().toLocaleLowerCase("fr"))
      .filter(Boolean)
  ).size !== form.characteristics.filter((item) => item.name.trim()).length;

  const submit = () => {
    const names = form.characteristics.map((item) => item.name.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if (new Set(names.map((name) => name.toLocaleLowerCase("fr"))).size !== names.length) return;
    onSave({
      characteristics: form.characteristics
        .map((item) => ({ ...item, name: item.name.trim() }))
        .filter((item) => item.name),
      skillRollModifier: !!form.skillRollModifier,
    });
  };

  const footer = (
    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
      <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}>Annuler</Button>
      <Button onClick={submit} disabled={saving || duplicateNames}>
        {saving ? "Enregistrementâ€¦" : "Enregistrer le modÃ¨le"}
      </Button>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <ResponsiveDialog
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={onOpenChange}
      title="ModÃ¨le de fiche de la campagne"
      description="DÃ©finissez les colonnes une fois. Les valeurs de chaque personnage restent indÃ©pendantes."
      footer={footer}
    >
      <div className="space-y-5 py-1">
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <div>
            <h3 className="font-display font-semibold">CaractÃ©ristiques</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Cochez les colonnes Ã  afficher sur chaque fiche.</p>
          </div>
          <div className="hidden grid-cols-[minmax(10rem,1fr)_repeat(3,5rem)_2.5rem] gap-2 px-2 font-mono text-[10px] uppercase text-muted-foreground sm:grid">
            <span>Nom</span><span className="text-center">Modif.</span><span className="text-center">Test</span><span className="text-center">Max</span><span />
          </div>
          {form.characteristics.map((item, index) => (
            <div key={index} className="grid grid-cols-2 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-border p-2 sm:grid-cols-[minmax(10rem,1fr)_repeat(3,5rem)_2.5rem]">
              <Input
                className="col-span-2 sm:col-span-1"
                aria-label={`Nom de la caractÃ©ristique ${index + 1}`}
                value={item.name}
                onChange={(event) => updateCharacteristic(index, "name", event.target.value)}
                placeholder="Ex. Force"
              />
              {[
                ["modifier", "Modificateur"],
                ["test", "Test"],
                ["max", "Maximum"],
              ].map(([field, label]) => (
                <label key={field} className="flex min-h-11 items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground sm:justify-center">
                  <span className="sm:hidden">{label}</span>
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    aria-label={`${label} pour ${item.name || `la caractÃ©ristique ${index + 1}`}`}
                    checked={!!item[field]}
                    onChange={(event) => updateCharacteristic(index, field, event.target.checked)}
                    className="h-4 w-4 accent-[hsl(var(--primary))]"
                  />
                </label>
              ))}
              <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label={`Supprimer la caractÃ©ristique ${index + 1}`} onClick={() => removeCharacteristic(index)}>
                <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
              </Button>
            </div>
          ))}
          {duplicateNames && <p className="text-xs text-destructive">Chaque caractÃ©ristique doit avoir un nom distinct.</p>}
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="outline"
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => setForm((current) => ({
              ...current,
              characteristics: [...current.characteristics, { name: "", modifier: false, test: false, max: false }],
            }))}
          >
            <Plus className="h-4 w-4" /> Ajouter une caractÃ©ristique
          </Button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 rounded-lg border border-border p-3">
          <div>
            <Label>Modificateur de lancer des compÃ©tences</Label>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Afficher cette colonne sur les fiches de personnage.</p>
          </div>
          <Switch
            checked={!!form.skillRollModifier}
            onCheckedChange={(value) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, skillRollModifier: value }))}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </ResponsiveDialog>
  );
}
