import React from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { Plus, MoreHorizontal, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import SceneNode from "@/components/home/SceneNode";
import { SCENE_STATUS } from "@/lib/sceneMeta";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
} from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu";

export default function ActColumn({
  act,
  scenes,
  sceneTitles,
  npcById,
  locationById,
  clueById,
  activeSceneId,
  expanded,
  onSelectScene,
  onOpenScene,
  onCycleStatus,
  onAddScene,
  onRenameAct,
  onDeleteAct,
  onDeleteScene,
}) {
  const ordered = [...scenes].sort((a, b) => (a.order ?? 0) - (b.order ?? 0));

  return (
    <div className={cn(
      "flex shrink-0 flex-col rounded-xl border border-border bg-card/50 p-4 transition-[width] duration-200",
      expanded ? "w-[380px]" : activeSceneId ? "w-[250px]" : "w-[290px]"
    )}>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        {act ? (
          <input
            defaultValue={act.title}
            onBlur={(e) => {
              const value = e.target.value.trim();
              if (value && value !== act.title) onRenameAct(act.id, value);
            }}
            className="min-w-0 flex-1 bg-transparent font-display text-sm font-semibold text-foreground outline-none focus:text-primary"
          />
        ) : (
          <span className="flex-1 font-display text-sm font-semibold text-muted-foreground">Sans acte</span>
        )}
        {act && (
          <DropdownMenu>
            <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
              <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="h-11 w-11 md:h-7 md:w-7">
                <MoreHorizontal className="h-4 w-4" />
              </Button>
            </DropdownMenuTrigger>
            <DropdownMenuContent align="end">
              <DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => onDeleteAct(act)} className="text-destructive focus:text-destructive">
                <Trash2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Supprimer l'acte
              </DropdownMenuItem>
            </DropdownMenuContent>
          </DropdownMenu>
        )}
      </div>
      <span className="mt-1 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground">
        {ordered.length} scÃ¨ne{ordered.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}
      </span>

      <div className="mt-4 flex flex-1 flex-col gap-3">
        {ordered.map((scene, i) => (
          <div key={scene.id} className="flex gap-3">
            <div className="relative w-3 shrink-0">
              {ordered.length > 1 && (
                <span
                  className={cn(
                    "absolute left-1/2 w-px -translate-x-1/2 bg-border",
                    i === 0 ? "top-5" : "top-0",
                    i === ordered.length - 1 ? "bottom-5" : "bottom-0"
                  )}
                />
              )}
              <span
                className={cn(
                  "absolute left-1/2 top-5 z-10 h-2.5 w-2.5 -translate-x-1/2 rounded-full ring-2 ring-background",
                  (SCENE_STATUS[scene.status] || SCENE_STATUS.brouillon).dot
                )}
              />
            </div>
            <SceneNode
              scene={scene}
              active={scene.id === activeSceneId}
              sceneTitles={sceneTitles}
              npcById={npcById}
              locationById={locationById}
              clueById={clueById}
              onSelect={onSelectScene}
              onOpen={onOpenScene}
              onCycleStatus={onCycleStatus}
              onDelete={onDeleteScene}
            />
          </div>
        ))}
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => onAddScene(act?.id || null)}
          className="justify-start text-muted-foreground hover:text-primary"
        >
          <Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> ScÃ¨ne
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import React from "react";
import { Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import ResponsiveDialog from "@/components/common/ResponsiveDialog";

const TypedInput = /** @type {React.ComponentType<React.InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement>>} */ (Input);
const TypedTextarea = /** @type {React.ComponentType<React.TextareaHTMLAttributes<HTMLTextAreaElement>>} */ (Textarea);

/**
 * @typedef {Object} CampaignDialogProps
 * @property {boolean} open
 * @property {(value: boolean) => void} onOpenChange
 * @property {{ name: string, system: string, summary: string }} form
 * @property {(field: string, value: string) => void} setField
 * @property {boolean} saving
 * @property {() => void} onSave
 * @property {boolean} [editing]
 * @property {() => void} [onDelete]
 */

/** @param {CampaignDialogProps} props */
export default function CampaignDialog({ open, onOpenChange, form, setField, saving, onSave, editing = false, onDelete }) {
  const footer = (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
      {editing && onDelete ? (
        <button
          type="button"
          className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-destructive transition-colors hover:bg-destructive/10"
          onClick={onDelete}
        >
          <Trash2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Supprimer la campagne
        </button>
      ) : (
        <span />
      )}
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <button
          type="button"
          className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-accent hover:text-accent-foreground"
          onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}
        >
          Annuler
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-primary px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-primary/90 disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50"
          onClick={onSave}
          disabled={saving || !form.name.trim()}
        >
          {saving ? "Enregistrementâ€¦" : editing ? "Enregistrer" : "CrÃ©er la campagne"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <ResponsiveDialog
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={onOpenChange}
      title={editing ? "Modifier la campagne" : "Nouvelle campagne"}
      description={
        editing
          ? "Modifiez les informations de la campagne ou supprimez-la avec tout son contenu."
          : "Donnez un nom Ã  votre campagne pour commencer Ã  tisser son arborescence."
      }
      footer={footer}
    >
      <div className="space-y-4 py-1">
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <label htmlFor="campaign-name" className="text-sm font-medium leading-none">
            Nom de la campagne
          </label>
          <TypedInput
            id="campaign-name"
            value={form.name}
            onChange={(e) => setField("name", e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Ex. Les Ombres de Val-Cendre"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">

import React from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";

export default function LinkPicker({ label, items, selected, onToggle, emptyText }) {
  const selectedIds = selected || [];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      <Label>{label}</Label>
      {items.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{emptyText}</p>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {items.map((item) => {
            const on = selectedIds.includes(item.id);
            return (
              <button
                key={item.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onToggle(item.id)}
                className={cn(
                  "tag border transition-colors",
                  on
                    ? "border-primary/50 bg-primary/15 text-primary"
                    : "border-border text-muted-foreground hover:border-primary/30 hover:text-foreground"
                )}
              >
                {item.name || item.title}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import React from "react";
import { Plus } from "lucide-react";
import {
  Accordion,
  AccordionItem,
  AccordionTrigger,
  AccordionContent,
} from "@/components/ui/accordion";
import SwipeSceneRow from "@/components/home/SwipeSceneRow";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

export default function MobileSceneList({
  acts,
  scenes,
  npcs,
  locations,
  clues,
  activeSceneId,
  onSelectScene,
  onOpenScene,
  onCycleStatus,
  onAddScene,
  onAddAct,
}) {
  const sorted = [...acts].sort((a, b) => (a.order ?? 0) - (b.order ?? 0));
  const orphans = scenes.filter((s) => !s.act_id || !acts.some((a) => a.id === s.act_id));
  const groups = [
    ...sorted.map((a) => ({ id: a.id, title: a.title, scenes: scenes.filter((s) => s.act_id === a.id) })),
    ...(orphans.length ? [{ id: "_none", title: "Sans acte", scenes: orphans }] : []),
  ];

  if (groups.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border p-8 text-center">
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Aucun acte pour cette campagne.</p>
        <Button variant="outline" className="mt-4" onClick={onAddAct}>
          <Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Ajouter un acte
        </Button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <Accordion type="multiple" defaultValue={groups.map((g) => g.id)} className="space-y-3">
        {groups.map((group) => (
          <AccordionItem
            key={group.id}
            value={group.id}
            className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-card/50 px-4"
          >
            <AccordionTrigger className="font-display text-sm hover:no-underline">
              <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                {group.title}
                <span className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground">
                  {group.scenes.length}
                </span>
              </span>
            </AccordionTrigger>
            <AccordionContent className="space-y-2 pt-1">
              {[...group.scenes]
                .sort((a, b) => (a.order ?? 0) - (b.order ?? 0))
                .map((scene) => (
                  <SwipeSceneRow
                    key={scene.id}
                    scene={scene}
                    active={scene.id === activeSceneId}
                    npcs={npcs}
                    locations={locations}
                    clues={clues}
                    onOpen={onOpenScene}
                    onSelect={onSelectScene}
                    onToggleStatus={onCycleStatus}
                  />
                ))}
              <Button
                variant="ghost"
                size="sm"
                onClick={() => onAddScene(group.id === "_none" ? null : group.id)}
                className="justify-start text-muted-foreground hover:text-primary"
              >
                <Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> ScÃ¨ne
              </Button>
            </AccordionContent>
          </AccordionItem>
        ))}
      </Accordion>
      <Button variant="outline" className="w-full" onClick={onAddAct}>
        <Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Ajouter un acte
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}
=== src\components\home\ProgressTracker.jsx ===
import React from "react";

export default function ProgressTracker({ progress }) {
  const total = progress?.total || 0;
  const played = progress?.joue || 0;
  const key = progress?.clef || 0;
  const drafts = progress?.brouillon || 0;
  const pct = total ? Math.round((played / total) * 100) : 0;

  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-background/60 p-4">
      <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
        <span className="font-mono text-sm uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground">
          Avancement
        </span>
        <span className="font-mono text-sm text-foreground">
          {played}/{total} jouÃ©es
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-3 h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-secondary">
        <div className="h-full rounded-full bg-primary transition-all" style={{ width: `${pct}%` }} />
      </div>
      <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-1 font-mono text-sm text-muted-foreground">
        <span className="text-primary">{key} scÃ¨nes clef</span>
        <span>{drafts} brouillons</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import React from "react";
import { Plus } from "lucide-react";
import ActColumn from "@/components/home/ActColumn";

export default function SceneBoard({
  acts,
  scenes,
  npcs,
  locations,
  clues,
  activeSceneId,
  onSelectScene,
  onOpenScene,
  onCycleStatus,
  onAddAct,
  onRenameAct,
  onDeleteAct,
  onAddScene,
  onDeleteScene,
}) {
  const sorted = [...acts].sort((a, b) => (a.order ?? 0) - (b.order ?? 0));
  const orphans = scenes.filter((s) => !s.act_id || !acts.some((a) => a.id === s.act_id));
  const sceneTitles = scenes.reduce((acc, s) => ({ ...acc, [s.id]: s.title }), {});
  const npcById = Object.fromEntries(npcs.map((npc) => [npc.id, npc]));
  const locationById = Object.fromEntries(locations.map((location) => [location.id, location]));
  const clueById = Object.fromEntries(clues.map((clue) => [clue.id, clue]));

  const common = {
    sceneTitles,
    npcById,
    locationById,
    clueById,
    activeSceneId,
    onSelectScene,
    onOpenScene,
    onCycleStatus,
    onAddScene,
    onRenameAct,
    onDeleteAct,
    onDeleteScene,
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex gap-4 overflow-x-auto pb-4">
      {sorted.map((act) => (
        <ActColumn
          key={act.id}
          act={act}
          scenes={scenes.filter((s) => s.act_id === act.id)}
          expanded={scenes.some((scene) => scene.act_id === act.id && scene.id === activeSceneId)}
          {...common}
        />
      ))}
      {orphans.length > 0 && (
        <ActColumn act={null} scenes={orphans} expanded={orphans.some((scene) => scene.id === activeSceneId)} {...common} />
      )}
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onAddAct}
        className="flex min-h-[220px] w-[200px] shrink-0 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:border-primary/40 hover:text-primary"
      >
        <Plus className="h-6 w-6" />
        <span className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest">Ajouter un acte</span>
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

import React from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { ArrowRight, MoreHorizontal, Pencil, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { SCENE_STATUS } from "@/lib/sceneMeta";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
} from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu";
import { useExpandedCard } from "@/lib/ExpandedCardContext";

export default function SceneNode({
  scene,
  active,
  sceneTitles,
  npcById,
  locationById,
  clueById,
  onSelect,
  onOpen,
  onCycleStatus,
  onDelete,
}) {
  const { expanded, cardProps } = useExpandedCard(`scene:${scene.id}`);
  const meta = SCENE_STATUS[scene.status] || SCENE_STATUS.brouillon;
  const branches = (scene.next_scene_ids || []).map((id) => sceneTitles[id]).filter(Boolean);
  const linked = (scene.npc_ids?.length || 0) + (scene.location_ids?.length || 0) + (scene.clue_ids?.length || 0);
  const linkedNpcs = (scene.npc_ids || []).map((id) => npcById[id]).filter(Boolean);
  const linkedLocations = (scene.location_ids || []).map((id) => locationById[id]).filter(Boolean);
  const linkedClues = (scene.clue_ids || []).map((id) => clueById[id]).filter(Boolean);

  return (
    <div
      {...cardProps}
      role="button"
      onClick={() => onSelect(scene)}
      onDoubleClick={() => onOpen(scene)}
      onKeyDown={(e) => {
        if (e.key === "Enter") onOpen(scene);
      }}
      className={cn(
        "group flex-1 cursor-pointer rounded-lg border bg-background/70 p-3 transition-colors",
        active || expanded ? "corner-accent border-primary/60 bg-primary/[0.06] ring-2 ring-primary/40" : "border-border hover:border-primary/30"
      )}
    >
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
        <button
          type="button"
          title="Changer le statut"
          onClick={(e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            onCycleStatus(scene);
          }}
          className={cn("tag border transition-opacity hover:opacity-80", meta.badge)}
        >
          <span className={cn("mr-1.5 h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full", meta.dot)} />
          {meta.label}
        </button>
        <div onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
          <DropdownMenu>
            <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
              <Button
                variant="ghost"
                size="icon"
                className="h-11 w-11 opacity-0 transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover:opacity-100 md:h-7 md:w-7"
              >
                <MoreHorizontal className="h-4 w-4" />
              </Button>
            </DropdownMenuTrigger>
            <DropdownMenuContent align="end">
              <DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => onOpen(scene)}>
                <Pencil className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Modifier
              </DropdownMenuItem>
              <DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => onDelete(scene)} className="text-destructive focus:text-destructive">
                <Trash2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Supprimer
              </DropdownMenuItem>
            </DropdownMenuContent>
          </DropdownMenu>
        </div>
      </div>

      <h3 className="mt-2 font-display text-sm font-semibold leading-snug text-foreground">{scene.title}</h3>
      {!active && scene.summary && <p className={cn("mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground", !expanded && "line-clamp-2")}>{scene.summary}</p>}

      {(linked > 0 || branches.length > 0) && (
        <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">
          {linked > 0 && <span>{linked} lien{linked > 1 ? "s" : ""}</span>}
          {branches.length > 0 && (
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-primary/80">
              <ArrowRight className="h-3 w-3" />
              {branches.join(" Â· ")}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
      {(active || expanded) && (
        <div className="mt-3 space-y-3 border-t border-border pt-3 text-xs">
          {scene.summary && <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap text-muted-foreground">{scene.summary}</p>}
          {scene.gm_notes && (
            <div>
              <p className="mb-1 font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">Notes du MJ</p>
              <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap text-foreground">{scene.gm_notes}</p>

=== src\components\home\SwipeSceneRow.jsx ===
import React, { useRef } from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { SCENE_STATUS } from "@/lib/sceneMeta";

export default function SwipeSceneRow({ scene, active, npcs, locations, clues, onOpen, onSelect, onToggleStatus }) {
  const startX = useRef(null);
  const meta = SCENE_STATUS[scene.status] || SCENE_STATUS.brouillon;

  return (
    <div
      data-expandable-card={`scene:${scene.id}`}
      aria-expanded={active}
      tabIndex={0}
      onKeyDown={(event) => {
        if ((event.key === "Enter" || event.key === " ") && event.target === event.currentTarget) {
          event.preventDefault();
          onSelect(scene);
        }
      }}
      onTouchStart={(e) => {
        startX.current = e.touches[0].clientX;
      }}
      onTouchEnd={(e) => {
        if (startX.current == null) return;
        const dx = e.changedTouches[0].clientX - startX.current;
        startX.current = null;
        if (dx > 60) onToggleStatus(scene);
      }}
      className={cn(
        "flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border p-3 transition-colors",
        active ? "border-primary/60 bg-primary/[0.06]" : "border-border bg-background/70"
      )}
    >
      <button type="button" className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-left" onClick={() => onSelect(scene)} onDoubleClick={() => onOpen(scene)}>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className={cn("h-1.5 w-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full", meta.dot)} />
          <span className="truncate font-display text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">{scene.title}</span>
        </div>
        {scene.summary && (
          <p className={cn("mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground", active ? "whitespace-pre-wrap" : "line-clamp-1")}>
            {scene.summary}
          </p>
        )}
        {active && scene.gm_notes && (
          <p className="mt-2 whitespace-pre-wrap text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            <span className="font-medium text-foreground">Notes du MJ : </span>{scene.gm_notes}
          </p>
        )}
        {active && (scene.npc_ids?.length > 0 || scene.location_ids?.length > 0 || scene.clue_ids?.length > 0) && (
          <div className="mt-2 space-y-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            {scene.npc_ids?.length > 0 && (

import React from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { GitBranch, BookMarked, CalendarDays, Dices, StickyNote, Swords } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { useTabStacks } from "@/lib/TabStackContext";

const TABS = [
  { to: "/", label: "ScÃ¨nes", icon: GitBranch },
  { to: "/codex", label: "Codex", icon: BookMarked },
  { to: "/calendrier", label: "Agenda", icon: CalendarDays },
  { to: "/tables", label: "Tables", icon: Dices },
  { to: "/notes", label: "Notes", icon: StickyNote },
  { to: "/jeu", label: "Jeu", icon: Swords },
];

export default function MobileNav() {
  const { activeTab, openTab, tabTarget } = useTabStacks();

  return (
    <nav className="glass-panel fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-40 flex items-stretch justify-around rounded-t-2xl pb-[env(safe-area-inset-bottom)] md:hidden">
      {TABS.map((tab) => {
        const Icon = tab.icon;
        const active = activeTab === tab.to;
        return (
          <Link
            key={tab.label}
            to={tabTarget ? tabTarget(tab.to) : tab.to}
            aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
            onClick={(event) => {
              if (!openTab) return;
              if (event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey || event.shiftKey || event.altKey) return;
              event.preventDefault();
              openTab(tab.to);
            }}
            className={cn(
              "flex flex-1 flex-col items-center gap-1 py-3",
              active ? "text-primary" : "text-muted-foreground"
            )}
          >
            <Icon className="h-5 w-5" />
            <span className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-wider">{tab.label}</span>
          </Link>
        );
      })}
    </nav>
  );
}

=== src\components\print\PrintButton.jsx ===
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { createPortal } from "react-dom";
import { Printer } from "lucide-react";
import { api } from "@/api/localClient";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import ResponsiveDialog from "@/components/common/ResponsiveDialog";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/use-toast";
import PrintSheet from "@/components/print/PrintSheet";
import { loadRelations } from "@/lib/npcRelations";
import {
    SECTIONS,
    buildPrintModel,
    buildTrees,
    countableIds,
    selectableIds,
} from "@/lib/printModel";

const PRESETS = {
    scenes: "scenes",
    clue: "clues",
    location: "locations",
    npc: "npcs",
    notes: "notes",
    tables: "tables",
    sessions: "sessions",
    characters: "characters",
};
const LIMIT = 5000;

// ---------- Arbre Ã  cases Ã  cocher ----------

function TriCheckbox({ state, onChange }) {
    const ref = useRef(null);
    useEffect(() => {
        if (ref.current) ref.current.indeterminate = state === "mixed";
    }, [state]);
    return (
        <input
            ref={ref}
            type="checkbox"
            className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 accent-[hsl(var(--primary))]"
            checked={state === "all"}
            onChange={onChange}
        />
    );
}

function descendantsOf(node) {
    const out = [];
    const go = (n) => (n.children || []).forEach((c) => (out.push(c), go(c)));
    go(node);
    return out;
}

function stateOf(node, selected) {
    if (node.derived) {
        const leaves = descendantsOf(node).filter((n) => !n.derived);
        const count = leaves.filter((n) => selected.has(n.id)).length;
        return count === 0 ? "none" : count === leaves.length ? "all" : "mixed";
    }
    if (selected.has(node.id)) return "all";
    return descendantsOf(node).some((n) => selected.has(n.id)) ? "mixed" : "none";
}

function TreeRows({ nodes, depth, selected, onToggle }) {
    return nodes.map((node) => (
        <React.Fragment key={node.id}>
            <label
                className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded px-1 py-1 text-sm hover:bg-secondary/50"
                style={{ paddingLeft: depth * 16 + 4 }}
            >
                <TriCheckbox state={stateOf(node, selected)} onChange={() => onToggle(node)} />
                <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-foreground">{node.label}</span>
                {node.hint && (
                    <span className="shrink-0 font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">
                        {node.hint}
                    </span>
                )}
            </label>
            {node.children?.length > 0 && (
                <TreeRows nodes={node.children} depth={depth + 1} selected={selected} onToggle={onToggle} />
            )}
        </React.Fragment>
    ));
}

// ---------- BoÃ®te de dialogue ----------

function PrintDialog({ open, onOpenChange, campaignId, preset, onPrint, allowPdfExport }) {
    const { toast } = useToast();
    const [data, setData] = useState(null);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState({});
    const [selected, setSelected] = useState({});
    const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState({});
    const [opts, setOpts] = useState({ secrets: true, cluesWithScenes: true, images: false });

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!open || !campaignId) return;
        let alive = true;
        setLoading(true);
        const list = (entity) => api.entities[entity].filter({ campaign_id: campaignId }, { limit: LIMIT });
        Promise.all([
            api.entities.Campaign.get(campaignId).catch(() => null),
            list("Act"),
            list("Scene"),
            list("NPC"),
            list("Location"),
            list("Clue"),
            list("Note"),
            list("RollTable"),
            list("GameSession"),
            list("GameCharacter"),
            api.entities.GameSheetModel.filter({ campaign_id: campaignId }, { limit: 1 }),
            loadRelations(campaignId),
        ])
            .then(([campaign, acts, scenes, npcs, locations, clues, notes, tables, sessions, characters, models, relations]) => {
                if (!alive) return;
                const loaded = {
                    campaign,
                    acts: acts.items,
                    scenes: scenes.items,
                    npcs: npcs.items,
                    locations: locations.items,
                    clues: clues.items,
                    notes: notes.items,
                    tables: tables.items,
                    sessions: sessions.items,
                    characters: characters.items,
                    gameSheetModel: models.items[0] || null,
                    relations,
                };
                const trees = buildTrees(loaded);
                setData(loaded);
                setSelected(Object.fromEntries(SECTIONS.map((s) => [s.key, new Set(selectableIds(trees[s.key]))])));
                setEnabled(Object.fromEntries(SECTIONS.map((s) => [s.key, preset === "all" || PRESETS[preset] === s.key])));
                setExpanded({});
            })
            .catch((error) => {
                console.error("Chargement impression :", error);
                toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Chargement impossible" });
            })
            .finally(() => alive && setLoading(false));
        return () => {
            alive = false;
        };
    }, [open, campaignId, preset, toast]);

    const trees = useMemo(() => (data ? buildTrees(data) : null), [data]);

    const counts = useMemo(() => {
        const out = {};
        if (!trees) return out;
        SECTIONS.forEach((s) => {
            const ids = countableIds(trees[s.key]);
            out[s.key] = { done: ids.filter((id) => selected[s.key]?.has(id)).length, total: ids.length };
        });
        return out;
    }, [trees, selected]);

    const hasContent = SECTIONS.some((s) => enabled[s.key] && counts[s.key]?.done > 0);

    const toggleNode = (key, node) => {
        const set = new Set(selected[key]);
        const kids = descendantsOf(node).filter((n) => !n.derived);
        const targets = node.derived ? kids : [node, ...kids];
        const full = stateOf(node, set) === "all";
        targets.forEach((n) => (full ? set.delete(n.id) : set.add(n.id)));
        setSelected((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: set }));
        setEnabled((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: true }));
    };

    const setAll = (key, on) =>
        setSelected((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: on ? new Set(selectableIds(trees[key])) : new Set() }));

    const submit = (action) => {
        const sel = Object.fromEntries(SECTIONS.map((s) => [s.key, enabled[s.key] ? selected[s.key] : new Set()]));
        const model = buildPrintModel(data, sel, opts);
        if (model.blocks.length === 0) {
            toast({ title: "Rien Ã  imprimer", description: "Cochez au moins un Ã©lÃ©ment." });
            return;
        }
        onPrint(model, action);
    };

    const footer = (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-end gap-2">
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}>
                Annuler
            </Button>
            {allowPdfExport && (
                <Button onClick={() => submit("pdf")} disabled={loading || !hasContent}>
                    <Printer className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> CrÃ©er un PDF avec champs Ã©ditables
                </Button>
            )}
            <Button onClick={() => submit("print")} disabled={loading || !hasContent}>
                <Printer className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Imprimer
            </Button>
        </div>
    );

    const option = (key, label, hint) => (
        <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-3">
            <input
                type="checkbox"
                className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 accent-[hsl(var(--primary))]"
                checked={opts[key]}
                onChange={(e) => setOpts((o) => ({ ...o, [key]: e.target.checked }))}
            />
            <span>
                <span className="block text-sm text-foreground">{label}</span>
                <span className="block text-xs text-muted-foreground">{hint}</span>
            </span>
        </label>
    );

    return (
        <ResponsiveDialog
            open={open}
            onOpenChange={onOpenChange}
            title={allowPdfExport ? "Imprimer ou exporter en PDF" : "Imprimer"}
            description={allowPdfExport
                ? "Choisissez les Ã©lÃ©ments Ã  inclure. Lâ€™export PDF conserve du texte sÃ©lectionnable et modifiable."
                : "Choisissez ce qui doit figurer dans le document. Chaque section peut s'imprimer seule."}
            footer={footer}
        >
            {loading || !trees ? (
                <p className="py-8 text-center font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground">
                    Chargementâ€¦
                </p>
            ) : (
                <div className="space-y-3 py-1">
                    {SECTIONS.map((s) => (
                        <div key={s.key} className="rounded-lg border border-border">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3">
                                <input
                                    id={`print-${s.key}`}
                                    type="checkbox"
                                    className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 accent-[hsl(var(--primary))]"
                                    checked={!!enabled[s.key]}
                                    onChange={(e) => setEnabled((prev) => ({ ...prev, [s.key]: e.target.checked }))}
                                />
                                <label htmlFor={`print-${s.key}`} className="min-w-0 flex-1 cursor-pointer">
                                    <span className="block font-display text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">{s.title}</span>
                                    <span className="block text-xs text-muted-foreground">{s.hint}</span>
                                </label>
                                <span className="font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                                    {counts[s.key]?.done}/{counts[s.key]?.total}
                                </span>
                                <Button
                                    type="button"
                                    size="sm"
                                    variant="ghost"
                                    onClick={() => setExpanded((prev) => ({ ...prev, [s.key]: !prev[s.key] }))}
                                >
                                    {expanded[s.key] ? "Masquer" : "Choisir"}
                                </Button>
                            </div>
                            {expanded[s.key] && (
                                <div className="space-y-2 border-t border-border p-3">
                                    <div className="flex gap-2">
                                        <Button type="button" size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => setAll(s.key, true)}>
                                            Tout
                                        </Button>
                                        <Button type="button" size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => setAll(s.key, false)}>
                                            Aucun
                                        </Button>
                                    </div>
                                    {trees[s.key].length === 0 ? (
                                        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Rien dans cette section.</p>
                                    ) : (
                                        <div className="max-h-64 overflow-y-auto">
                                            <TreeRows
                                                nodes={trees[s.key]}
                                                depth={0}
                                                selected={selected[s.key] || new Set()}
                                                onToggle={(node) => toggleNode(s.key, node)}
                                            />
                                        </div>
                                    )}
                                </div>
                            )}
                        </div>
                    ))}

                    <div className="space-y-3 rounded-lg border border-border p-3">
                        <Label>Options</Label>
                        {option("secrets", "Inclure les notes du MJ dans les scÃ¨nes", "Notes secrÃ¨tes de chaque scÃ¨ne, Ã  dÃ©cocher pour un document destinÃ© aux joueurs.")}
                        {option("cluesWithScenes", "Imprimer les indices avec leur scÃ¨ne", "Sinon, les indices liÃ©s ne sont pas repris dans les scÃ¨nes.")}
                        {option("images", "Inclure les images jointes", "Lieux, PNJ et indices. Les PDF ne sont pas imprimÃ©s.")}
                        {allowPdfExport && (
                            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                                Les champs sont prÃ©remplis et peuvent Ãªtre modifiÃ©s dans un lecteur PDF compatible avec les formulaires.
                            </p>
                        )}
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </ResponsiveDialog>
    );
}

// ---------- Bouton ----------

export default function PrintButton({
    campaignId,
    preset = "all",
    label = "Imprimer",
    variant = "outline",
    size = "default",
    className,
    allowPdfExport = false,
}) {
    const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
    const [job, setJob] = useState(null);
    const { toast } = useToast();

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!job) return;
        let cancelled = false;
        if (job.action === "pdf") {
            import("@/lib/editablePdf")
                .then(({ createEditablePdf }) => createEditablePdf(job.model))
                .then(async ({ bytes, filename }) => {
                    if (window.arcanesPdf?.save) {
                        const result = await window.arcanesPdf.save(bytes, filename);
                        if (!result?.ok) throw new Error(result?.error || "Le PDF n'a pas pu Ãªtre enregistrÃ©.");
                        if (!result.canceled) {
                            toast({ title: "PDF Ã©ditable enregistrÃ©", description: result.filePath });
                        }
                        return;
                    }

                    const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([bytes], { type: "application/pdf" }));
                    const link = document.createElement("a");
                    link.href = url;
                    link.download = filename;
                    link.click();
                    window.setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
                    toast({ title: "TÃ©lÃ©chargement du PDF Ã©ditable lancÃ©" });
                })
                .catch((error) => {
                    console.error("Export du PDF Ã©ditable impossible :", error);
                    toast({
                        variant: "destructive",
                        title: "Export PDF impossible",
                        description: error?.message || "Le document n'a pas pu Ãªtre gÃ©nÃ©rÃ©.",
                    });
                })
                .finally(() => {
                    if (!cancelled) setJob(null);
                });
            return () => {
                cancelled = true;
            };
        }

        const run = async () => {
            await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 80));
            const imgs = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(".print-root img"));
            await Promise.all(imgs.map((img) => (img.decode ? img.decode().catch(() => { }) : null)));
            if (!cancelled) window.print();
        };
        const done = () => setJob(null);
        run();
        window.addEventListener("afterprint", done);
        return () => {
            cancelled = true;
            window.removeEventListener("afterprint", done);
        };
    }, [job, toast]);

    return (
        <>
            <Button
                type="button"
                variant={variant}
                size={size}
                className={className}
                disabled={!campaignId}
                onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
            >
                <Printer className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> {label}
            </Button>
            <PrintDialog
                open={open}
                onOpenChange={setOpen}
                campaignId={campaignId}
                preset={preset}
                allowPdfExport={allowPdfExport}
                onPrint={(built, action) => {
                    setOpen(false);
                    setJob({ model: built, action });
                }}
            />
            {job?.action === "print" && createPortal(<PrintSheet model={job.model} />, document.body)}
        </>
    );
}

function CharacterContent({ character, characteristics, skillRollModifier }) {
    const stats = characteristics.flatMap((definition) => {
        const values = character.characteristics?.[definition.name] || {};
        const entries = [
            ["Valeur", values.value],
            ...(definition.modifier ? [["Modificateur", values.modifier]] : []),
            ...(definition.test ? [["Test (seuil)", values.test]] : []),
            ...(definition.max ? [["Maximum", values.max]] : []),
        ].filter(([, value]) => hasValue(value));
        return entries.length ? [{ name: definition.name, entries }] : [];
    });

    return (
        <>
            {stats.length > 0 && (
                <section className="prt-section">
                    <h2>CaractÃ©ristiques</h2>
                    <div className="prt-character-grid">
                        {stats.map((stat) => (
                            <article className="prt-character-stat" key={stat.name}>
                                <h3>{stat.name}</h3>
                                {stat.entries.map(([label, value]) => <p key={label}><strong>{label} :</strong> {value}</p>)}
                            </article>
                        ))}
                    </div>
                </section>
            )}
            {(character.skills || []).length > 0 && (
                <section className="prt-section">
                    <h2>CompÃ©tences</h2>
                    {character.skills.map((skill, index) => (
                        <div className="prt-character-item" key={`${skill.name}-${index}`}>
                            <span className="prt-title">{skill.name}</span>
                            {skillRollModifier && hasValue(skill.roll_modifier) && <span> Â· Modificateur de lancer : {skill.roll_modifier}</span>}
                        </div>
                    ))}
                </section>
            )}
            {(character.equipment || []).length > 0 && (
                <section className="prt-section">
                    <h2>Ã‰quipement</h2>
                    {character.equipment.map((item, index) => (
                        <div className="prt-character-item" key={`${item.name}-${index}`}>
                            <div className="prt-title">{item.name}</div>
                            {item.type && <div className="prt-meta">{item.type}</div>}
                            {(item.fields || []).filter((field) => field.name || field.value).map((field, fieldIndex) => (
                                <p key={`${field.name}-${fieldIndex}`}><strong>{field.name}</strong>{hasValue(field.value) && ` : ${field.value}`}</p>
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    ))}
                </section>
            )}
            {character.notes && (
                <section className="prt-section">
                    <h2>Notes</h2>
                    <p className="prt-text prt-character-notes">{character.notes}</p>
                </section>
            )}
        </>
    );
}

// @ts-nocheck
/** @jsxRuntime classic */
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { Dices, Pencil, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { categoryMeta, rollTable, validEntries } from "@/lib/tableMeta";
import { useExpandedCard } from "@/lib/ExpandedCardContext";

/**
 * @typedef {Object} TableCardProps
 * @property {any} table
 * @property {(table: any) => void} onEdit
 * @property {(table: any) => void} onDelete
 */

/** @param {TableCardProps} props */
export default function TableCard({ table, onEdit, onDelete }) {
  const { expanded, cardProps } = useExpandedCard(`table:${table.id}`);
  const [result, setResult] = useState(null);
  const [rolling, setRolling] = useState(false);

  const meta = categoryMeta(table.category);
  const entries = validEntries(table);
  const empty = entries.length === 0;

  const roll = () => {
    if (empty) return;
    setRolling(true);
    setResult(null);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setResult(rollTable(entries));
      setRolling(false);
    }, 350);
  };

  return (
    <div {...cardProps} className={cn("flex cursor-pointer flex-col rounded-xl border border-border bg-card/50 p-4", expanded && "ring-2 ring-primary/50")}>
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <h3 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-foreground">{table.name}</h3>
        <span className={cn("tag shrink-0 border", meta.badge)}>{meta.label}</span>
      </div>

      <span className="mt-1 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground">
        {`${entries.length} entrÃ©e${entries.length > 1 ? "s" : ""}`}
      </span>

      {table.description && (
        <p className={cn("mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground", !expanded && "line-clamp-2")}>{table.description}</p>
      )}

      {expanded && entries.length > 0 && (
        <ol className="mt-3 list-inside list-decimal space-y-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          {entries.map((entry, index) => <li key={`${entry.text}-${index}`}>{entry.text}</li>)}
        </ol>
      )}

      <div className="mt-4 flex-1" />

      {(rolling || result) && (
        <div className="corner-accent mb-3 rounded-lg border border-primary/40 bg-primary/[0.07] p-4">
          {rolling ? (
            <span className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-primary">Lancerâ€¦</span>
          ) : (
            <p className="font-display text-base leading-snug text-foreground">{result?.text}</p>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {empty && (
        <p className="mb-3 rounded-lg border border-dashed border-border p-3 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          Table vide â€” ajoutez des entrÃ©es pour pouvoir tirer.
        </p>
      )}

      <Button
        aria-label={`Tirer une entrÃ©e de ${table.name}`}
        className="w-full"
        onClick={roll}
        disabled={empty || rolling}
      >
        <Dices className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
        Tirer
      </Button>

      <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-2">
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label={`Modifier ${table.name}`}
          onClick={() => onEdit(table)}
          className="tag min-touch border border-border text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground"
        >
          <Pencil className="mr-1.5 h-3 w-3" />
          Modifier
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label={`Supprimer ${table.name}`}
          onClick={() => onDelete(table)}
          className="tag min-touch border border-border text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:border-destructive/40 hover:text-destructive"
        >
          <Trash2 className="mr-1.5 h-3 w-3" />
          Supprimer
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Plus, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import {
  Select,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import ResponsiveDialog from "@/components/common/ResponsiveDialog";
import { TABLE_CATEGORIES } from "@/lib/tableMeta";

export default function TableEditor({ open, onOpenChange, form, setForm, saving, onSave }) {
  const [quick, setQuick] = useState("");
  const entries = form.entries || [];

  const updateEntry = (index, key, value) =>
    setForm((f) => ({
      ...f,
      entries: (f.entries || []).map((entry, i) => (i === index ? { ...entry, [key]: value } : entry)),
    }));

  const addEntry = () =>
    setForm((f) => ({ ...f, entries: [...(f.entries || []), { text: "", weight: 1 }] }));

  const removeEntry = (index) =>
    setForm((f) => ({ ...f, entries: (f.entries || []).filter((_, i) => i !== index) }));

  const addQuickLines = () => {
    const lines = quick
      .split("\n")
      .map((line) => line.trim())
      .filter(Boolean);
    if (lines.length === 0) return;
    setForm((f) => ({
      ...f,
      entries: [...(f.entries || []), ...lines.map((text) => ({ text, weight: 1 }))],
    }));
    setQuick("");
  };

  const footer = (
    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
      <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}>
        Annuler
      </Button>
      <Button onClick={onSave} disabled={saving || !form.name.trim()}>
        {saving ? "Enregistrementâ€¦" : "Enregistrer"}
      </Button>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <ResponsiveDialog
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={onOpenChange}
      title={form.id ? "Modifier la table" : "Nouvelle table alÃ©atoire"}
      description="Listez les rÃ©sultats possibles, puis tirez au hasard en pleine partie."
      footer={footer}
    >
      <div className="space-y-5 py-1">
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label>Nom de la table</Label>
          <Input
            value={form.name}
            onChange={(e) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, name: e.target.value }))}
            placeholder="Ex. Rencontres en forÃªt"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label>CatÃ©gorie</Label>
            <Select
              value={form.category || "autre"}
              onValueChange={(value) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, category: value }))}
            >
              <SelectTrigger>
                <SelectValue />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                {TABLE_CATEGORIES.map((category) => (
                  <SelectItem key={category.key} value={category.key}>
                    {category.label}
                  </SelectItem>
                ))}
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label>Description</Label>
            <Input
              value={form.description || ""}
              onChange={(e) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, description: e.target.value }))}
              placeholder="Quand l'utiliser ?"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <Label>EntrÃ©es ({entries.length})</Label>
            <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={addEntry} className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-primary">
              <Plus className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" /> Ajouter
            </Button>
          </div>

          {entries.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="rounded-lg border border-dashed border-border p-3 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              Aucune entrÃ©e pour l'instant.
            </p>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-1 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">
                <span className="flex-1">RÃ©sultat</span>
                <span className="w-16 text-center">Poids</span>
                <span className="w-8" />
              </div>
              {entries.map((entry, index) => (
                <div key={index} className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Input
                    className="flex-1"
                    value={entry.text || ""}
                    onChange={(e) => updateEntry(index, "text", e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Ex. Une meute de loups affamÃ©s"
                  />
                  <Input
                    className="w-16"
                    type="number"
                    min="1"
                    value={entry.weight ?? 1}
                    onChange={(e) => updateEntry(index, "weight", e.target.value)}
                  />
                  <Button
                    variant="ghost"
                    size="icon"
                    className="h-9 w-8 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive"
                    onClick={() => removeEntry(index)}
                  >
                    <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                  </Button>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-2 rounded-lg border border-border p-3">
          <Label>Ajout rapide</Label>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Collez une liste : une entrÃ©e par ligne.</p>
          <Textarea
            rows={3}
            value={quick}
            onChange={(e) => setQuick(e.target.value)}
            placeholder={"Un sanglier blessÃ©\nDes traces de pas fraÃ®ches\nUn bÃ»cheron mÃ©fiant"}
          />
          <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={addQuickLines} disabled={!quick.trim()}>
            <Plus className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" /> Ajouter ces lignes
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </ResponsiveDialog>
  );
}

=== src\components\ui\button.jsx ===
import * as React from "react"
import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot"
import { cva } from "class-variance-authority";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const buttonVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md text-sm font-medium transition-colors focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-1 focus-visible:ring-ring disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:size-4 [&_svg]:shrink-0",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default:
          "bg-primary text-primary-foreground shadow hover:bg-primary/90",
        destructive:
          "bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground shadow-sm hover:bg-destructive/90",
        outline:
          "border border-input bg-transparent shadow-sm hover:bg-accent hover:text-accent-foreground",
        secondary:
          "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground shadow-sm hover:bg-secondary/80",
        ghost: "hover:bg-accent hover:text-accent-foreground",
        link: "text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline",
      },
      size: {
        default: "h-9 min-h-11 px-4 py-2 md:min-h-0",
        sm: "h-8 rounded-md px-3 text-xs",
        lg: "h-10 rounded-md px-8",
        icon: "h-9 w-9",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      size: "default",
    },
  }
)

const Button = React.forwardRef(({ className, variant, size, asChild = false, ...props }, ref) => {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot : "button"
  return (
    (<Comp
      className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size, className }))}
      ref={ref}
      {...props} />)
  );
})
Button.displayName = "Button"

export { Button, buttonVariants }

import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/use-toast";
import {
  Toast,
  ToastClose,
  ToastDescription,
  ToastProvider,
  ToastTitle,
  ToastViewport,
} from "@/components/ui/toast";

export function Toaster() {
  const { toasts } = useToast();

  return (
    <ToastProvider>
      {toasts.map(function ({ id, title, description, action, ...props }) {
        return (
          <Toast key={id} {...props}>
            <div className="grid gap-1">
              {title && <ToastTitle>{title}</ToastTitle>}
              {description && (
                <ToastDescription>{description}</ToastDescription>
              )}
            </div>
            {action}
            <ToastClose />
          </Toast>
        );
      })}
      <ToastViewport />
    </ToastProvider>
  );
} 
=== src\components\ui\use-toast.jsx ===
// Inspired by react-hot-toast library
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";

const TOAST_LIMIT = 20;
const TOAST_REMOVE_DELAY = 1000000;

const actionTypes = {
  ADD_TOAST: "ADD_TOAST",
  UPDATE_TOAST: "UPDATE_TOAST",
  DISMISS_TOAST: "DISMISS_TOAST",
  REMOVE_TOAST: "REMOVE_TOAST",
};

let count = 0;

function genId() {
  count = (count + 1) % Number.MAX_VALUE;
  return count.toString();
}

const toastTimeouts = new Map();

const addToRemoveQueue = (toastId) => {
  if (toastTimeouts.has(toastId)) {
    return;
  }

  const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
    toastTimeouts.delete(toastId);
    dispatch({
      type: actionTypes.REMOVE_TOAST,
      toastId,
    });
  }, TOAST_REMOVE_DELAY);

  toastTimeouts.set(toastId, timeout);
};

const _clearFromRemoveQueue = (toastId) => {
  const timeout = toastTimeouts.get(toastId);
  if (timeout) {
    clearTimeout(timeout);
    toastTimeouts.delete(toastId);
  }
};

export const reducer = (state, action) => {
  switch (action.type) {
    case actionTypes.ADD_TOAST:
      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: [action.toast, ...state.toasts].slice(0, TOAST_LIMIT),
      };

    case actionTypes.UPDATE_TOAST:
      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: state.toasts.map((t) =>
          t.id === action.toast.id ? { ...t, ...action.toast } : t
        ),
      };

    case actionTypes.DISMISS_TOAST: {
      const { toastId } = action;

      // ! Side effects ! - This could be extracted into a dismissToast() action,
      // but I'll keep it here for simplicity
      if (toastId) {
        addToRemoveQueue(toastId);
      } else {
        state.toasts.forEach((toast) => {
          addToRemoveQueue(toast.id);
        });
      }

      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: state.toasts.map((t) =>
          t.id === toastId || toastId === undefined
            ? {
                ...t,
                open: false,
              }
            : t
        ),
      };
    }
    case actionTypes.REMOVE_TOAST:
      if (action.toastId === undefined) {
        return {
          ...state,
          toasts: [],
        };
      }
      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: state.toasts.filter((t) => t.id !== action.toastId),
      };
  }
};

const listeners = [];

let memoryState = { toasts: [] };

function dispatch(action) {
  memoryState = reducer(memoryState, action);
  listeners.forEach((listener) => {
    listener(memoryState);
  });
}

function toast({ ...props }) {
  const id = genId();

  const update = (props) =>
    dispatch({
      type: actionTypes.UPDATE_TOAST,
      toast: { ...props, id },
    });

  const dismiss = () =>
    dispatch({ type: actionTypes.DISMISS_TOAST, toastId: id });

  dispatch({
    type: actionTypes.ADD_TOAST,
    toast: {
      ...props,
      id,
      open: true,
      onOpenChange: (open) => {
        if (!open) dismiss();
      },
    },
  });

  return {
    id,
    dismiss,
    update,
  };
}

function useToast() {
  const [state, setState] = useState(memoryState);

  useEffect(() => {
    listeners.push(setState);
    return () => {
      const index = listeners.indexOf(setState);
      if (index > -1) {
        listeners.splice(index, 1);
      }
    };
  }, [state]);

  return {
    ...state,
    toast,
    dismiss: (toastId) => dispatch({ type: actionTypes.DISMISS_TOAST, toastId }),
  };
}

export { useToast, toast };

import React, { Suspense, useCallback } from "react";
import { useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import SideRail from "@/components/nav/SideRail";
import MobileNav from "@/components/nav/MobileNav";
import MobileBackBar from "@/components/MobileBackBar";
import PageLoader from "@/components/PageLoader";
import PullToRefresh from "@/components/PullToRefresh";
import { queryClientInstance } from "@/lib/query-client";
import { useDataRefresh } from "@/lib/RefreshContext";
import { useExpandedCardState } from "@/lib/ExpandedCardContext";

export default function AppShell({ children }) {
  const { pathname } = useLocation();
  const { refresh } = useDataRefresh();
  const { setExpandedCardId } = useExpandedCardState();

  const handleRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    queryClientInstance.invalidateQueries();
    await refresh();
  }, [refresh]);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-background text-foreground">
      <SideRail />
      <main className="pb-24 pl-[env(safe-area-inset-left)] pr-[env(safe-area-inset-right)] md:pb-0 md:pl-20">
        <MobileBackBar />
        <PullToRefresh onRefresh={handleRefresh}>
          <Suspense fallback={<PageLoader />}>
            <div key={pathname} className="page-transition">
              <div
                onClick={(event) => {
                  if (!event.target.closest("[data-expandable-card]")) setExpandedCardId(null);
                }}
              >
                {children}
              </div>
            </div>
          </Suspense>
        </PullToRefresh>
      </main>
      <MobileNav />
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Folder, FolderOpen, RotateCcw, Save } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/use-toast";
import { hasAutosave, saveNow, useAutosaveStatus } from "@/lib/autosave";
import { restoreFromPayload, validateBackupPayload } from "@/lib/backup";

const KIND_LABEL = { current: "Courante", auto: "Automatique", archive: "Archive", other: "Autre" };

const when = (t) => new Date(t).toLocaleString("fr-FR", { dateStyle: "short", timeStyle: "medium" });
const sizeOf = (n) => (n > 1048576 ? `${(n / 1048576).toFixed(1)} Mo` : `${Math.max(1, Math.round(n / 1024))} Ko`);

export default function AutosavePanel() {
    const { toast } = useToast();
    const status = useAutosaveStatus();
    const [info, setInfo] = useState(null);
    const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

    const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
        if (!hasAutosave()) return;
        try {
            setInfo(await window.arcanesBackup.info());
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(error);
        }
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
        refresh();
    }, [refresh, status.savedAt]);

    if (!hasAutosave()) {
        return (
            <section className="space-y-2 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card/50 p-4">
                <h2 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-foreground">Sauvegarde automatique</h2>
                <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                    Disponible dans le programme Windows (installateur ou <code>npm run electron</code>), pas dans le
                    navigateur.
                </p>
            </section>
        );
    }

    const saveManual = async () => {
        setBusy(true);
        const result = await saveNow({ force: true });
        setBusy(false);
        toast(
            result.ok
                ? { title: "Sauvegarde effectuÃ©e" }
                : { variant: "destructive", title: "Sauvegarde impossible", description: result.error }
        );
    };

    const chooseFolder = async () => {
        const folder = await window.arcanesBackup.chooseFolder();
        if (!folder) return;
        await saveNow({ force: true });
        await refresh();
        toast({ title: "Dossier modifiÃ©", description: "Les anciennes sauvegardes restent dans l'ancien dossier." });
    };

    const restore = async (file) => {
        if (
            !window.confirm(
                `Remplacer toutes les donnÃ©es actuelles par la sauvegarde du ${when(file.mtime)} ?\nL'Ã©tat actuel sera d'abord conservÃ© dans une sauvegarde.`
            )
        )
            return;
        setBusy(true);
        try {
            // Lecture d'abord : l'instantanÃ© ci-dessous peut supprimer la plus ancienne version.
            const text = await window.arcanesBackup.read(file.name);
            if (!text) throw new Error("Fichier introuvable.");
            const payload = JSON.parse(text);
            validateBackupPayload(payload);
            await saveNow({ force: true });
            await restoreFromPayload(payload);
            await saveNow();
            window.location.reload();
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Restauration impossible :", error);
            setBusy(false);
            toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Restauration impossible", description: error?.message });
        }
    };

    const statusText =
        status.state === "saving"
            ? "Enregistrement en coursâ€¦"
            : status.state === "error"
                ? `Erreur : ${status.error}`
                : status.savedAt
                    ? `DerniÃ¨re sauvegarde : ${when(status.savedAt)}`
                    : "En attente de la premiÃ¨re modification.";

    const files = (info?.files || []).slice(0, 12);

    return (
        <section className="space-y-3 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card/50 p-4">
            <h2 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-foreground">Sauvegarde automatique</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                Vos donnÃ©es sont enregistrÃ©es dans un fichier aprÃ¨s chaque modification, avec des versions datÃ©es.
                Les images sont incluses : le fichier peut Ãªtre volumineux.
            </p>
            <p className={status.state === "error" ? "text-sm text-destructive" : "text-sm text-foreground"}>
                {statusText}
            </p>
            {info?.folder && (
                <p className="break-all font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground">Dossier : {info.folder}</p>
            )}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={saveManual} disabled={busy}>
                    <Save className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Sauvegarder maintenant
                </Button>
                <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={chooseFolder} disabled={busy}>
                    <Folder className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Changer le dossier
                </Button>
                <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => window.arcanesBackup.openFolder()}>
                    <FolderOpen className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Ouvrir le dossier
                </Button>
            </div>

            {files.length > 0 && (
                <ul className="max-h-64 space-y-1.5 overflow-y-auto pt-1">
                    {files.map((file) => (
                        <li
                            key={file.name}
                            className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-lg border border-border bg-background/60 p-2 text-xs"
                        >
                            <span className="min-w-0">
                                <span className="block font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">
                                    {KIND_LABEL[file.kind]}
                                </span>
                                <span className="block text-foreground">
                                    {when(file.mtime)} Â· {sizeOf(file.size)}
                                </span>
                            </span>
                            {file.kind !== "current" && (
                                <Button type="button" size="sm" variant="ghost" disabled={busy} onClick={() => restore(file)}>
                                    <RotateCcw className="mr-1 h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Restaurer
                                </Button>
                            )}
                        </li>
                    ))}
                </ul>
            )}
        </section>
    );
}

export default function BackupPanel() {

  const inputRef = useRef(null);

  const handleImport = async (event) => {
    const file = event.target.files?.[0];
    event.target.value = "";
    if (!file) return;
    if (!window.confirm("Remplacer toutes les donnÃ©es actuelles par cette sauvegarde ?")) return;
    try {
      await saveNow({ force: true });
      await importBackup(file);
      await saveNow();
      toast({ title: "Sauvegarde importÃ©e", description: "Les donnÃ©es ont bien Ã©tÃ© restaurÃ©es." });
      window.location.reload();

      toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Import impossible", description: message });
    }
  };

        </Button>

      </div>

    <div className="sticky top-0 z-30 flex items-center gap-1 border-b border-border bg-background/90 px-1 py-1 pt-[env(safe-area-inset-top)] backdrop-blur md:hidden">
      <button
        type="button"

        onClick={() => navigate(-1)}
        className="grid h-11 w-11 place-items-center rounded-lg text-muted-foreground transition-colors active:bg-secondary"
      >
        <ChevronLeft className="h-5 w-5" />
      </button>

=== src\components\PullToRefresh.jsx ===
import React, { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { RefreshCw } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const THRESHOLD = 70;

const isAtTop = (node) => {
  if (window.scrollY > 0) return false;
  let parent = node?.parentElement;
  while (parent) {
    if (parent.scrollTop > 0) return false;
    parent = parent.parentElement;
  }
  return true;
};

export default function PullToRefresh({ children, onRefresh }) {
  const [pull, setPull] = useState(0);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const startY = useRef(null);
  const busy = useRef(false);

  const handleTouchStart = (event) => {
    if (busy.current) return;
    startY.current = isAtTop(event.currentTarget) ? event.touches[0].clientY : null;
  };

  const handleTouchMove = (event) => {
    if (startY.current === null) return;
    const delta = event.touches[0].clientY - startY.current;
    setPull(delta <= 0 ? 0 : Math.min(delta * 0.5, 90));
  };

  const handleTouchEnd = async () => {
    if (startY.current === null) return;
    startY.current = null;
    if (pull < THRESHOLD) {
      setPull(0);
      return;
    }
    setPull(THRESHOLD);
    setRefreshing(true);
    busy.current = true;
    try {
      if (onRefresh) await onRefresh();
      else window.location.reload();
    } finally {
      busy.current = false;
      setRefreshing(false);
      setPull(0);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      onTouchStart={handleTouchStart}
      onTouchMove={handleTouchMove}
      onTouchEnd={handleTouchEnd}
      onTouchCancel={handleTouchEnd}
    >
      <div
        className="flex items-end justify-center overflow-hidden transition-[height] duration-200 md:hidden"
        style={{ height: pull }}
      >
        <RefreshCw
          className={cn("mb-2 h-5 w-5 text-primary", refreshing && "animate-spin")}
          style={
            refreshing
              ? undefined
              : { opacity: Math.min(pull / THRESHOLD, 1), transform: `rotate(${pull * 4}deg)` }
          }
        />
      </div>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { db } from "@/api/localClient";
import {
    AlertDialog,
    AlertDialogAction,
    AlertDialogCancel,
    AlertDialogContent,
    AlertDialogDescription,
    AlertDialogFooter,
    AlertDialogHeader,
    AlertDialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/use-toast";
import { hasAutosave, saveNow } from "@/lib/autosave";
import { restoreFromPayload, validateBackupPayload } from "@/lib/backup";

export default function RestorePrompt() {
    const { toast } = useToast();
    const [found, setFound] = useState(null);
    const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!hasAutosave()) return;
        let alive = true;
        (async () => {
            try {
                if ((await db.table("Campaign").count()) > 0) return;
                const text = await window.arcanesBackup.read("arcanes-courante.json");
                if (!text) return;
                const payload = JSON.parse(text);
                validateBackupPayload(payload);
                const campaigns = payload.data.Campaign?.length || 0;
                if (campaigns > 0 && alive) setFound({ payload, campaigns });
            } catch (error) {
                console.error("VÃ©rification de la sauvegarde automatique :", error);
            }
        })();
        return () => {
            alive = false;
        };
    }, []);

    const restore = async () => {
        setBusy(true);
        try {
            await restoreFromPayload(found.payload);
            await saveNow();
            window.location.reload();
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Restauration impossible :", error);
            setBusy(false);
            toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Restauration impossible", description: error?.message });
        }
    };

    // Refus : l'ancienne sauvegarde est archivÃ©e (jamais supprimÃ©e), puis l'Ã©tat vide devient la rÃ©fÃ©rence.
    const decline = async () => {
        setBusy(true);
        await window.arcanesBackup.archiveCurrent();
        await saveNow();
        setBusy(false);
        setFound(null);
    };

    return (
        <AlertDialog open={!!found} onOpenChange={() => { }}>
            <AlertDialogContent>
                <AlertDialogHeader>
                    <AlertDialogTitle>Sauvegarde automatique trouvÃ©e</AlertDialogTitle>
                    <AlertDialogDescription>
                        L'application est vide, mais une sauvegarde automatique contient {found?.campaigns} campagne
                        {found?.campaigns > 1 ? "s" : ""}. Voulez-vous la restaurer ? Si vous refusez, elle est archivÃ©e dans
                        le dossier des sauvegardes.
                    </AlertDialogDescription>
                </AlertDialogHeader>
                <AlertDialogFooter>
                    <AlertDialogCancel disabled={busy} onClick={decline}>
                        Ne pas restaurer
                    </AlertDialogCancel>
                    <AlertDialogAction
                        disabled={busy}
                        onClick={(e) => {
                            e.preventDefault();
                            restore();
                        }}
                    >
                        {busy ? "En coursâ€¦" : "Restaurer"}
                    </AlertDialogAction>
                </AlertDialogFooter>
            </AlertDialogContent>
        </AlertDialog>
    );
}
=== src\components\ScrollToTop.jsx ===
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useLocation, useNavigationType } from "react-router-dom";

const getHashId = (hash) => {
  const rawId = hash.slice(1);

  try {
    return decodeURIComponent(rawId);
  } catch {
    return rawId;
  }
};

export default function ScrollToTop() {
  const { pathname, hash } = useLocation();
  const navigationType = useNavigationType();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (navigationType === "POP") return;

    if (hash) {
      const id = getHashId(hash);
      const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
        document.getElementById(id)?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" });
      }, 50);
      return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
    }

    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, left: 0, behavior: "instant" });
  }, [pathname, hash, navigationType]);

  return null;
}

=== src\hooks\use-mobile.jsx ===
import * as React from "react"

const MOBILE_BREAKPOINT = 768

export function useIsMobile() {
  const [isMobile, setIsMobile] = React.useState(undefined)

  React.useEffect(() => {
    const mql = window.matchMedia(`(max-width: ${MOBILE_BREAKPOINT - 1}px)`)
    const onChange = () => {
      setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT)
    }
    mql.addEventListener("change", onChange)
    setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT)
    return () => mql.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, [])

  return !!isMobile
}
