=== src\lib\autosave.js ===
import { useSyncExternalStore } from "react";
import { db, TABLES } from "@/api/localClient";
import { buildBackupPayload } from "@/lib/backup";

const DEBOUNCE_MS = 2000;
const MAX_WAIT_MS = 20000;

export const hasAutosave = () => typeof window !== "undefined" && !!window.arcanesBackup;

// ---------- Ã‰tat observable (pour l'affichage dans les ParamÃ¨tres) ----------

let status = { supported: hasAutosave(), state: "idle", savedAt: null, error: "" };
const listeners = new Set();

const setStatus = (patch) => {
    status = { ...status, ...patch };
    listeners.forEach((listener) => listener());
};

const subscribe = (listener) => {
    listeners.add(listener);
    return () => listeners.delete(listener);
};

export const useAutosaveStatus = () => useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, () => status);

// ---------- Ã‰criture ----------

let chain = Promise.resolve();

function write(options) {
    chain = chain.then(async () => {
        setStatus({ state: "saving" });
        try {
            const payload = await buildBackupPayload();
            const result = await window.arcanesBackup.write(JSON.stringify(payload), options);
            if (!result?.ok) throw new Error(result?.error || "Ã‰criture impossible.");
            setStatus({ state: "ok", savedAt: result.savedAt, error: "" });
            return result;
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Sauvegarde automatique :", error);
            const message = error?.message || String(error);
            setStatus({ state: "error", error: message });
            return { ok: false, error: message };
        }
    });
    return chain;
}

let timer = null;
let firstChangeAt = 0;

export function scheduleAutosave() {
    if (!hasAutosave()) return;
    const now = Date.now();
    if (!firstChangeAt) firstChangeAt = now;
    clearTimeout(timer);
    const wait = Math.min(DEBOUNCE_MS, Math.max(0, firstChangeAt + MAX_WAIT_MS - now));
    timer = setTimeout(() => {
        timer = null;
        firstChangeAt = 0;
        write({});
    }, wait);
}

// Ã‰crit tout de suite. { force: true } ajoute aussi un instantanÃ© datÃ©.
export async function saveNow(options = {}) {
    if (!hasAutosave()) return { ok: false, error: "Indisponible hors du programme Windows." };
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = null;
    firstChangeAt = 0;
    return write(options);
}

let initialized = false;

export function initAutosave() {
    if (initialized || !hasAutosave()) return;
    initialized = true;
    TABLES.forEach((name) => {
        const table = db.table(name);
        table.hook("creating", () => {
            scheduleAutosave();
        });
        table.hook("updating", () => {
            scheduleAutosave();
        });
        table.hook("deleting", () => {
            scheduleAutosave();
        });
    });
}
=== src\lib\backup.js ===
import { db, TABLES } from "@/api/localClient";

const APP_ID = "arcanes-scenario";
const BACKUP_VERSION = 1;
const MAX_BACKUP_SIZE = 500 * 1024 * 1024;

export function validateBackupPayload(parsed) {
  if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== "object") throw new Error("Fichier de sauvegarde invalide.");
  if (parsed.app !== APP_ID) throw new Error("Ce fichier n'est pas une sauvegarde de cette application.");
  if (parsed.version !== BACKUP_VERSION) throw new Error("Version de sauvegarde incompatible.");
  if (!parsed.data || typeof parsed.data !== "object") throw new Error("Contenu de sauvegarde absent.");

  for (const table of TABLES) {
    const rows = parsed.data[table];
    if (rows != null && !Array.isArray(rows)) {
      throw new Error(`DonnÃ©es de sauvegarde invalides pour ${table}.`);
    }
  }
}

export async function buildBackupPayload() {
  const data = {};
  for (const t of TABLES) data[t] = await db.table(t).toArray();
  return { app: APP_ID, version: BACKUP_VERSION, exported_at: new Date().toISOString(), data };
}

export async function restoreFromPayload(parsed) {
  validateBackupPayload(parsed);
  await db.transaction("rw", TABLES.map((t) => db.table(t)), async () => {
    for (const t of TABLES) {
      await db.table(t).clear();
      const rows = parsed.data[t] || [];
      if (rows.length) await db.table(t).bulkPut(rows);
    }
  });
}

export async function exportBackup() {
  const payload = await buildBackupPayload();
  const json = JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2);
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([json], { type: "application/json" }));
  const a = document.createElement("a");
  a.href = url;
  a.download = `arcanes-sauvegarde-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.json`;
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  a.remove();
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
}

export async function importBackup(file) {
  if (!file || !(file instanceof File)) throw new Error("Aucun fichier sÃ©lectionnÃ©.");
  if (file.size > MAX_BACKUP_SIZE) throw new Error("Le fichier de sauvegarde est trop volumineux.");
  if (!file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith(".json") && file.type && !file.type.includes("json")) {
    throw new Error("Le fichier doit Ãªtre un JSON valide.");
  }

  let parsed;
  try {
    parsed = JSON.parse(await file.text());
  } catch {
    throw new Error("Le fichier de sauvegarde est invalide ou corrompu.");
  }

  await restoreFromPayload(parsed);
}

import { api } from "@/api/localClient";

// Enfants d'abord, la campagne en dernier.
export const LINKED_ENTITIES = [
  { entity: "SessionPoll", label: "sondage", plural: "sondages" },
  { entity: "NPCRelation", label: "relation entre PNJ", plural: "relations entre PNJ" },
  { entity: "GameCharacter", label: "personnage", plural: "personnages" },
  { entity: "GameSheetModel", label: "modÃ¨le de fiche", plural: "modÃ¨les de fiche" },
  { entity: "SkillCatalog", label: "compÃ©tence du catalogue", plural: "compÃ©tences du catalogue" },
  { entity: "EquipmentCatalog", label: "Ã©quipement du catalogue", plural: "Ã©quipements du catalogue" },
  { entity: "Clue", label: "indice", plural: "indices" },
  { entity: "Scene", label: "scÃ¨ne", plural: "scÃ¨nes" },
  { entity: "Act", label: "acte", plural: "actes" },
  { entity: "NPC", label: "PNJ", plural: "PNJ" },
  { entity: "Location", label: "lieu", plural: "lieux" },
  { entity: "Note", label: "note", plural: "notes" },
  { entity: "RollTable", label: "table alÃ©atoire", plural: "tables alÃ©atoires" },
  { entity: "GameSession", label: "session de jeu", plural: "sessions de jeu" },
];

const PAGE = 5000;

// Liste ce qui sera supprimÃ©, pour la confirmation.
export async function countCampaignContent(campaignId) {
  const results = await Promise.all(
    LINKED_ENTITIES.map(async ({ entity, label, plural }) => {
      const res = await api.entities[entity].filter({ campaign_id: campaignId }, { limit: PAGE });
      const rows = res.items.length;
      // Une relation est stockÃ©e en deux lignes (une par PNJ) : on compte les paires.
      const count = entity === "NPCRelation" ? Math.ceil(rows / 2) : rows;
      return {
        entity,
        count,
        text: `${count}${rows >= PAGE ? "+" : ""} ${count > 1 ? plural : label}`,
      };
    })
  );
  return results.filter((r) => r.count > 0);
}

// Supprime tout le contenu, vÃ©rifie qu'il ne reste rien, puis supprime la campagne.
export async function deleteCampaignCascade(campaignId) {
  for (const { entity } of LINKED_ENTITIES) {
    await api.entities[entity].deleteMany({ campaign_id: campaignId });
    const left = await api.entities[entity].filter({ campaign_id: campaignId }, { limit: 1 });
    if (left.items.length > 0) {
      throw new Error(`Il reste des Ã©lÃ©ments de type ${entity} : la campagne n'a pas Ã©tÃ© supprimÃ©e.`);
    }
  }
  await api.entities.Campaign.delete(campaignId);
}

=== src\lib\editablePdf.js ===
import { PDFDocument, StandardFonts, rgb } from "pdf-lib";

const PAGE_WIDTH = 595.28;
const PAGE_HEIGHT = 841.89;
const MARGIN = 42;
const CONTENT_WIDTH = PAGE_WIDTH - MARGIN * 2;
const BODY_COLOR = rgb(0.08, 0.08, 0.08);
const MUTED_COLOR = rgb(0.32, 0.32, 0.32);
const FIELD_BORDER = rgb(0.55, 0.55, 0.55);
const FIELD_BACKGROUND = rgb(1, 1, 1);

const safeFieldText = (value) =>
    String(value ?? "")
        .replace(/[â€œâ€]/g, '"')
        .replace(/[â€˜â€™]/g, "'")
        .replace(/[â€”â€“]/g, "-")
        .replace(/â€¦/g, "...")
        .replace(/[â€¢Â·]/g, "-")
        .replace(/[^\u0000-\u00FF]/g, "?");

const fileSafe = (value) =>
    String(value || "Campagne").replace(/[<>:"/\\|?*\x00-\x1F]/g, "_").slice(0, 100);

const fieldSafe = (value) =>
    String(value || "champ")
        .normalize("NFD")
        .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
        .toLowerCase()
        .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_")
        .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "")
        .slice(0, 32) || "champ";

function wrapText(text, font, size, maxWidth) {
    const lines = [];
    String(text ?? "").split(/\r?\n/).forEach((paragraph) => {
        if (!paragraph) {
            lines.push("");
            return;
        }
        let line = "";
        paragraph.split(/\s+/).forEach((word) => {
            const candidate = line ? `${line} ${word}` : word;
            if (line && font.widthOfTextAtSize(candidate, size) > maxWidth) {
                lines.push(line);
                line = word;
            } else {
                line = candidate;
            }
        });
        lines.push(line);
    });
    return lines;
}

async function embedImage(document, dataUrl) {
    const match = /^data:image\/(png|jpe?g);base64,([\s\S]+)$/i.exec(dataUrl || "");
    if (match) {
        const binary = atob(match[2]);
        const bytes = Uint8Array.from(binary, (character) => character.charCodeAt(0));
        return match[1].toLowerCase() === "png" ? document.embedPng(bytes) : document.embedJpg(bytes);
    }

    const response = await fetch(dataUrl);
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error("Une image jointe n'a pas pu Ãªtre lue pour le PDF.");
    const bitmap = await createImageBitmap(await response.blob());
    const canvas = globalThis.document.createElement("canvas");
    canvas.width = bitmap.width;
    canvas.height = bitmap.height;
    const context = canvas.getContext("2d");
    if (!context) throw new Error("La conversion d'une image jointe en PDF est impossible.");
    context.drawImage(bitmap, 0, 0);
    bitmap.close();
    const png = await new Promise((resolve) => canvas.toBlob(resolve, "image/png"));
    if (!png) throw new Error("La conversion d'une image jointe en PDF est impossible.");
    return document.embedPng(new Uint8Array(await png.arrayBuffer()));
}

export async function createEditablePdf(model) {
    const document = await PDFDocument.create();
    const form = document.getForm();
    const regular = await document.embedFont(StandardFonts.Helvetica);
    const bold = await document.embedFont(StandardFonts.HelveticaBold);
    const imageCache = new Map();
    let page;
    let cursorY;
    let fieldIndex = 0;

    const addPage = () => {
        page = document.addPage([PAGE_WIDTH, PAGE_HEIGHT]);
        cursorY = PAGE_HEIGHT - MARGIN;
    };

    const ensureSpace = (height) => {
        if (!page) addPage();
        if (cursorY - height < MARGIN) addPage();
    };

    const drawText = (text, { size = 10, font = regular, color = BODY_COLOR, indent = 0 } = {}) => {
        const lines = wrapText(safeFieldText(text), font, size, CONTENT_WIDTH - indent);
        const lineHeight = size * 1.4;
        lines.forEach((line) => {
            ensureSpace(lineHeight);
            page.drawText(line, {
                x: MARGIN + indent,
                y: cursorY - size,
                size,
                font,
                color,
            });
            cursorY -= lineHeight;
        });
        return lines.length;
    };

    const drawHeading = (text, level = 2) => {
        const size = level === 1 ? 20 : level === 2 ? 14 : 11;
        ensureSpace(size * 2.4);
        cursorY -= 5;
        drawText(text, { size, font: bold });
        page.drawLine({
            start: { x: MARGIN, y: cursorY + 2 },
            end: { x: PAGE_WIDTH - MARGIN, y: cursorY + 2 },
            thickness: level === 1 ? 1.5 : 0.6,
            color: FIELD_BORDER,
        });
        cursorY -= level === 1 ? 12 : 8;
    };

    const drawField = (label, value, { multiline = false, height = 24 } = {}) => {
        const labelSize = 8;
        ensureSpace(labelSize * 1.5 + height + 9);
        if (label) {
            page.drawText(safeFieldText(label), {
                x: MARGIN,
                y: cursorY - labelSize,
                size: labelSize,
                font: bold,
                color: MUTED_COLOR,
            });
            cursorY -= labelSize + 4;
        }

        const name = `field_${String(++fieldIndex).padStart(4, "0")}_${fieldSafe(label)}`;
        const field = form.createTextField(name);
        const text = safeFieldText(value);
        if (text) field.setText(text);
        if (multiline) field.enableMultiline();
        field.addToPage(page, {

            textColor: BODY_COLOR,
        });

    };

                }

        }
    }

        filename: `Arcanes Scenario - ${fileSafe(model.campaignName)}.pdf`,

=== src\lib\ExpandedCardContext.jsx ===
import React, { createContext, useContext, useMemo, useState } from "react";

const ExpandedCardContext = createContext({ expandedCardId: null, setExpandedCardId: () => {} });

export function ExpandedCardProvider({ children }) {
  const [expandedCardId, setExpandedCardId] = useState(null);
  const value = useMemo(() => ({ expandedCardId, setExpandedCardId }), [expandedCardId]);
  return <ExpandedCardContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</ExpandedCardContext.Provider>;
}

export function useExpandedCard(cardId) {
  const context = useContext(ExpandedCardContext);
  const expanded = context.expandedCardId === cardId;
  return {
    expanded,
    cardProps: {
      "data-expandable-card": cardId,
      "aria-expanded": expanded,
      tabIndex: 0,
      onClick: (event) => {
        if (event.target.closest("button, a, input, select, textarea, [role='button']")) return;
        context.setExpandedCardId((current) => current === cardId ? null : cardId);
      },
      onKeyDown: (event) => {
        if ((event.key === "Enter" || event.key === " ") && event.target === event.currentTarget) {
          event.preventDefault();
          context.setExpandedCardId((current) => current === cardId ? null : cardId);
        }
      },
    },
    setExpanded: (next) => context.setExpandedCardId(next ? cardId : null),
  };
}

export function useExpandedCardState() {
  return useContext(ExpandedCardContext);
}

=== src\lib\locationTree.js ===
import { api } from "@/api/localClient";

const parentOf = (location) => location.parent_id || "";

const byName = (a, b) => (a.name || "").localeCompare(b.name || "", "fr", { numeric: true });

// Regroupe les lieux par parent. Un parent introuvable est traitÃ© comme "premier niveau".
export function childrenMap(locations) {
  const ids = new Set(locations.map((l) => l.id));
  const map = new Map();
  for (const location of locations) {
    const parent = ids.has(parentOf(location)) ? parentOf(location) : "";
    if (!map.has(parent)) map.set(parent, []);
    map.get(parent).push(location);
  }
  map.forEach((list) => list.sort(byName));
  return map;
}

// Liste Ã  plat dans l'ordre de l'arbre, avec la profondeur de chaque lieu.
export function flattenTree(locations, { collapsed = new Set(), exclude = new Set() } = {}) {
  const map = childrenMap(locations);
  const rows = [];
  const walk = (parentId, depth, seen) => {
    for (const location of map.get(parentId) || []) {
      if (exclude.has(location.id) || seen.has(location.id)) continue;
      const kids = (map.get(location.id) || []).filter((k) => !exclude.has(k.id));
      rows.push({ location, depth, childCount: kids.length });
      if (!collapsed.has(location.id)) walk(location.id, depth + 1, new Set(seen).add(location.id));
    }
  };
  walk("", 0, new Set());
  return rows;
}

// Tous les descendants d'un lieu (tous niveaux).
export function descendantIds(locations, id) {
  const map = childrenMap(locations);
  const out = [];
  const seen = new Set([id]);
  const walk = (parentId) => {
    for (const child of map.get(parentId) || []) {
      if (seen.has(child.id)) continue;
      seen.add(child.id);
      out.push(child.id);
      walk(child.id);
    }
  };
  walk(id);
  return out;
}

// Chemin de la racine jusqu'au lieu (lui compris).
export function pathOf(locations, id) {
  const byId = new Map(locations.map((l) => [l.id, l]));
  const path = [];
  const seen = new Set();
  let current = byId.get(id);
  while (current && !seen.has(current.id)) {
    seen.add(current.id);
    path.unshift(current);
    current = byId.get(parentOf(current));
  }
  return path;
}

// Choix possibles pour le menu "Lieu parent" (sans le lieu lui-mÃªme ni ses descendants).
export function parentOptions(locations, excludeId) {
  const exclude = new Set();
  if (excludeId) {
    exclude.add(excludeId);
    descendantIds(locations, excludeId).forEach((id) => exclude.add(id));
  }
  return flattenTree(locations, { exclude }).map(({ location }) => ({
    id: location.id,
    label: pathOf(locations, location.id).map((l) => l.name).join(" â€º "),
  }));
}

async function removeFromScenes(campaignId, removedIds) {
  const gone = new Set(removedIds);
  const res = await api.entities.Scene.filter({ campaign_id: campaignId }, { limit: 1000 });
  for (const scene of res.items) {
    const ids = scene.location_ids || [];
    if (ids.some((id) => gone.has(id))) {
      await api.entities.Scene.update(scene.id, { location_ids: ids.filter((id) => !gone.has(id)) });
    }
  }
}

// mode = "promote" : les enfants directs remontent d'un niveau.
// mode = "cascade" : le lieu et tous ses descendants sont supprimÃ©s.
async function reassignNpcs(campaignId, removedIds, newLocationId) {
  const gone = new Set(removedIds);
  const res = await api.entities.NPC.filter({ campaign_id: campaignId }, { limit: 1000 });
  for (const npc of res.items) {
    if (gone.has(npc.location_id)) {
      await api.entities.NPC.update(npc.id, { location_id: newLocationId });
    }
  }
}
export async function deleteLocation(location, locations, mode) {
  let removed = [location.id];
  if (mode === "cascade") {
    removed = [location.id, ...descendantIds(locations, location.id)];
    await api.entities.Location.deleteMany({ id: { $in: removed } });
  } else {
    const direct = locations.filter((l) => parentOf(l) === location.id);
    for (const child of direct) {
      await api.entities.Location.update(child.id, { parent_id: parentOf(location) });
    }
    await api.entities.Location.delete(location.id);
  }
  await removeFromScenes(location.campaign_id, removed);
  await reassignNpcs(location.campaign_id, removed, mode === "cascade" ? "" : parentOf(location));
}

// Copie tous les sous-lieux d'un lieu sous un nouveau parent (nouveaux identifiants).
export async function copyLocationSubtree(locations, sourceId, newParentId) {
  const map = childrenMap(locations);
  const walk = async (oldParentId, newParent, depth) => {
    if (depth > 30) return;
    for (const child of map.get(oldParentId) || []) {
      const { id, created_date, updated_date, created_by_id, created_by, ...rest } = child;
      const copy = await api.entities.Location.create({ ...rest, parent_id: newParent });
      await walk(child.id, copy.id, depth + 1);
    }
  };
  await walk(sourceId, newParentId, 0);
}
=== src\lib\npcRelations.js ===
import { api } from "@/api/localClient";
import { CUSTOM_TYPE, groupIndex, inverseOf, parentEdge, relationLabel } from "@/lib/relationMeta";
const table = () => api.entities.NPCRelation;

const uid = () =>
  globalThis.crypto?.randomUUID?.() ||
  `${Date.now().toString(36)}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)}`;

export async function loadRelations(campaignId) {
  const res = await table().filter({ campaign_id: campaignId }, { limit: 5000 });
  return res.items;
}

export const mirrorOf = (row, relations) =>
  relations.find((r) => r.pair_id === row.pair_id && r.id !== row.id) || null;

// "ok" | "one_sided" (pas de rÃ©ciproque) | "mismatch" (rÃ©ciproque diffÃ©rente)
export function pairStatus(row, relations) {
  const mirror = mirrorOf(row, relations);
  if (!mirror) return "one_sided";
  const sameEnds = mirror.from_id === row.to_id && mirror.to_id === row.from_id;
  const sameTypes =
    row.type === CUSTOM_TYPE || mirror.type === CUSTOM_TYPE
      ? row.type === mirror.type
      : mirror.type === inverseOf(row.type);
  return sameEnds && sameTypes ? "ok" : "mismatch";
}

// Renvoie un message d'erreur, ou "" si la relation est valide.
export function validateRelation(relations, { fromId, toId, type, ignorePairId }) {
  if (!toId) return "Choisissez un PNJ.";
  if (fromId === toId) return "Un PNJ ne peut pas Ãªtre liÃ© Ã  lui-mÃªme.";
  const others = relations.filter((r) => r.pair_id !== ignorePairId);

  if (type !== CUSTOM_TYPE && others.some((r) => r.from_id === fromId && r.to_id === toId && r.type === type)) {
    return "Cette relation existe dÃ©jÃ  entre ces deux PNJ.";
  }

  const edge = parentEdge({ type, from_id: fromId, to_id: toId });
  if (edge) {
    const [parent, child] = edge;
    const edges = others.map(parentEdge).filter(Boolean);
    const seen = new Set([parent]);
    const queue = [parent];
    while (queue.length) {
      const node = queue.pop();
      for (const [p, c] of edges) {
        if (c !== node || seen.has(p)) continue;
        if (p === child) return "Cette filiation crÃ©erait une boucle : un PNJ serait son propre ancÃªtre.";
        seen.add(p);
        queue.push(p);
      }
    }
  }
  return "";
}

export async function createRelation(
  campaignId,
  { fromId, toId, type, label = "", inverseLabel = "", notes = "", oneSided = false }
) {
  const pair_id = uid();
  const base = { campaign_id: campaignId, pair_id, notes };
  const first = await table().create({
    ...base,
    from_id: fromId,
    to_id: toId,
    type,
    custom_label: type === CUSTOM_TYPE ? label : "",
  });
  if (oneSided) return;
  try {
    await table().create({
      ...base,
      from_id: toId,
      to_id: fromId,
      type: inverseOf(type),
      custom_label: type === CUSTOM_TYPE ? inverseLabel : "",
    });
  } catch (error) {
    await table().delete(first.id).catch(() => { });
    throw error;
  }
}

// manual = true : seul ce cÃ´tÃ© est modifiÃ©. Sinon la ligne miroir est mise Ã  jour (ou recrÃ©Ã©e).
export async function updateRelation(
  row,
  relations,
  { toId, type, label = "", inverseLabel = "", notes = "", manual = false }
) {
  await table().update(row.id, {
    to_id: toId,
    type,
    custom_label: type === CUSTOM_TYPE ? label : "",
    notes,
  });
  if (manual) return;
  const mirror = mirrorOf(row, relations);
  const data = {
    from_id: toId,
    to_id: row.from_id,
    type: inverseOf(type),
    custom_label: type === CUSTOM_TYPE ? inverseLabel : "",
    notes,
  };
  if (mirror) await table().update(mirror.id, data);
  else await table().create({ campaign_id: row.campaign_id, pair_id: row.pair_id, ...data });
}

// RÃ©aligne la ligne miroir sur cette ligne.
export async function repairRelation(row, relations) {
  const mirror = mirrorOf(row, relations);
  await updateRelation(row, relations, {
    toId: row.to_id,
    type: row.type,
    label: row.custom_label || "",
    inverseLabel: mirror?.custom_label || "",
    notes: row.notes || "",
  });
}

export async function deleteRelation(row, relations, both = true) {
  const ids = [row.id];
  if (both) {
    const mirror = mirrorOf(row, relations);
    if (mirror) ids.push(mirror.id);
  }
  await table().deleteMany({ id: { $in: ids } });
}

// Ã€ appeler quand un PNJ est supprimÃ©.
export async function deleteNpcRelations(campaignId, npcId) {
  const all = await loadRelations(campaignId);
  const pairs = new Set(all.filter((r) => r.from_id === npcId || r.to_id === npcId).map((r) => r.pair_id));
  const ids = all.filter((r) => pairs.has(r.pair_id)).map((r) => r.id);
  if (ids.length) await table().deleteMany({ id: { $in: ids } });
}

// Relations de chaque PNJ, triÃ©es (famille d'abord), pour l'affichage sur les cartes.
export function relationsByNpc(relations, npcs) {
  const names = new Map(npcs.map((n) => [n.id, n.name]));
  const map = new Map();
  relations.forEach((r) => {
    if (!names.has(r.from_id) || !names.has(r.to_id)) return;
    if (!map.has(r.from_id)) map.set(r.from_id, []);
    map.get(r.from_id).push({
      id: r.id,
      order: groupIndex(r.type),
      text: `${relationLabel(r)} ${names.get(r.to_id)}`,
    });
  });
  map.forEach((list) =>
    list.sort((a, b) => a.order - b.order || a.text.localeCompare(b.text, "fr"))
  );
  return map;
}

// Reprend sur un nouveau PNJ les relations d'un PNJ existant (cÃ´tÃ© inverse compris).
export async function copyNpcRelations(campaignId, sourceId, newId) {
  const all = await loadRelations(campaignId);
  const mine = all.filter((r) => r.from_id === sourceId && r.to_id !== newId);
  for (const row of mine) {
    const mirror = mirrorOf(row, all);
    await createRelation(campaignId, {
      fromId: newId,
      toId: row.to_id,
      type: row.type,
      label: row.custom_label || "",
      inverseLabel: mirror?.custom_label || "",
      notes: row.notes || "",
      oneSided: !mirror,
    });
  }
}
=== src\lib\openAttachment.js ===
export const openAttachment = (file) => {
  const url = file?.url || "";
  if (!url.startsWith("data:")) {
    window.open(url, "_blank", "noopener,noreferrer");
    return;
  }
  const [head, b64] = url.split(",");
  const mime = head.match(/data:(.*?);base64/)?.[1] || file.type || "application/octet-stream";
  const bytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(b64), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
  const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([bytes], { type: mime }));
  window.open(blobUrl, "_blank");
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl), 60000);
};

import { useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { api } from '@/api/localClient';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';


export default function PageNotFound({}) {
    const location = useLocation();
    const pageName = location.pathname.substring(1);

    const { data: authData, isFetched } = useQuery({
        queryKey: ['user'],
        queryFn: async () => {
            try {
                const user = await api.auth.me();
                return { user, isAuthenticated: true };
            } catch (error) {
                return { user: null, isAuthenticated: false };
            }
        }
    });
    
    return (
        <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center p-6 bg-background">
            <div className="max-w-md w-full">
                <div className="text-center space-y-6">
                    {/* 404 Error Code */}
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                        <h1 className="text-7xl font-light text-muted-foreground/30">404</h1>
                        <div className="h-0.5 w-16 bg-border mx-auto"></div>
                    </div>
                    
                    {/* Main Message */}
                    <div className="space-y-3">
                        <h2 className="text-2xl font-medium text-foreground">
                            Page Not Found
                        </h2>
                        <p className="text-muted-foreground leading-relaxed">
                            The page <span className="font-medium text-foreground">"{pageName}"</span> could not be found in this application.
                        </p>
                    </div>
                    
                    {/* Admin Note */}
                    {isFetched && authData.isAuthenticated && authData.user?.role === 'admin' && (
                        <div className="mt-8 p-4 bg-muted rounded-lg border border-border">
                            <div className="flex items-start space-x-3">
                                <div className="flex-shrink-0 w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-destructive/10 flex items-center justify-center mt-0.5">
                                    <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-destructive"></div>
                                </div>
                                <div className="text-left space-y-1">
                                    <p className="text-sm font-medium text-foreground">Admin Note</p>
                                    <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground leading-relaxed">
                                        This could mean that the AI hasn't implemented this page yet. Ask it to implement it in the chat.
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    )}
                    
                    {/* Action Button */}
                    <div className="pt-6">
                        <button 
                            onClick={() => window.location.href = '/'} 
                            className="inline-flex items-center px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-foreground bg-card border border-border rounded-lg hover:bg-muted/50 hover:border-muted-foreground/40 transition-colors duration-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-ring"
                        >
                            <svg className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M3 12l2-2m0 0l7-7 7 7M5 10v10a1 1 0 001 1h3m10-11l2 2m-2-2v10a1 1 0 01-1 1h-3m-6 0a1 1 0 001-1v-4a1 1 0 011-1h2a1 1 0 011 1v4a1 1 0 001 1m-6 0h6" />
                            </svg>
                            Go Home
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    )
}
=== src\lib\pollDates.js ===
/** @param {string} value @returns {string} */
export function toPollIsoDate(value) {
  const match = /^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{2}|\d{4})$/.exec(String(value || "").trim());
  if (!match) return "";

  const [, dayValue, monthValue, yearValue] = match;
  const year = yearValue.length === 2 ? `20${yearValue}` : yearValue;
  const isoDate = `${year}-${monthValue.padStart(2, "0")}-${dayValue.padStart(2, "0")}`;
  const date = new Date(`${isoDate}T00:00:00.000Z`);
  return !Number.isNaN(date.valueOf()) && date.toISOString().slice(0, 10) === isoDate ? isoDate : "";
}

/** @param {string} value @returns {string} */
export function formatPollDateInput(value) {
  const digits = String(value || "").replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 8);
  if (digits.length <= 2) return digits;
  if (digits.length <= 4) return `${digits.slice(0, 2)}/${digits.slice(2)}`;
  return `${digits.slice(0, 2)}/${digits.slice(2, 4)}/${digits.slice(4)}`;
}

=== src\lib\printModel.js ===
import { childrenMap, flattenTree, pathOf } from "@/lib/locationTree";
import { groupIndex, relationLabel } from "@/lib/relationMeta";
import { NPC_ATTITUDE, SCENE_STATUS } from "@/lib/sceneMeta";
import { sessionStatusMeta } from "@/lib/sessionMeta";

export const SECTIONS = [
    { key: "scenes", title: "ScÃ¨nes et actes", hint: "Chaque scÃ¨ne est imprimÃ©e avec ses indices liÃ©s." },
    { key: "clues", title: "Indices", hint: "Uniquement ceux non imprimÃ©s avec une scÃ¨ne." },
    { key: "locations", title: "Lieux", hint: "Dans l'ordre de l'arborescence." },
    { key: "npcs", title: "PNJ", hint: "ClassÃ©s par lieu, dans l'ordre des scÃ¨nes, avec leurs relations." },
    { key: "notes", title: "Notes du MJ", hint: "Ã‰pinglÃ©es en premier." },
    { key: "tables", title: "Tables alÃ©atoires", hint: "EntrÃ©es et poids de tirage." },
    { key: "sessions", title: "Sessions", hint: "Dates, heures, lieux et notes." },
    { key: "characters", title: "Fiches de personnages", hint: "CaractÃ©ristiques, compÃ©tences, Ã©quipements et notes." },
];

const txt = (v) => String(v ?? "");
const byName = (a, b) => txt(a.name).localeCompare(txt(b.name), "fr", { numeric: true });
const byOrder = (a, b) =>
    (Number(a.order) || 0) - (Number(b.order) || 0) ||
    txt(a.title).localeCompare(txt(b.title), "fr", { numeric: true });

// Actes dans l'ordre, chacun avec ses scÃ¨nes ; les scÃ¨nes sans acte forment un dernier groupe.
export function orderScenes(acts, scenes) {
    const actIds = new Set(acts.map((a) => a.id));
    const groups = [...acts]
        .sort(byOrder)
        .map((act) => ({ act, scenes: scenes.filter((s) => s.act_id === act.id).sort(byOrder) }));
    const orphans = scenes.filter((s) => !s.act_id || !actIds.has(s.act_id)).sort(byOrder);
    if (orphans.length) groups.push({ act: null, scenes: orphans });
    return groups;
}

// ---------- Arbres de sÃ©lection (pour la boÃ®te de dialogue) ----------

const leaf = (id, label, hint = "") => ({ id, label, hint, countable: true, derived: false, children: [] });

export function buildTrees({ acts, scenes, npcs, locations, clues, notes, tables = [], sessions = [], characters = [] }) {
    let n = 0;
    const sceneNodes = orderScenes(acts, scenes).map((g) => {
        const kids = g.scenes.map((s) =>
            leaf(s.id, `${++n}. ${s.title || "Sans titre"}`, SCENE_STATUS[s.status]?.label || "")
        );
        if (!g.act) return { id: "__noact__", label: "Sans acte", hint: "", countable: false, derived: true, children: kids };
        return {
            id: g.act.id,
            label: g.act.title || "Acte",
            hint: "Acte",
            derived: kids.length > 0, // un acte qui contient des scÃ¨nes n'est pas un Ã©lÃ©ment Ã  lui seul
            countable: kids.length === 0,
            children: kids,
        };
    });

    const map = childrenMap(locations);
    const walk = (parentId, seen) =>
        (map.get(parentId) || [])
            .filter((l) => !seen.has(l.id))
            .map((l) => ({
                ...leaf(l.id, l.name || "Lieu", l.type || ""),
                children: walk(l.id, new Set(seen).add(l.id)),
            }));

    const pinnedFirst = (a, b) => Number(!!b.pinned) - Number(!!a.pinned);
    return {
        scenes: sceneNodes,
        clues: [...clues].sort(byName).map((c) => leaf(
            c.id,
            c.name || (c.clue_type === "info" ? "Info" : "Indice"),
            c.clue_type === "info"
                ? `Info Â· ${c.known ? "Connue" : "Inconnue"} Â· ${c.used ? "UtilisÃ©e" : "Non utilisÃ©e"}`
                : c.discovered ? "DÃ©couvert" : "CachÃ©"
        )),
        locations: walk("", new Set()),
        npcs: [...npcs].sort(byName).map((p) => leaf(p.id, p.name || "PNJ", p.role || "")),
        notes: [...notes]
            .sort((a, b) => pinnedFirst(a, b) || txt(b.updated_date).localeCompare(txt(a.updated_date)))
            .map((x) => leaf(x.id, x.title || "Note", x.pinned ? "Ã‰pinglÃ©e" : "")),
        tables: [...tables].sort(byName).map((table) =>
            leaf(table.id, table.name || "Table", `${table.category || "autre"} Â· ${(table.entries || []).length} entrÃ©es`)
        ),
        sessions: [...sessions]
            .sort((a, b) => txt(a.date).localeCompare(txt(b.date)) || txt(a.time).localeCompare(txt(b.time)))
            .map((session) => leaf(session.id, session.title || "Session", `${session.date || ""} ${session.time || ""}`.trim())),
        characters: [...characters].sort(byName).map((character) =>
            leaf(character.id, character.name || "Personnage")
        ),
    };
}

const walkNodes = (nodes, fn) =>
    nodes.forEach((node) => {
        fn(node);
        walkNodes(node.children || [], fn);
    });

export const selectableIds = (nodes) => {
    const out = [];
    walkNodes(nodes, (node) => !node.derived && out.push(node.id));
    return out;
};

export const countableIds = (nodes) => {
    const out = [];
    walkNodes(nodes, (node) => node.countable && out.push(node.id));
    return out;
};

// ---------- ModÃ¨le du document imprimÃ© ----------

export function buildPrintModel(data, sel, opts) {
    const { campaign, acts, scenes, npcs, locations, clues, notes, tables = [], sessions = [], relations, characters = [], gameSheetModel } = data;
    const sceneById = new Map(scenes.map((s) => [s.id, s]));
    const npcById = new Map(npcs.map((p) => [p.id, p]));
    const clueById = new Map(clues.map((c) => [c.id, c]));
    const locById = new Map(locations.map((l) => [l.id, l]));
    const locPath = (id) => pathOf(locations, id).map((l) => l.name).join(" â€º ");
    const images = (entity) =>
        opts.images
            ? (entity.attachments || []).filter((a) => (a.type || "").startsWith("image/")).map((a) => ({ url: a.url, name: a.name }))
            : [];

    const groups = orderScenes(acts, scenes);
    const number = new Map();
    let n = 0;
    groups.forEach((g) => g.scenes.forEach((s) => number.set(s.id, ++n)));

    // PremiÃ¨re apparition de chaque PNJ / indice dans le dÃ©roulÃ©.
    const npcFirst = new Map();
    const clueFirst = new Map();
    const clueScenes = new Map();
    groups.forEach((g) =>
        g.scenes.forEach((s) => {
            (s.npc_ids || []).forEach((id) => !npcFirst.has(id) && npcFirst.set(id, number.get(s.id)));
            (s.clue_ids || []).forEach((id) => {
                if (!clueFirst.has(id)) clueFirst.set(id, number.get(s.id));
                clueScenes.set(id, [...(clueScenes.get(id) || []), `ScÃ¨ne ${number.get(s.id)}`]);
            });
        })
    );

    const toClue = (c) => ({
        name: c.name,
        description: c.description || "",
        clueType: c.clue_type === "info" ? "info" : "indice",
        discovered: !!c.discovered,
        known: !!c.known,
        used: !!c.used,
        images: images(c),
    });

    const blocks = [];
    const printedClues = new Set();

    // 1. ScÃ¨nes et actes
    const sceneIds = sel.scenes || new Set();
    const sceneGroups = [];
    groups.forEach((g) => {
        const picked = g.scenes.filter((s) => sceneIds.has(s.id));
        const emptyActSelected = g.act && g.scenes.length === 0 && sceneIds.has(g.act.id);
        if (!picked.length && !emptyActSelected) return;
        sceneGroups.push({
            actTitle: g.act ? g.act.title || "Acte" : "Sans acte",
            scenes: picked.map((s) => {
                const sceneClues = opts.cluesWithScenes
                    ? (s.clue_ids || []).map((id) => clueById.get(id)).filter(Boolean)
                    : [];
                sceneClues.forEach((c) => printedClues.add(c.id));
                return {
                    number: number.get(s.id),
                    title: s.title || "Sans titre",
                    status: SCENE_STATUS[s.status]?.label || "",
                    summary: s.summary || "",
                    gmNotes: opts.secrets ? s.gm_notes || "" : null,
                    branches: (s.next_scene_ids || [])
                        .map((id) => sceneById.get(id))
                        .filter(Boolean)
                        .map((t) => `ScÃ¨ne ${number.get(t.id)} : ${t.title}`),
                    npcs: (s.npc_ids || [])
                        .map((id) => npcById.get(id))
                        .filter(Boolean)
                        .map((p) => (p.role ? `${p.name} (${p.role})` : p.name)),
                    locations: (s.location_ids || []).filter((id) => locById.has(id)).map(locPath),
                    clues: sceneClues.map(toClue),
                };
            }),
        });
    });
    if (sceneGroups.length) blocks.push({ kind: "scenes", groups: sceneGroups });

    // 2. Indices non dÃ©jÃ  imprimÃ©s avec leur scÃ¨ne
    const clueIds = sel.clues || new Set();
    const standalone = clues
        .filter((c) => clueIds.has(c.id) && !printedClues.has(c.id))
        .sort((a, b) => (clueFirst.get(a.id) ?? Infinity) - (clueFirst.get(b.id) ?? Infinity) || byName(a, b))
        .map((c) => ({ ...toClue(c), scenes: (clueScenes.get(c.id) || []).join(", ") }));
    if (standalone.length) blocks.push({ kind: "clues", clues: standalone });

    // 3. Lieux, dans l'ordre de l'arborescence
    const locIds = sel.locations || new Set();
    const placeItems = flattenTree(locations)
        .filter(({ location }) => locIds.has(location.id))
        .map(({ location }) => ({
            path: locPath(location.id),
            type: location.type || "",
            description: location.description || "",
            images: images(location),
        }));
    if (placeItems.length) blocks.push({ kind: "locations", items: placeItems });

    // 4. PNJ par lieu, dans l'ordre d'apparition dans les scÃ¨nes
    const npcIds = sel.npcs || new Set();
    const first = (p) => npcFirst.get(p.id) ?? Infinity;
    const byLocation = new Map();
    npcs
        .filter((p) => npcIds.has(p.id))
        .forEach((p) => {
            const key = locById.has(p.location_id) ? p.location_id : "";
            byLocation.set(key, [...(byLocation.get(key) || []), p]);
        });
    const npcGroups = [...byLocation]
        .map(([key, list]) => {
            list.sort((a, b) => first(a) - first(b) || byName(a, b));
            return {
                key,
                rank: first(list[0]),
                label: key ? locPath(key) : "Sans lieu",
                npcs: list.map((p) => ({
                    name: p.name,
                    role: p.role || "",
                    attitude: NPC_ATTITUDE[p.attitude]?.label || "",
                    description: p.description || "",
                    images: images(p),
                    relations: relations
                        .filter((r) => r.from_id === p.id && npcById.has(r.to_id))
                        .sort((a, b) => groupIndex(a.type) - groupIndex(b.type))
                        .map((r) => `${relationLabel(r)} ${npcById.get(r.to_id).name}`),
                })),
            };
        })
        .sort(
            (a, b) =>
                Number(a.key === "") - Number(b.key === "") ||
                a.rank - b.rank ||
                a.label.localeCompare(b.label, "fr")
        );
    if (npcGroups.length) blocks.push({ kind: "npcs", groups: npcGroups });

    // 5. Notes du MJ
    const noteIds = sel.notes || new Set();
    const noteItems = notes
        .filter((x) => noteIds.has(x.id))
        .sort((a, b) => Number(!!b.pinned) - Number(!!a.pinned) || txt(b.updated_date).localeCompare(txt(a.updated_date)))
        .map((x) => ({ title: x.title || "Note", content: x.content || "", pinned: !!x.pinned }));
    if (noteItems.length) blocks.push({ kind: "notes", notes: noteItems });

    // 6. Tables alÃ©atoires
    const tableIds = sel.tables || new Set();
    const tableItems = tables.filter((table) => tableIds.has(table.id)).sort(byName).map((table) => ({
        name: table.name || "Table",
        category: table.category || "",
        description: table.description || "",
        entries: (table.entries || []).map((entry) => ({
            text: entry.text || "",
            weight: Number(entry.weight) > 0 ? Number(entry.weight) : 1,
        })),
    }));
    if (tableItems.length) blocks.push({ kind: "tables", tables: tableItems });

    // 7. Sessions
    const sessionIds = sel.sessions || new Set();
    const sessionItems = sessions
        .filter((session) => sessionIds.has(session.id))
        .sort((a, b) => txt(a.date).localeCompare(txt(b.date)) || txt(a.time).localeCompare(txt(b.time)))
        .map((session) => ({
            title: session.title || "Session",
            date: session.date || "",
            time: session.time || "",
            location: session.location || "",
            status: sessionStatusMeta(session.status).label,
            notes: session.notes || "",
        }));
    if (sessionItems.length) blocks.push({ kind: "sessions", sessions: sessionItems });

    // 8. Fiches de personnages
    const characterIds = sel.characters || new Set();
    const selectedCharacters = characters
        .filter((character) => characterIds.has(character.id))
        .sort(byName)
        .map((character) => ({
            character,
            characteristics: gameSheetModel?.characteristics || [],
            skillRollModifier: !!gameSheetModel?.skillRollModifier,
        }));
    if (selectedCharacters.length) {
        blocks.push({ kind: "characters", characters: selectedCharacters });
    }

    return {
        campaignName: campaign?.name || "Campagne",
        dateLabel: new Date().toLocaleDateString("fr-FR", { dateStyle: "long" }),
        blocks,
    };
}

=== src\lib\RefreshContext.jsx ===
import React, { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useMemo, useState } from "react";

// DurÃ©e minimale d'affichage de l'indicateur, le temps que les donnÃ©es reviennent.
const SETTLE_MS = 600;

const RefreshContext = createContext({ refreshKey: 0, refresh: async () => {} });

export function RefreshProvider({ children }) {
  const [refreshKey, setRefreshKey] = useState(0);

  // Relance les chargements de la page affichÃ©e, sans recharger l'application.
  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
    setRefreshKey((key) => key + 1);
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, SETTLE_MS));
  }, []);

  const value = useMemo(() => ({ refreshKey, refresh }), [refreshKey, refresh]);

  return <RefreshContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</RefreshContext.Provider>;
}

export function useDataRefresh() {
  return useContext(RefreshContext);
}

export const RELATION_TYPES = {
  parent_de: { group: "famille", label: "est parent de", inverse: "enfant_de" },
  enfant_de: { group: "famille", label: "est enfant de", inverse: "parent_de" },

  petit_enfant_de: { group: "famille", label: "est petit-enfant de", inverse: "grand_parent_de" },

  famille_eloignee: { group: "famille", label: "est de la famille de", inverse: "famille_eloignee" },

  amoureux: { group: "amour", label: "a une relation amoureuse avec", inverse: "amoureux" },
  conjoint: { group: "amour", label: "est uni par le mariage Ã ", inverse: "conjoint" },
  ancien_amour: { group: "amour", label: "a eu une relation amoureuse avec", inverse: "ancien_amour" },

  allie: { group: "diplomatie", label: "est alliÃ©Â·e de", inverse: "allie" },
  ennemi: { group: "diplomatie", label: "est ennemiÂ·e de", inverse: "ennemi" },
  rival: { group: "diplomatie", label: "est rivalÂ·e de", inverse: "rival" },

  mentor_de: { group: "social", label: "est le mentor de", inverse: "eleve_de" },
  eleve_de: { group: "social", label: "est l'Ã©lÃ¨ve de", inverse: "mentor_de" },
  dirige: { group: "social", label: "dirige", inverse: "sous_ordres_de" },
  sous_ordres_de: { group: "social", label: "est sous les ordres de", inverse: "dirige" },
  protege: { group: "social", label: "protÃ¨ge", inverse: "protege_par" },
  protege_par: { group: "social", label: "est protÃ©gÃ©Â·e par", inverse: "protege" },
};

// Relation libre : l'utilisateur saisit lui-mÃªme les deux libellÃ©s.
export const CUSTOM_TYPE = "custom";

export const inverseOf = (type) => RELATION_TYPES[type]?.inverse || type;

export const groupIndex = (type) =>
  type === CUSTOM_TYPE
    ? RELATION_GROUPS.length
    : RELATION_GROUPS.findIndex((g) => g.key === RELATION_TYPES[type]?.group);

export const groupLabel = (type) =>
  type === CUSTOM_TYPE
    ? "PersonnalisÃ©e"
    : RELATION_GROUPS.find((g) => g.key === RELATION_TYPES[type]?.group)?.label || "";

export const RELATION_OPTIONS = [
  ...Object.entries(RELATION_TYPES).map(([key, t]) => ({
    key,
    label: `${groupLabel(key)} Â· ${t.label}`,
  })),
  { key: CUSTOM_TYPE, label: "PersonnalisÃ©e Â· libellÃ©s libres" },
];

export const relationLabel = (row) =>
  row.type === CUSTOM_TYPE ? row.custom_label || "est liÃ© Ã " : RELATION_TYPES[row.type]?.label || row.type;

// Pour la dÃ©tection de boucles familiales : renvoie [parent, enfant] ou null.
export function parentEdge(row) {
  switch (row.type) {
    case "parent_de":
    case "grand_parent_de":
      return [row.from_id, row.to_id];
    case "enfant_de":
    case "petit_enfant_de":
      return [row.to_id, row.from_id];
    default:
      return null;
  }
}

import { api } from "@/api/localClient";

const SERVICE_URL_KEY = "arcanes_poll_service_url";
const DEFAULT_SERVICE_URL = "https://arcanes-planification.pages.dev";

export function getPollServiceUrl() {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(SERVICE_URL_KEY) || DEFAULT_SERVICE_URL;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Lecture de l'adresse du service de sondage impossible :", error);
    return DEFAULT_SERVICE_URL;
  }
}

export function savePollServiceUrl(value) {
  try {
    if (!value) {
      localStorage.removeItem(SERVICE_URL_KEY);
      return;
    }
    localStorage.setItem(SERVICE_URL_KEY, value);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Enregistrement de l'adresse du service de sondage impossible :", error);
    throw new Error("L'adresse du service n'a pas pu Ãªtre mÃ©morisÃ©e sur cet appareil.");
  }
}

export function normalizePollServiceUrl(value) {
  let url;
  try {
    url = new URL(String(value || "").trim());
  } catch {
    throw new Error("Saisissez l'adresse complÃ¨te du site de sondage Cloudflare Pages.");
  }
  const localHttp = ["localhost", "127.0.0.1"].includes(url.hostname);
  if ((url.protocol !== "https:" && !(localHttp && url.protocol === "http:")) ||
      url.pathname !== "/" || url.search || url.hash) {
    throw new Error("L'adresse doit Ãªtre l'origine HTTPS du site de sondage, sans chemin supplÃ©mentaire.");
  }
  return url.origin;
}

export async function pollRequest(baseUrl, path, options = {}) {
  const response = await fetch(`${normalizePollServiceUrl(baseUrl)}${path}`, {
    ...options,
    headers: {
      Accept: "application/json",
      ...(options.body ? { "Content-Type": "application/json" } : {}),
      ...options.headers,
    },
  });
  let body;
  try {
    body = await response.json();
  } catch {
    throw new Error(`Le service de sondage a renvoyÃ© une rÃ©ponse illisible (${response.status}).`);
  }
  if (!response.ok) {
    const error = new Error(body.error || `Le service de sondage a rÃ©pondu ${response.status}.`);
    error.status = response.status;
    throw error;
  }
  return body;
}

export async function refreshSessionPoll(poll, baseUrl) {
  const results = await pollRequest(baseUrl, `/api/polls/${poll.poll_id}/results`, {
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${poll.owner_token}` },
  });
  const updated = {
    comment: results.comment || "",
    expected_players: results.players,
    slots: results.slots,
    votes: results.votes,
    timezone: results.timezone || poll.timezone,
    state: results.state,
    expires_at: results.expiresAt,
    synced_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  };
  await api.entities.SessionPoll.update(poll.id, updated);
  return { ...poll, ...updated };
}

=== src\lib\tableMeta.js ===
export const TABLE_CATEGORIES = [
  { key: "rencontres", label: "Rencontres", badge: "border-destructive/40 bg-destructive/10 text-destructive" },
  { key: "noms", label: "Noms", badge: "border-primary/50 bg-primary/10 text-primary" },
  { key: "evenements", label: "Ã‰vÃ©nements", badge: "border-played/40 bg-played/10 text-played" },
  { key: "objets", label: "Objets", badge: "border-border bg-secondary text-muted-foreground" },
  { key: "rumeurs", label: "Rumeurs", badge: "border-border bg-secondary text-muted-foreground" },
  { key: "autre", label: "Autre", badge: "border-border bg-secondary text-muted-foreground" },
];

/** @param {string} key */
export const categoryMeta = (key) =>
  TABLE_CATEGORIES.find((c) => c.key === key) || TABLE_CATEGORIES[TABLE_CATEGORIES.length - 1];

/**
 * @param {{ entries?: Array<{ text?: string; weight?: number }> } | null | undefined} table
 * @returns {Array<{ text?: string; weight?: number }>}
 */
export const validEntries = (table) =>
  (table?.entries || []).filter((entry) => entry?.text?.trim());

/** @param {{ weight?: number } | null | undefined} entry */
const weightOf = (entry) => {
  const weight = Number(entry?.weight);
  return weight > 0 ? weight : 1;
};

/**
 * @param {Array<{ text?: string; weight?: number }> | null | undefined} entries
 * @returns {{ text?: string; weight?: number } | null}
 */
export const rollTable = (entries) => {
  const pool = (entries || []).filter((entry) => entry?.text?.trim());
  if (pool.length === 0) return null;
  const total = pool.reduce((sum, entry) => sum + weightOf(entry), 0);
  let cursor = Math.random() * total;
  for (const entry of pool) {
    cursor -= weightOf(entry);
    if (cursor <= 0) return entry;
  }
  return pool[pool.length - 1];
};
=== src\lib\TabStackContext.jsx ===
import React, { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useLocation, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

// Racines de la barre d'onglets mobile : chaque onglet tient sa propre pile.
export const TAB_ROOTS = ["/", "/codex", "/calendrier", "/tables", "/notes", "/jeu"];

const MAX_STACK = 20;

const tabForPath = (pathname) => {
  if (pathname === "/") return "/";
  return (
    TAB_ROOTS.find(
      (root) => root !== "/" && (pathname === root || pathname.startsWith(`${root}/`))
    ) || null
  );
};

const emptyStacks = () => TAB_ROOTS.reduce((acc, root) => ({ ...acc, [root]: [root] }), {});

const TabStackContext = createContext({ stacks: {}, activeTab: null, openTab: null, tabTarget: null });

export function TabStackProvider({ children }) {
  const { pathname } = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [stacks, setStacks] = useState(emptyStacks);

  const activeTab = tabForPath(pathname);

  // Chaque onglet mÃ©morise les chemins qu'on y a visitÃ©s.
  useEffect(() => {
    const tab = tabForPath(pathname);
    if (!tab) return;
    setStacks((prev) => {
      const stack = prev[tab] || [tab];
      if (stack[stack.length - 1] === pathname) return prev;
      return { ...prev, [tab]: [...stack, pathname].slice(-MAX_STACK) };
    });
  }, [pathname]);

  const tabTarget = useCallback(
    (root) => {
      const stack = stacks[root] || [root];
      return stack[stack.length - 1] || root;
    },
    [stacks]
  );

  const openTab = useCallback(
    (root) => {
      if (root === activeTab) {
        // Onglet dÃ©jÃ  actif : on repart de sa racine et on remonte en haut de page.
        setStacks((prev) => ({ ...prev, [root]: [root] }));
        if (pathname !== root) navigate(root);
        window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
        return;
      }
      navigate(tabTarget(root));
    },
    [activeTab, navigate, pathname, tabTarget]
  );

  const value = useMemo(
    () => ({ stacks, activeTab, openTab, tabTarget }),
    [stacks, activeTab, openTab, tabTarget]
  );

  return <TabStackContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</TabStackContext.Provider>;
}

export function useTabStacks() {
  return useContext(TabStackContext);
}

        if (alive) setPolls(items.filter((poll) => poll.state === "open"));
        let failed = 0;

          const pollUrl = poll.service_url || serviceUrl;
          if (!pollUrl) continue;
          try {
            const updated = await refreshSessionPoll(poll, pollUrl);
            if (alive) {
              setPolls((current) => updated.state === "open"
                ? [updated, ...current.filter((item) => item.id !== updated.id)]
                : current.filter((item) => item.id !== updated.id));
            }
          } catch (error) {
            failed += 1;
            console.error(`Actualisation du sondage Â« ${poll.title} Â» impossible :`, error);
          }
        }
        if (alive && failed > 0) {
          toast({
            variant: "destructive",

          <p className="mt-2 max-w-xl text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Planifiez vos prochaines parties par date, gardez une trace de celles dÃ©jÃ  jouÃ©es et
            prÃ©parez la scÃ¨ne de chaque sÃ©ance.

            <MonthGrid
              month={month}

              onSelectSession={openEdit}
              onAddSession={openNew}

          <div className="space-y-6">

            {sessions.length > 0 && (
              <div className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-card/50 p-4">
                <h2 className="font-display text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">
                  Ce mois-ci
                </h2>
                <ul className="mt-3 space-y-2">
                  {sessions.map((session) => {
                    const meta = sessionStatusMeta(session.status);
                    const day = dateKey(session.date);
                    return (
                      <li key={session.id}>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => openEdit(session)}

                  })}

              </div>
            )}

  String(value || "")
    .normalize("NFD")
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
    .toLowerCase();

const count = (n, one, many) => `${n} ${n > 1 ? many : one}`;

  const { toast } = useToast();

  const [items, setItems] = useState([]);

  const [duplicateOf, setDuplicateOf] = useState(null);

  const [deleteMode, setDeleteMode] = useState("promote");
  const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState(() => new Set());
  const [allLocations, setAllLocations] = useState([]);
  const [locationFilter, setLocationFilter] = useState("all");
  const [relations, setRelations] = useState([]);

  const kind = CODEX_TABS.find((t) => t.key === tab) || CODEX_TABS[0];

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    const run = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.entities.Campaign.filter({}, { sort: "-created_date", limit: 100 });
        if (!alive) return;
        setCampaigns(res.items);
        const stored = getSelectedCampaignId();
        const valid = res.items.some((c) => c.id === stored) ? stored : res.items[0]?.id || "";
        setCampaignId(valid);
        setSelectedCampaignId(valid);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Chargement des campagnes impossible :", error);
        if (alive) toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Campagnes indisponibles", description: "Impossible de charger les campagnes locales." });
      }
    };
    run();
    return () => { alive = false; };
  }, [dataRefreshKey, toast]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!campaignId) {
      setItems([]);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }
    let alive = true;
    const run = async () => {
      setLoading(true);
      setItems([]);
      try {
        const res = await api.entities[kind.entity].filter(
          { campaign_id: campaignId },
          { sort: "name", limit: 1000 }
        );
        if (alive) setItems(res.items);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Chargement du codex impossible :", error);
        if (alive) toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Codex indisponible", description: "Impossible de charger les Ã©lÃ©ments de cette campagne." });

        if (alive) setLoading(false);
      }

    run();
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };

    if (!campaignId) {
      setAllLocations([]);
      return;

      alive = false;

      labels.set(l.id, pathOf(allLocations, l.id).map((x) => x.name).join(" â€º "))

  }, [allLocations]);

        if (tab !== "npc" || locationFilter === "all") return true;
        if (locationFilter === "_none") return !item.location_id;

      }),
    [items, q, tab, locationFilter, locationScope]

  );

  const openEdit = (k, e) => {
    setTab(k);

        entry: e,

      }
      setRefreshKey((k) => k + 1);
      toast({ title: "Ã‰lÃ©ment supprimÃ©" });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Suppression impossible :", error);

        title: "Suppression impossible",
        description: "Une erreur est survenue, la liste a Ã©tÃ© rechargÃ©e.",
      });
    }

  const hasChildren = (pendingDelete?.totalCount || 0) > 0;
  const deleteChoices = [
    {
      key: "promote",
      title: "Conserver les sous-lieux",
      text: `Les ${count(pendingDelete?.directCount || 0, "sous-lieu direct", "sous-lieux directs")} remontent d'un niveau.`,

    {

    },

const buildProgress = (list) => {
  const progress = { total: 0 };
  list.forEach((scene) => {
    progress[scene.status] = (progress[scene.status] || 0) + 1;
    progress.total += 1;
  });
  return progress;
};
