  const [clues, setClues] = useState(/** @type {any[]} */([]));
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(/** @type {{ total: number, [key: string]: number }} */({ total: 0 }));
  const { expandedCardId, setExpandedCardId } = useExpandedCardState();
  const activeSceneId = expandedCardId?.startsWith("scene:") ? expandedCardId.slice(6) : null;
  const setActiveSceneId = (next) => setExpandedCardId((current) => {
    const currentId = current?.startsWith("scene:") ? current.slice(6) : null;
    const nextId = typeof next === "function" ? next(currentId) : next;
    return nextId ? `scene:${nextId}` : null;
  });
  const [codexRefresh, setCodexRefresh] = useState(0);

  const [editorOpen, setEditorOpen] = useState(false);
  const [sceneForm, setSceneForm] = useState(EMPTY_SCENE);
  const [editingSceneId, setEditingSceneId] = useState(null);
  const [savingScene, setSavingScene] = useState(false);

  const [campaignOpen, setCampaignOpen] = useState(false);
  const [campaignForm, setCampaignForm] = useState(EMPTY_CAMPAIGN);
  const [savingCampaign, setSavingCampaign] = useState(false);
  const [editingCampaignId, setEditingCampaignId] = useState(null);
  const [pendingCampaignDelete, setPendingCampaignDelete] = useState(null);
  const [deletingCampaign, setDeletingCampaign] = useState(false);

  const [codexOpen, setCodexOpen] = useState(false);
  const [codexKind, setCodexKind] = useState("npc");
  const [codexEntry, setCodexEntry] = useState(null);

  const [pendingDelete, setPendingDelete] = useState(null);

  const campaign = useMemo(
    () => campaigns.find((c) => c.id === campaignId) || null,
    [campaigns, campaignId]
  );
  const activeScene = useMemo(
    () => scenes.find((s) => s.id === activeSceneId) || null,
    [scenes, activeSceneId]
  );

  const loadCampaigns = useCallback(async () => {
    const requestId = ++campaignRequest.current;
    try {
      const res = await api.entities.Campaign.filter({}, { sort: "-created_date", limit: 100 });
      if (requestId !== campaignRequest.current) return;
      setCampaigns(res.items);
      const stored = getSelectedCampaignId();
      const valid = res.items.some((c) => c.id === stored) ? stored : res.items[0]?.id || "";
      setCampaignId(valid);
      setSelectedCampaignId(valid);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Chargement des campagnes impossible :", error);
      toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Campagnes indisponibles", description: "Impossible de charger les campagnes locales." });
    }
  }, [toast]);

  const loadTree = useCallback(async (cid) => {
    const requestId = ++treeRequest.current;
    if (!cid) {
      setActs([]);
      setScenes([]);
      setNpcs([]);
      setLocations([]);
      setClues([]);
      setProgress({ total: 0 });
      return;
    }
    setActs([]);
    setScenes([]);
    setNpcs([]);
    setLocations([]);
    setClues([]);
    setProgress({ total: 0 });
    try {
      const [a, s, n, l, c, agg] = await Promise.all([
        api.entities.Act.filter({ campaign_id: cid }, { sort: "order", limit: 200 }),
        api.entities.Scene.filter({ campaign_id: cid }, { sort: "order", limit: 500 }),
        api.entities.NPC.filter({ campaign_id: cid }, { sort: "name", limit: 300 }),
        api.entities.Location.filter({ campaign_id: cid }, { sort: "name", limit: 300 }),
        api.entities.Clue.filter({ campaign_id: cid }, { sort: "name", limit: 300 }),
        api.entities.Scene.aggregate({ query: { campaign_id: cid }, groupBy: "status" }),
      ]);
      if (requestId !== treeRequest.current) return;
      setActs(a.items);
      setScenes(s.items);
      setNpcs(n.items);
      setLocations(l.items);
      setClues(c.items);
      const next = { total: 0 };
      (agg.rows || []).forEach((row) => {
        next[row.status] = row.count;
        next.total += row.count;
      });
      setProgress(next);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Chargement de l'arborescence impossible :", error);
      if (requestId === treeRequest.current) {
        toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Campagne indisponible", description: "Impossible de charger tout le contenu de cette campagne." });
      }
    }
  }, [toast]);

  const reloadCodex = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!campaignId) return;
    const requestId = ++codexRequest.current;
    try {
      const [n, l, c] = await Promise.all([
        api.entities.NPC.filter({ campaign_id: campaignId }, { sort: "name", limit: 300 }),
        api.entities.Location.filter({ campaign_id: campaignId }, { sort: "name", limit: 300 }),
        api.entities.Clue.filter({ campaign_id: campaignId }, { sort: "name", limit: 300 }),
      ]);
      if (requestId !== codexRequest.current) return;
      setNpcs(n.items);
      setLocations(l.items);
      setClues(c.items);
      setCodexRefresh((k) => k + 1);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Actualisation du codex impossible :", error);
      if (requestId === codexRequest.current) {
        toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Codex non actualisÃ©", description: "Les changements n'ont pas pu Ãªtre rechargÃ©s." });
      }
    }
  }, [campaignId, toast]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadCampaigns();
    return () => { campaignRequest.current += 1; };
  }, [loadCampaigns, dataRefreshKey]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadTree(campaignId);
    return () => { treeRequest.current += 1; };
  }, [campaignId, loadTree, dataRefreshKey]);

  useEffect(() => {
    codexRequest.current += 1;
  }, [campaignId]);

  const selectCampaign = (id) => {
    if (id === "__new__") {
      openNewCampaign(); return;
    }
    setCampaignId(id);
    setSelectedCampaignId(id);
    setActiveSceneId(null);
  };

  const openNewScene = useCallback(
    (actId) => {
      const inAct = scenes.filter((s) => (s.act_id || "") === (actId || ""));
      const maxOrder = inAct.reduce((max, s) => Math.max(max, s.order ?? 0), 0);
      setEditingSceneId(null);
      setSceneForm({ ...EMPTY_SCENE, act_id: actId || acts[0]?.id || "", order: maxOrder + 1 });
      setEditorOpen(true);
    },
    [scenes, acts]
  );

  const openEditScene = (scene) => {
    setEditingSceneId(scene.id);
    setSceneForm({ ...EMPTY_SCENE, ...scene });
    setEditorOpen(true);
  };

  const setSceneField = (key, value) => setSceneForm((f) => ({ ...f, [key]: value }));

  const toggleSceneId = (key, id) =>
    setSceneForm((f) => {
      const list = f[key] || [];
      return {
        ...f,
        [key]: list.includes(id) ? list.filter((x) => x !== id) : [...list, id],
      };
    });

  const saveScene = async () => {
    if (!sceneForm.title.trim() || !campaignId) return;
    setSavingScene(true);
    const payload = {
      campaign_id: campaignId,
      act_id: sceneForm.act_id || "",
      title: sceneForm.title.trim(),
      summary: sceneForm.summary || "",
      gm_notes: sceneForm.gm_notes || "",
      status: sceneForm.status || "brouillon",
      order: Number(sceneForm.order) || 0,
      next_scene_ids: sceneForm.next_scene_ids || [],
      npc_ids: sceneForm.npc_ids || [],
      location_ids: sceneForm.location_ids || [],
      clue_ids: sceneForm.clue_ids || [],
    };
    const previous = scenes;
    const previousActiveId = activeSceneId;
    const optimistic = { ...payload, id: editingSceneId || `scene-${Date.now()}` };
    setScenes((prev) => {
      const next = [...prev.filter((s) => s.id !== optimistic.id), optimistic];
      setProgress(buildProgress(next));
      return next;
    });
    setEditorOpen(false);
    if (!editingSceneId) setActiveSceneId(optimistic.id);
    try {
      if (editingSceneId) {
        await api.entities.Scene.update(editingSceneId, payload);
      } else {
        const created = await api.entities.Scene.create(payload);
        if (created?.id) {
          setScenes((prev) => prev.map((s) => (s.id === optimistic.id ? created : s)));
          setActiveSceneId(created.id);
        }
      }
      toast({ title: editingSceneId ? "ScÃ¨ne mise Ã  jour" : "ScÃ¨ne crÃ©Ã©e" });
    } catch {
      setScenes(previous);
      setProgress(buildProgress(previous));
      setActiveSceneId(previousActiveId);
      toast({
        variant: "destructive",
        title: "Enregistrement impossible",
        description: "La scÃ¨ne n'a pas Ã©tÃ© enregistrÃ©e.",
      });
    } finally {
      setSavingScene(false);
    }
  };

  const cycleStatus = async (scene) => {
    const status = nextStatus(scene.status);
    const previous = scenes;
    setScenes((prev) => {
      const next = prev.map((s) => (s.id === scene.id ? { ...s, status } : s));
      setProgress(buildProgress(next));
      return next;
    });
    try {
      await api.entities.Scene.update(scene.id, { status });
    } catch {
      setScenes(previous);
      setProgress(buildProgress(previous));
      toast({
        variant: "destructive",
        title: "Statut non enregistrÃ©",
        description: "La scÃ¨ne a repris son statut prÃ©cÃ©dent.",
      });
    }
  };

  const addAct = async () => {
    if (!campaignId) return;
    const order = acts.reduce((max, a) => Math.max(max, a.order ?? 0), 0) + 1;
    const previous = acts;
    const title = `Acte ${acts.length + 1}`;
    const optimistic = { id: `act-${Date.now()}`, campaign_id: campaignId, title, order };
    setActs((prev) => [...prev, optimistic]);
    try {
      const created = await api.entities.Act.create({ campaign_id: campaignId, title, order });
      setActs((prev) => prev.map((a) => (a.id === optimistic.id ? created : a)));
      toast({ title: "Acte ajoutÃ©", description: "Cliquez sur son titre pour le renommer." });
    } catch {
      setActs(previous);
      toast({
        variant: "destructive",
        title: "Acte non ajoutÃ©",
        description: "RÃ©essayez dans un instant.",
      });
    }
  };

  const renameAct = async (id, title) => {
    const previous = acts;
    setActs((prev) => prev.map((a) => (a.id === id ? { ...a, title } : a)));
    try {
      await api.entities.Act.update(id, { title });
    } catch {
      setActs(previous);
      toast({
        variant: "destructive",
        title: "Renommage impossible",
        description: "Le titre prÃ©cÃ©dent a Ã©tÃ© rÃ©tabli.",
      });
    }
  };

  const confirmDelete = async () => {
    const pending = pendingDelete;
    setPendingDelete(null);
    if (!pending) return;
    const previous = { acts, scenes, activeSceneId };
    if (pending.type === "scene") {
      const remaining = scenes.filter((s) => s.id !== pending.item.id);
      setScenes(remaining);
      setProgress(buildProgress(remaining));
      if (activeSceneId === pending.item.id) setActiveSceneId(null);
      try {
        await api.entities.Scene.delete(pending.item.id);
        toast({ title: "ScÃ¨ne supprimÃ©e" });
      } catch {
        setScenes(previous.scenes);
        setProgress(buildProgress(previous.scenes));
        setActiveSceneId(previous.activeSceneId);
        toast({
          variant: "destructive",
          title: "Suppression impossible",
          description: "La scÃ¨ne a Ã©tÃ© rÃ©tablie.",
        });
      }
      return;
    }
    const remaining = scenes.filter((s) => s.act_id !== pending.item.id);
    setActs((prev) => prev.filter((a) => a.id !== pending.item.id));
    setScenes(remaining);
    setProgress(buildProgress(remaining));
    try {
      await api.entities.Scene.deleteMany({ act_id: pending.item.id });
      await api.entities.Act.delete(pending.item.id);
      toast({ title: "Acte supprimÃ©" });
    } catch {
      setActs(previous.acts);
      setScenes(previous.scenes);
      setProgress(buildProgress(previous.scenes));
      toast({
        variant: "destructive",
        title: "Suppression impossible",
        description: "L'acte a Ã©tÃ© rÃ©tabli.",
      });
    }
  };

  const openNewCampaign = () => {
    setEditingCampaignId(null);
    setCampaignForm(EMPTY_CAMPAIGN);
    setCampaignOpen(true);
  };

  const openEditCampaign = () => {
    if (!campaign) return;
    setEditingCampaignId(campaign.id);
    setCampaignForm({
      name: campaign.name || "",
      system: campaign.system || "",
      summary: campaign.summary || "",
    });
    setCampaignOpen(true);
  };

  const requestDeleteCampaign = async () => {
    if (!campaign) return;
    const target = campaign;
    setCampaignOpen(false);
    setPendingCampaignDelete({ campaign: target, counts: null, countError: false });
    try {
      const counts = await countCampaignContent(target.id);
      setPendingCampaignDelete((p) => (p ? { ...p, counts } : p));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Comptage impossible :", error);
      setPendingCampaignDelete((p) => (p ? { ...p, counts: [], countError: true } : p));
    }
  };

  const confirmCampaignDelete = async () => {
    const target = pendingCampaignDelete?.campaign;
    if (!target) return;
    setDeletingCampaign(true);
    try {
      await deleteCampaignCascade(target.id);
      const remaining = campaigns.filter((c) => c.id !== target.id);
      const nextId = remaining[0]?.id || "";
      setCampaigns(remaining);
      setCampaignId(nextId);
      setSelectedCampaignId(nextId);
      setActiveSceneId(null);
      setPendingCampaignDelete(null);
      toast({ title: "Campagne supprimÃ©e", description: `Â« ${target.name} Â» et son contenu ont Ã©tÃ© supprimÃ©s.`, duration: 4000, });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Ã‰chec suppression campagne :", error);
      loadTree(target.id);
      toast({
        variant: "destructive",
        title: "Suppression incomplÃ¨te",
        description: "Une partie du contenu a pu Ãªtre supprimÃ©e. Vous pouvez relancer la suppression.",
      });
    } finally {
      setDeletingCampaign(false);
    }
  };

  const saveCampaign = async () => {
    if (!campaignForm.name.trim()) return;
    setSavingCampaign(true);
    const payload = { ...campaignForm, name: campaignForm.name.trim() };

    if (editingCampaignId) {
      try {
        const updated = await api.entities.Campaign.update(editingCampaignId, payload);
        setCampaigns((prev) =>
          prev.map((c) => (c.id === editingCampaignId ? { ...c, ...payload, ...(updated || {}) } : c))
        );
        setCampaignOpen(false);
        setEditingCampaignId(null);
        toast({ title: "Campagne mise Ã  jour" });
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Ã‰chec modification campagne :", error);
        toast({
          variant: "destructive",
          title: "Modification impossible",
          description: "La campagne n'a pas Ã©tÃ© enregistrÃ©e.",
        });
      } finally {
        setSavingCampaign(false);
      }
      return;
    }

    const previous = campaigns;
    const optimistic = { ...payload, id: `campaign-${Date.now()}` };
    setCampaigns((prev) => [optimistic, ...prev]);
    setCampaignOpen(false);
    setCampaignForm(EMPTY_CAMPAIGN);
    try {
      const created = await api.entities.Campaign.create(payload);
      setCampaigns((prev) => prev.map((c) => (c.id === optimistic.id ? created : c)));
      setCampaignId(created.id);
      setSelectedCampaignId(created.id);
      setActiveSceneId(null);
      toast({ title: "Campagne crÃ©Ã©e" });
    } catch {
      setCampaigns(previous);
      toast({
        variant: "destructive",
        title: "CrÃ©ation impossible",
        description: "La campagne n'a pas Ã©tÃ© enregistrÃ©e.",
      });
    } finally {
      setSavingCampaign(false);
    }
  };

  const toggleSceneLink = async (kind, entryId) => {
    if (!activeScene) {
      toast({
        title: "SÃ©lectionnez une scÃ¨ne",
        description: "Choisissez une scÃ¨ne dans l'arborescence pour y rattacher cet Ã©lÃ©ment.",
      });
      return;
    }
    const key = { npc: "npc_ids", location: "location_ids", clue: "clue_ids" }[kind];
    const list = activeScene[key] || [];
    const next = list.includes(entryId) ? list.filter((x) => x !== entryId) : [...list, entryId];
    const previous = scenes;
    setScenes((prev) => prev.map((s) => (s.id === activeScene.id ? { ...s, [key]: next } : s)));
    try {
      await api.entities.Scene.update(activeScene.id, { [key]: next });
    } catch {
      setScenes(previous);
      toast({
        variant: "destructive",
        title: "Lien non enregistrÃ©",
        description: "RÃ©essayez dans un instant.",
      });
    }
  };

  const toggleClueFlag = async (entry, field, value) => {
    const previous = clues;
    setClues((current) => current.map((clue) =>
      clue.id === entry.id ? { ...clue, [field]: value } : clue
    ));
    try {
      await api.entities.Clue.update(entry.id, { [field]: value });
      setCodexRefresh((key) => key + 1);
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Ã‰chec mise Ã  jour de lâ€™indice ou de lâ€™info :", error);
      setClues(previous);
      toast({
        variant: "destructive",
        title: "Statut non enregistrÃ©",
        description: "La fiche a repris son statut prÃ©cÃ©dent.",
      });
      return false;
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e) => {
      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === "n") {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (campaignId) openNewScene(null);
        else toast({ title: "CrÃ©ez d'abord une campagne" });
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [campaignId, openNewScene, toast]);

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1600px] space-y-6 px-4 py-6 md:px-8 md:py-8">
      <HeroArrival
        campaign={campaign}
        campaigns={campaigns}
        onSelectCampaign={selectCampaign}
        progress={progress}
        onCreateScene={() => (campaignId ? openNewScene(null) : setCampaignOpen(true))}
        onCreateNpc={() => {
          setCodexKind("npc");
          setCodexEntry(null);
          setCodexOpen(true);
        }}
        onCreateLocation={() => {
          setCodexKind("location");
          setCodexEntry(null);
          setCodexOpen(true);
        }}
        onCreateCampaign={() => {
          setCampaignForm(EMPTY_CAMPAIGN);
          setCampaignOpen(true);
        }}
      />

      <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.9fr)_minmax(0,1fr)]">
        <section>
          <header className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-foreground">Arborescence &amp; ScÃ¨nes</h2>
            {campaign && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <span className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground">
                  {acts.length} acte{acts.length > 1 ? "s" : ""} Â· {scenes.length} scÃ¨ne
                  {scenes.length > 1 ? "s" : ""}
                </span>
                <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={openEditCampaign}>
                  <Pencil className="mr-2 h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Modifier la campagne
                </Button>
                <PrintButton campaignId={campaignId} preset="scenes" size="sm" />
              </div>
            )}
          </header>

          {!campaignId ? (
            <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border p-10 text-center">
              <h3 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-foreground">
                Aucune campagne pour l'instant
              </h3>
              <p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-sm text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                CrÃ©ez votre premiÃ¨re campagne pour commencer Ã  dessiner ses actes et ses scÃ¨nes.
              </p>
              <Button
                className="mt-5"
                onClick={() => {
                  setCampaignForm(EMPTY_CAMPAIGN);
                  setCampaignOpen(true);
                }}
              >
                <Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> CrÃ©er une campagne
              </Button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div className="hidden md:block">
                <SceneBoard
                  acts={acts}
                  scenes={scenes}
                  npcs={npcs}
                  locations={locations}
                  clues={clues}
                  activeSceneId={activeSceneId}
                  onSelectScene={(scene) => setActiveSceneId((id) => id === scene.id ? null : scene.id)}
                  onOpenScene={openEditScene}
                  onCycleStatus={cycleStatus}
                  onAddAct={addAct}
                  onRenameAct={renameAct}
                  onDeleteAct={(act) => setPendingDelete({ type: "act", item: act })}
                  onAddScene={openNewScene}
                  onDeleteScene={(scene) => setPendingDelete({ type: "scene", item: scene })}
                />
              </div>
              <div className="md:hidden">
                <MobileSceneList
                  acts={acts}
                  scenes={scenes}
                  npcs={npcs}
                  locations={locations}
                  clues={clues}
                  activeSceneId={activeSceneId}
                  onSelectScene={(scene) => setActiveSceneId((id) => id === scene.id ? null : scene.id)}
                  onOpenScene={openEditScene}
                  onCycleStatus={cycleStatus}
                  onAddScene={openNewScene}
                  onAddAct={addAct}
                />
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </section>

        <CodexSidebar
          campaignId={campaignId}
          activeScene={activeScene}
          refreshKey={codexRefresh}
          onToggleLink={toggleSceneLink}
          onToggleClueFlag={toggleClueFlag}
          onAdd={(kind) => {
            setCodexKind(kind);
            setCodexEntry(null);
            setCodexOpen(true);
          }}
          onEdit={(kind, entry) => {
            setCodexKind(kind);
            setCodexEntry(entry);
            setCodexOpen(true);
          }}
        />
      </div>

      <SceneEditor
        open={editorOpen}
        onOpenChange={setEditorOpen}
        form={sceneForm}
        setField={setSceneField}
        toggleId={toggleSceneId}
        acts={acts}
        scenes={scenes}
        npcs={npcs}
        locations={locations}
        clues={clues}
        editingId={editingSceneId}
        saving={savingScene}
        onSave={saveScene}
      />

      <CampaignDialog
        open={campaignOpen}
        onOpenChange={setCampaignOpen}
        form={campaignForm}
        setField={(key, value) => setCampaignForm((f) => ({ ...f, [key]: value }))}
        saving={savingCampaign}
        onSave={saveCampaign}
        editing={!!editingCampaignId}
        onDelete={requestDeleteCampaign}
      />

      <CodexEntryDialog
        open={codexOpen}
        onOpenChange={setCodexOpen}
        kind={codexKind}
        campaignId={campaignId}
        entry={codexEntry}
        onSaved={reloadCodex}
      />

      <AlertDialog open={!!pendingDelete} onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setPendingDelete(null)}>
        <AlertDialogContent>
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle>Confirmer la suppression</AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription>
              {pendingDelete?.type === "act"
                ? `L'acte Â« ${pendingDelete?.item?.title} Â» et toutes ses scÃ¨nes seront dÃ©finitivement supprimÃ©s.`
                : `La scÃ¨ne Â« ${pendingDelete?.item?.title} Â» sera dÃ©finitivement supprimÃ©e.`}
            </AlertDialogDescription>
          </AlertDialogHeader>
          <AlertDialogFooter>
            <AlertDialogCancel>Annuler</AlertDialogCancel>
            <AlertDialogAction
              onClick={confirmDelete}
              className="bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground hover:bg-destructive/90"
            >
              Supprimer
            </AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>

      <AlertDialog
        open={!!pendingCampaignDelete}
        onOpenChange={(isOpen) => {
          if (!isOpen && !deletingCampaign) setPendingCampaignDelete(null);
        }}
      >
        <AlertDialogContent>
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle>Supprimer la campagne Â« {pendingCampaignDelete?.campaign?.name} Â» ?</AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription asChild>

                    <ul className="list-disc pl-5">
                      {(pendingCampaignDelete?.counts ?? []).map((c) => (

                    </ul>

        >
          <Plus className="h-6 w-6" />
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );

import { createPortal } from "react-dom";

import { api } from "@/api/localClient";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import PrintSheet from "@/components/print/PrintSheet";
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogCancel,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";
import GameSheetModelEditor from "@/components/game/GameSheetModelEditor";
import GameCharacterEditor from "@/components/game/GameCharacterEditor";
import { getSelectedCampaignId, setSelectedCampaignId } from "@/lib/campaignStore";

import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/use-toast";
import CampaignSelect from "@/components/common/CampaignSelect";
import PrintButton from "@/components/print/PrintButton";

const EMPTY_MODEL = { characteristics: [], skillRollModifier: false };
const normalize = (value) => String(value || "").trim().toLocaleLowerCase("fr");

export default function Jeu() {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const { refreshKey } = useDataRefresh();
  const { expandedCardId, setExpandedCardId } = useExpandedCardState();
  const [campaigns, setCampaigns] = useState([]);
  const [campaignId, setCampaignId] = useState(getSelectedCampaignId());
  const [model, setModel] = useState(EMPTY_MODEL);
  const [modelId, setModelId] = useState("");
  const [characters, setCharacters] = useState([]);
  const [skillCatalog, setSkillCatalog] = useState([]);
  const [equipmentCatalog, setEquipmentCatalog] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [savingModel, setSavingModel] = useState(false);
  const [savingCharacter, setSavingCharacter] = useState(false);
  const [modelOpen, setModelOpen] = useState(false);
  const [characterOpen, setCharacterOpen] = useState(false);
  const [characterDraft, setCharacterDraft] = useState(null);
  const [characterToPrint, setCharacterToPrint] = useState(null);
  const [pendingDelete, setPendingDelete] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!characterToPrint) return;
    let cancelled = false;
    const print = async () => {
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 80));
      if (!cancelled) window.print();
    };
    print();
    const done = () => setCharacterToPrint(null);
    window.addEventListener("afterprint", done);
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      window.removeEventListener("afterprint", done);
    };
  }, [characterToPrint]);

  const loadCampaigns = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const result = await api.entities.Campaign.filter({}, { sort: "-created_date", limit: 100 });
      setCampaigns(result.items);
      const stored = getSelectedCampaignId();
      const valid = result.items.some((item) => item.id === stored) ? stored : result.items[0]?.id || "";
      setCampaignId(valid);
      setSelectedCampaignId(valid);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Chargement des campagnes impossible :", error);
      toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Campagnes indisponibles", description: "Impossible de charger les campagnes locales." });
    }
  }, [toast]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadCampaigns();
  }, [loadCampaigns, refreshKey]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!campaignId) {
      setModel(EMPTY_MODEL);
      setModelId("");
      setCharacters([]);
      setSkillCatalog([]);
      setEquipmentCatalog([]);
      return;
    }
    let alive = true;
    const load = async () => {
      setLoading(true);
      try {
        const [models, people, skills, equipment] = await Promise.all([
          api.entities.GameSheetModel.filter({ campaign_id: campaignId }, { limit: 10 }),
          api.entities.GameCharacter.filter({ campaign_id: campaignId }, { sort: "name", limit: 1000 }),
          api.entities.SkillCatalog.filter({ campaign_id: campaignId }, { sort: "name", limit: 1000 }),
          api.entities.EquipmentCatalog.filter({ campaign_id: campaignId }, { sort: "name", limit: 1000 }),
        ]);
        if (!alive) return;
        const savedModel = models.items[0] || null;
        setModelId(savedModel?.id || "");
        setModel({
          characteristics: savedModel?.characteristics || [],
          skillRollModifier: !!savedModel?.skillRollModifier,
        });
        setCharacters(people.items);
        setSkillCatalog(skills.items);
        setEquipmentCatalog(equipment.items);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Chargement des fiches de jeu impossible :", error);
        if (alive) toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Fiches indisponibles", description: "Impossible de charger les donnÃ©es de jeu de cette campagne." });
      } finally {
        if (alive) setLoading(false);
      }
    };
    load();
    return () => { alive = false; };
  }, [campaignId, refreshKey, toast]);

  const saveModel = async (nextModel) => {
    if (!campaignId) return;
    setSavingModel(true);
    try {
      let saved;
      if (modelId) {
        saved = await api.entities.GameSheetModel.update(modelId, {
          campaign_id: campaignId,
          ...nextModel,
        });
      } else {
        saved = await api.entities.GameSheetModel.create({
          campaign_id: campaignId,
          ...nextModel,
        });
        setModelId(saved.id);
      }

      let valuesMigrated = true;
      if (modelId) {
        const updatedNames = new Map();
        model.characteristics.forEach((previous, index) => {
          const current = nextModel.characteristics[index];
          if (current && previous.name !== current.name) updatedNames.set(previous.name, current.name);
        });
        if (updatedNames.size > 0) {
          try {
            await Promise.all(characters.map((character) => {
              const characteristics = { ...(character.characteristics || {}) };
              updatedNames.forEach((newName, previousName) => {
                if (characteristics[previousName] && !characteristics[newName]) {
                  characteristics[newName] = characteristics[previousName];
                }
                delete characteristics[previousName];
              });
              return api.entities.GameCharacter.update(character.id, { characteristics });
            }));
            setCharacters((current) => current.map((character) => {
              const characteristics = { ...(character.characteristics || {}) };
              updatedNames.forEach((newName, previousName) => {
                if (characteristics[previousName] && !characteristics[newName]) characteristics[newName] = characteristics[previousName];
                delete characteristics[previousName];
              });
              return { ...character, characteristics };
            }));
          } catch (error) {
            valuesMigrated = false;
            console.error("Migration des valeurs de caractÃ©ristiques impossible :", error);
          }
        }
      }
      setModel({ characteristics: saved.characteristics || [], skillRollModifier: !!saved.skillRollModifier });
      setModelOpen(false);
      toast(valuesMigrated
        ? { title: "ModÃ¨le enregistrÃ©" }
        : { variant: "destructive", title: "ModÃ¨le enregistrÃ©, migration incomplÃ¨te", description: "Certaines valeurs de caractÃ©ristiques des personnages nâ€™ont pas pu Ãªtre renommÃ©es." });
      return true;
    } catch (error) {

      return false;
    } finally {
      setSavingModel(false);
    }
  };

  const openNewCharacter = () => {
    setCharacterDraft(null);
    setCharacterOpen(true);
  };

  const openDuplicateCharacter = (character) => {
    setCharacterDraft({
      ...structuredClone(character),
      id: undefined,
      name: `${character.name} (copie)`,
    });
    setCharacterOpen(true);
  };

  const saveCatalogItem = async (entity, existing, payload) => {
    const matched = existing.find((item) => normalize(item.name) === normalize(payload.name));
    if (matched) return api.entities[entity].update(matched.id, payload);
    return api.entities[entity].create({ campaign_id: campaignId, ...payload });
  };

  const saveCharacter = async (draft) => {
    if (!campaignId) return false;
    setSavingCharacter(true);
    const payload = {
      campaign_id: campaignId,
      name: draft.name,
      notes: draft.notes || "",
      characteristics: draft.characteristics || {},
      skills: draft.skills || [],
      equipment: draft.equipment || [],
    };
    try {
      const saved = draft.id
        ? await api.entities.GameCharacter.update(draft.id, payload)
        : await api.entities.GameCharacter.create(payload);
      setCharacters((current) => [
        ...current.filter((item) => item.id !== saved.id),
        saved,
      ].sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, "fr", { numeric: true })));
      setCharacterOpen(false);
      setCharacterDraft(null);

      try {
        const nextSkills = [...skillCatalog];
        const nextEquipment = [...equipmentCatalog];
        for (const skill of payload.skills) {
          if (skill.name) {

              name: skill.name,

            });
            const existingIndex = nextSkills.findIndex((item) => item.id === catalogItem.id);
            if (existingIndex >= 0) nextSkills[existingIndex] = catalogItem;
            else nextSkills.push(catalogItem);
          }
        }
        for (const item of payload.equipment) {
          if (!item.name) continue;

              .flatMap((known) => known.field_names || (known.fields || []).map((field) => field.name)),
            ...item.fields.map((field) => field.name),
          ].filter(Boolean))];

          });

        });
      }

      toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Fiche non enregistrÃ©e", description: "La fiche personnage nâ€™a pas Ã©tÃ© enregistrÃ©e." });
      return false;

    }

    const character = pendingDelete;
    setPendingDelete(null);

      await api.entities.GameCharacter.delete(character.id);
      setCharacters((current) => current.filter((item) => item.id !== character.id));
      toast({ title: "Personnage supprimÃ©" });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Suppression du personnage impossible :", error);
      toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Suppression impossible", description: "Le personnage nâ€™a pas Ã©tÃ© supprimÃ©." });
    }
  };

          <span className="tag border border-primary/40 text-primary">Personnages</span>
          <h1 className="mt-3 font-display text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">SystÃ¨me de jeu</h1>

        <PrintSheet
          model={{
            character: characterToPrint,
            campaignName: campaign?.name || "",
            characteristics: model.characteristics,
            skillRollModifier: model.skillRollModifier,
          }}

      )}

import { api } from "@/api/localClient";
import { Pin, Plus, StickyNote } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

import NoteEditor from "@/components/notes/NoteEditor";
import { getSelectedCampaignId, setSelectedCampaignId } from "@/lib/campaignStore";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/use-toast";
import { useDataRefresh } from "@/lib/RefreshContext";
import PrintButton from "@/components/print/PrintButton";
import CampaignSelect from "@/components/common/CampaignSelect";

const EMPTY = { title: "", content: "", pinned: false };

export default function Notes() {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const { refreshKey: dataRefreshKey } = useDataRefresh();
  const [campaigns, setCampaigns] = useState([]);
  const [campaignId, setCampaignId] = useState(getSelectedCampaignId());
  const [pinnedNotes, setPinnedNotes] = useState([]);
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [pendingDelete, setPendingDelete] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    const run = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.entities.Campaign.filter({}, { sort: "-created_date", limit: 100 });
        if (!alive) return;
        setCampaigns(res.items);
        const stored = getSelectedCampaignId();
        const valid = res.items.some((c) => c.id === stored) ? stored : res.items[0]?.id || "";
        setCampaignId(valid);
        setSelectedCampaignId(valid);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Chargement des campagnes impossible :", error);
        if (alive) toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Campagnes indisponibles", description: "Impossible de charger les campagnes locales." });
      }
    };
    run();
    return () => { alive = false; };
  }, [dataRefreshKey, toast]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!campaignId) {
      setPinnedNotes([]);
      setNotes([]);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }
    let alive = true;
    setPinnedNotes([]);
    setNotes([]);
    const run = async () => {
      setLoading(true);
      try {
        const [pinnedRes, restRes] = await Promise.all([
          api.entities.Note.filter(
            { campaign_id: campaignId, pinned: true },
            { sort: "-updated_date", limit: 50 }
          ),
          api.entities.Note.filter(
            { campaign_id: campaignId, pinned: false },
            { sort: "-updated_date", limit: 100 }
          ),
        ]);
        if (!alive) return;
        setPinnedNotes(pinnedRes.items);
        setNotes(restRes.items);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Chargement des notes impossible :", error);
        if (alive) toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Notes indisponibles", description: "Impossible de charger les notes de cette campagne." });
      } finally {
        if (alive) setLoading(false);
      }
    };
    run();
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [campaignId, dataRefreshKey, toast]);

  const openNew = () => {
    setForm(EMPTY);
    setOpen(true);
  };

  const openEdit = (note) => {
    setForm({
      id: note.id,
      title: note.title || "",
      content: note.content || "",
      pinned: !!note.pinned,
    });
    setOpen(true);
  };

  const applyNote = (note, replacedId) => {
    setPinnedNotes((list) => {
      const rest = list.filter((n) => n.id !== note.id && n.id !== replacedId);
      return note.pinned ? [note, ...rest] : rest;
    });
    setNotes((list) => {
      const rest = list.filter((n) => n.id !== note.id && n.id !== replacedId);
      return note.pinned ? rest : [note, ...rest];
    });
  };

  const save = async () => {
    if (!form.title.trim() || !campaignId) return;
    const payload = {
      campaign_id: campaignId,
      title: form.title.trim(),
      content: form.content || "",
      pinned: !!form.pinned,
    };
    const optimistic = {
      ...payload,
      id: form.id || `note-${Date.now()}`,
      updated_date: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
    const previous = { pinnedNotes, notes };
    setSaving(true);
    applyNote(optimistic);
    setOpen(false);
    setForm(EMPTY);
    try {
      const saved = form.id
        ? await api.entities.Note.update(form.id, payload)
        : await api.entities.Note.create(payload);
      if (saved?.id) applyNote({ ...optimistic, ...saved }, optimistic.id);
      toast({ title: form.id ? "Note mise Ã  jour" : "Note crÃ©Ã©e" });
    } catch {
      setPinnedNotes(previous.pinnedNotes);
      setNotes(previous.notes);
      toast({
        variant: "destructive",
        title: "Enregistrement impossible",
        description: "La note n'a pas Ã©tÃ© enregistrÃ©e.",
      });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const togglePin = async (note) => {
    const previous = { pinnedNotes, notes };
    const pinned = !note.pinned;
    applyNote({ ...note, pinned });
    try {
      await api.entities.Note.update(note.id, { pinned });
      toast({ title: pinned ? "Note Ã©pinglÃ©e" : "Note dÃ©sÃ©pinglÃ©e" });
    } catch {
      setPinnedNotes(previous.pinnedNotes);
      setNotes(previous.notes);
      toast({
        variant: "destructive",
        title: "Ã‰pingle non modifiÃ©e",
        description: "La note a repris sa place prÃ©cÃ©dente.",
      });
    }
  };

  const removeNote = async () => {
    const pending = pendingDelete;
    setPendingDelete(null);
    if (!pending) return;
    const previous = { pinnedNotes, notes };
    setPinnedNotes((list) => list.filter((n) => n.id !== pending.id));
    setNotes((list) => list.filter((n) => n.id !== pending.id));
    try {
      await api.entities.Note.delete(pending.id);
      toast({ title: "Note supprimÃ©e" });
    } catch {
      setPinnedNotes(previous.pinnedNotes);
      setNotes(previous.notes);
      toast({
        variant: "destructive",
        title: "Suppression impossible",
        description: "La note a Ã©tÃ© rÃ©tablie.",
      });
    }
  };

  const isEmpty = pinnedNotes.length === 0 && notes.length === 0;

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-6xl space-y-6 px-4 py-6 md:px-8 md:py-8">
      <header className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <span className="tag border border-primary/40 text-primary">Bloc-notes</span>
          <h1 className="mt-3 font-display text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">Notes du MJ</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 max-w-xl text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Consignez les idÃ©es, les dÃ©cisions des joueurs et les fils laissÃ©s en suspens, pour ne
            rien perdre d'une sÃ©ance Ã  l'autre.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex w-full items-center gap-2 sm:w-auto">
          <CampaignSelect
            campaigns={campaigns}
            value={campaignId}
            onValueChange={(id) => { setCampaignId(id); setSelectedCampaignId(id); }}
          />
          <PrintButton campaignId={campaignId} preset="notes" />
          <Button onClick={openNew} disabled={!campaignId}>
            <Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Nouvelle note
          </Button>
        </div>
      </header>

      {!campaignId ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border p-10 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          CrÃ©ez une campagne depuis l'arborescence pour commencer votre bloc-notes.
        </div>
      ) : loading ? (
        <p className="py-10 text-center font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground">
          Chargementâ€¦
        </p>
      ) : isEmpty ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border p-10 text-center">
          <StickyNote className="mx-auto h-7 w-7 text-primary" />
          <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Aucune note pour le moment.</p>
          <Button className="mt-5" onClick={openNew}>
            <Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Nouvelle note
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-6">
          {pinnedNotes.length > 0 && (
            <section className="space-y-3">
              <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-primary">
                <Pin className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Ã‰pinglÃ©es
              </h2>
              <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
                {pinnedNotes.map((note) => (
                  <NoteCard
                    key={note.id}
                    note={note}
                    onEdit={openEdit}
                    onDelete={setPendingDelete}
                    onTogglePin={togglePin}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            </section>
          )}

          {notes.length > 0 && (
            <section className="space-y-3">
              <h2 className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground">
                {pinnedNotes.length > 0 ? "Autres notes" : "Toutes les notes"}
              </h2>
              <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
                {notes.map((note) => (
                  <NoteCard
                    key={note.id}
                    note={note}
                    onEdit={openEdit}
                    onDelete={setPendingDelete}
                    onTogglePin={togglePin}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            </section>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      <NoteEditor
        open={open}
        onOpenChange={setOpen}
        form={form}
        setForm={setForm}
        saving={saving}
        onSave={save}
        isEditing={!!form.id}
      />

      <AlertDialog open={!!pendingDelete} onOpenChange={(isOpen) => !isOpen && setPendingDelete(null)}>
        <AlertDialogContent>
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle>Confirmer la suppression</AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription>
              La note Â« {pendingDelete?.title} Â» sera dÃ©finitivement supprimÃ©e.
            </AlertDialogDescription>
          </AlertDialogHeader>
          <AlertDialogFooter>
            <AlertDialogCancel>Annuler</AlertDialogCancel>
            <AlertDialogAction
              onClick={removeNote}
              className="bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground hover:bg-destructive/90"
            >
              Supprimer
            </AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "@/api/localClient";
import { AlertTriangle } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogCancel,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";
import BackupPanel from "@/components/BackupPanel";
import { deleteAccountData } from "@/lib/accountData";
import { getSelectedCampaignId, setSelectedCampaignId } from "@/lib/campaignStore";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/use-toast";
import PrintButton from "@/components/print/PrintButton";
import AutosavePanel from "@/components/AutosavePanel";
import CampaignSelect from "@/components/common/CampaignSelect";

export default function Settings() {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [campaigns, setCampaigns] = useState([]);
  const [campaignId, setCampaignId] = useState(getSelectedCampaignId());
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    api.entities.Campaign.filter({}, { sort: "-created_date", limit: 100 })
      .then((result) => {
        if (!alive) return;
        setCampaigns(result.items);
        const stored = getSelectedCampaignId();
        const selected = result.items.some((campaign) => campaign.id === stored)
          ? stored
          : result.items[0]?.id || "";
        setCampaignId(selected);
        setSelectedCampaignId(selected);
      })
      .catch((error) => {
        console.error("Chargement des campagnes impossible :", error);
        if (alive) toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Campagnes indisponibles", description: "Impossible de charger les campagnes locales." });
      });
    return () => { alive = false; };
  }, [toast]);

  const confirmDelete = async () => {
    setDeleting(true);
    try {
      await deleteAccountData();
      setSelectedCampaignId("");
      window.location.reload();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Effacement impossible :", error);
      setDeleting(false);
      setOpen(false);
      toast({
        title: "Effacement impossible",
        description: "Une erreur est survenue, rÃ©essayez dans un instant.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl space-y-6 px-4 py-6 md:px-8 md:py-8">
      <header>
        <span className="tag border border-primary/40 text-primary">Configuration</span>
        <h1 className="mt-3 font-display text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">ParamÃ¨tres</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 max-w-xl text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          Sauvegardez vos donnÃ©es de jeu ou repartez de zÃ©ro. Tout est stockÃ© dans ce navigateur.
        </p>
      </header>
      <AutosavePanel />
      <BackupPanel />

      <section className="space-y-3 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card/50 p-4">
        <h2 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-foreground">Impression</h2>
        <CampaignSelect
          campaigns={campaigns}
          value={campaignId}
          onValueChange={(id) => { setCampaignId(id); setSelectedCampaignId(id); }}
        />
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          Imprimez ou exportez en PDF Ã©ditable les scÃ¨nes, indices, lieux, PNJ, notes, tables, sessions et fiches de personnages de la campagne sÃ©lectionnÃ©e.
        </p>
        <PrintButton
          campaignId={campaignId}
          preset="all"
          label="Choisir les Ã©lÃ©ments"
          allowPdfExport
        />
      </section>

      <section className="rounded-xl border border-destructive/40 bg-destructive/[0.06] p-5">
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
          <AlertTriangle className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-destructive" />
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-foreground">Effacer toutes les donnÃ©es</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              Cette action supprime dÃ©finitivement toutes les donnÃ©es de ce navigateur : campagnes,
              actes, scÃ¨nes, PNJ, lieux, indices, personnages, modÃ¨les et catalogues de jeu,
              tables alÃ©atoires, sessions et notes.
            </p>
            <Button
              variant="destructive"
              className="mt-3"
              onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
              disabled={deleting}
            >
              {deleting ? "Effacement..." : "Effacer toutes les donnÃ©es"}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <AlertDialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
        <AlertDialogContent>
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle>Confirmer lâ€™effacement</AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription>
              Cette action est irrÃ©versible. Toutes vos donnÃ©es seront supprimÃ©es de ce navigateur.
            </AlertDialogDescription>
          </AlertDialogHeader>
          <AlertDialogFooter>
            <AlertDialogCancel disabled={deleting}>Annuler</AlertDialogCancel>
            <AlertDialogAction onClick={confirmDelete} disabled={deleting}>
              {deleting ? "Effacement..." : "Oui, tout effacer"}
            </AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>

      <section className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-card/50 p-4">
        <h2 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-foreground">Ã€ propos</h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          Arcanes &amp; ScÃ©nario Â· version{" "}
          <span className="font-mono text-foreground">{__APP_VERSION__}</span>
        </p>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "@/api/localClient";
import { Dices, Plus } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogCancel,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";
import TableCard from "@/components/tables/TableCard";
import TableEditor from "@/components/tables/TableEditor";
import { TABLE_CATEGORIES } from "@/lib/tableMeta";
import { getSelectedCampaignId, setSelectedCampaignId } from "@/lib/campaignStore";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/use-toast";
import { useDataRefresh } from "@/lib/RefreshContext";
import PrintButton from "@/components/print/PrintButton";
import CampaignSelect from "@/components/common/CampaignSelect";

const SafeAlertDialogContent = (
  /** @type {React.ComponentProps<typeof AlertDialogContent>} */ props
) => <AlertDialogContent {...props} />;

const EMPTY = /** @type {{ id?: string; name: string; category: string; description: string; entries: Array<{ text: string; weight: number }> }} */ ({
  id: undefined,
  name: "",
  category: "autre",
  description: "",
  entries: [],
});

export default function Tables() {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const { refreshKey: dataRefreshKey } = useDataRefresh();
  const [campaigns, setCampaigns] = useState(
    /** @type {Array<{ id: string; name: string }>} */([])
  );
  const [campaignId, setCampaignId] = useState(getSelectedCampaignId());
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("all");
  const [tables, setTables] = useState(
    /** @type {Array<{ id: string; name: string; category?: string; description?: string; entries?: Array<{ text?: string; weight?: number }> }>} */([])
  );
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [pendingDelete, setPendingDelete] = useState(
    /** @type {{ id: string; name: string; category?: string; description?: string; entries?: Array<{ text?: string; weight?: number }> } | null} */
    (null)
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    const run = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.entities.Campaign.filter({}, { sort: "-created_date", limit: 100 });
        if (!alive) return;
        const campaignList = /** @type {Array<{ id: string; name: string }>} */ (res.items || []);
        setCampaigns(campaignList);
        const stored = getSelectedCampaignId();
        const valid = campaignList.some((c) => c.id === stored) ? stored : campaignList[0]?.id || "";
        setCampaignId(valid);
        setSelectedCampaignId(valid);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Chargement des campagnes impossible :", error);
        if (alive) toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Campagnes indisponibles", description: "Impossible de charger les campagnes locales." });
      }
    };
    run();
    return () => { alive = false; };
  }, [dataRefreshKey, toast]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!campaignId) {
      setTables([]);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }
    let alive = true;
    setTables([]);
    const run = async () => {
      setLoading(true);
      const query = /** @type {Record<string, string>} */ ({ campaign_id: campaignId });
      if (category !== "all") query.category = category;
      try {
        const res = await api.entities.RollTable.filter(query, { sort: "-created_date", limit: 200 });
        if (!alive) return;
        const tableList = /** @type {Array<{ id: string; name: string; category?: string; description?: string; entries?: Array<{ text?: string; weight?: number }> }>} */ (res.items || []);
        setTables(tableList);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Chargement des tables impossible :", error);
        if (alive) toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Tables indisponibles", description: "Impossible de charger les tables de cette campagne." });
      } finally {
        if (alive) setLoading(false);
      }
    };
    run();
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [campaignId, category, dataRefreshKey, toast]);

  const openNew = () => {
    setForm({ ...EMPTY, entries: [] });
    setOpen(true);
  };

  const openEdit = (
    /** @type {{ id: string; name: string; category?: string; description?: string; entries?: Array<{ text?: string; weight?: number }> }} */
    table
  ) => {
    setForm({
      id: table.id,
      name: table.name || "",
      category: table.category || "autre",
      description: table.description || "",
      entries: (table.entries || []).map((entry) => ({ text: entry.text || "", weight: entry.weight ?? 1 })),
    });
    setOpen(true);
  };

  const applyTable = (
    /** @type {{ id: string; name: string; category?: string; description?: string; entries?: Array<{ text?: string; weight?: number }> }} */
    table,
    /** @type {string | undefined} */
    replacedId
  ) => {
    setTables((list) => {
      const rest = list.filter((t) => t.id !== table.id && t.id !== replacedId);
      const matches = category === "all" || (table.category || "autre") === category;
      return matches ? [table, ...rest] : rest;
    });
  };

  const save = async () => {
    if (!form.name.trim() || !campaignId) return;
    setSaving(true);
    const entries = (form.entries || [])
      .filter((entry) => entry.text?.trim())
      .map((entry) => ({
        text: entry.text.trim(),
        weight: Number(entry.weight) > 0 ? Number(entry.weight) : 1,
      }));
    const payload = {
      campaign_id: campaignId,
      name: form.name.trim(),
      category: form.category || "autre",
      description: form.description || "",
      entries,
    };
    try {
      const saved = form.id
        ? await api.entities.RollTable.update(form.id, payload)
        : await api.entities.RollTable.create(payload);
      console.log("RollTable enregistrÃ©e :", saved);
      if (saved?.id) {
        applyTable(saved, form.id);
      } else {
        // rÃ©ponse sans id : on recharge la liste depuis le serveur
        const query = /** @type {Record<string, string>} */ ({ campaign_id: campaignId });
        if (category !== "all") query.category = category;
        const res = await api.entities.RollTable.filter(query, { sort: "-created_date", limit: 200 });
        const tableList = /** @type {Array<{ id: string; name: string; category?: string; description?: string; entries?: Array<{ text?: string; weight?: number }> }>} */ (res.items || []);
        setTables(tableList);
      }
      setOpen(false);
      setForm({ ...EMPTY, entries: [] });
      toast({ title: form.id ? "Table mise Ã  jour" : "Table crÃ©Ã©e" });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Ã‰chec enregistrement RollTable :", error);
      toast({
        variant: "destructive",
        title: "Enregistrement impossible",
        description: "La table n'a pas Ã©tÃ© enregistrÃ©e.",
      });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const removeTable = async () => {
    const pending = pendingDelete;
    setPendingDelete(null);
    if (!pending) return;

    const pendingId = pending.id;
    const previous = tables;
    setTables((list) => list.filter((t) => t.id !== pendingId));
    try {
      await api.entities.RollTable.delete(pendingId);
      toast({ title: "Table supprimÃ©e" });
    } catch {
      setTables(previous);
      toast({
        variant: "destructive",
        title: "Suppression impossible",
        description: "La table a Ã©tÃ© rÃ©tablie.",
      });
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-6xl space-y-6 px-4 py-6 md:px-8 md:py-8">
      <header className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <span className="tag border border-primary/40 text-primary">Aide de jeu</span>
          <h1 className="mt-3 font-display text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">Tables alÃ©atoires</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 max-w-xl text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            PrÃ©parez vos rencontres, noms et Ã©vÃ©nements, puis tirez au hasard quand vos joueurs
            partent hors des sentiers battus.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex w-full items-center gap-2 sm:w-auto">
          <CampaignSelect
            campaigns={campaigns}
            value={campaignId}
            onValueChange={(id) => { setCampaignId(id); setSelectedCampaignId(id); }}
          />
          <PrintButton campaignId={campaignId} preset="tables" />
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={openNew}
            disabled={!campaignId}
            className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md bg-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground shadow transition-colors hover:bg-primary/90 disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            <Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Nouvelle table
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setCategory("all")}
          className={cn(
            "tag border transition-colors",
            category === "all" ? "border-primary/50 bg-primary/15 text-primary" : "border-border text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
          )}
        >
          Toutes
        </button>
        {TABLE_CATEGORIES.map((c) => (
          <button
            key={c.key}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setCategory(c.key)}
            className={cn(
              "tag border transition-colors",
              category === c.key ? "border-primary/50 bg-primary/15 text-primary" : "border-border text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
            )}
          >
            {c.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {!campaignId ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border p-10 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          CrÃ©ez une campagne depuis l'arborescence pour commencer vos tables.
        </div>
      ) : loading ? (
        <p className="py-10 text-center font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground">
          Chargementâ€¦
        </p>
      ) : tables.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border p-10 text-center">
          <Dices className="mx-auto h-7 w-7 text-primary" />
          <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            {category === "all"
              ? "Aucune table pour le moment."
              : "Aucune table dans cette catÃ©gorie."}
          </p>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="mt-5 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md bg-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground shadow transition-colors hover:bg-primary/90 disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50"
            onClick={openNew}
          >
            <Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Nouvelle table
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {tables.map((table) => (
            <TableCard
              key={table.id}
              table={table}
              onEdit={openEdit}
              onDelete={(t) => setPendingDelete(t)}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <TableEditor
        open={open}
        onOpenChange={setOpen}
        form={form}
        setForm={setForm}
        saving={saving}
        onSave={save}
      />

      <AlertDialog open={!!pendingDelete} onOpenChange={(isOpen) => !isOpen && setPendingDelete(null)}>
        <SafeAlertDialogContent>
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle>Confirmer la suppression</AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription>
              La table Â« {pendingDelete?.name} Â» et toutes ses entrÃ©es seront dÃ©finitivement supprimÃ©es.
            </AlertDialogDescription>
          </AlertDialogHeader>
          <AlertDialogFooter>
            <AlertDialogCancel>Annuler</AlertDialogCancel>
            <AlertDialogAction
              onClick={removeTable}
              className="bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground hover:bg-destructive/90"
            >
              Supprimer
            </AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
        </SafeAlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>
    </div>
  );
}
=== src\App.jsx ===
import { Suspense, lazy } from "react";
import { ThemeProvider } from "next-themes";
import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/toaster";
import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { queryClientInstance } from "@/lib/query-client";
import { HashRouter as Router, Route, Routes } from "react-router-dom";
import PageNotFound from "./lib/PageNotFound";
import { AuthProvider } from "@/lib/AuthContext";
import ScrollToTop from "./components/ScrollToTop";
import Layout from "@/components/Layout";
import RestorePrompt from "@/components/RestorePrompt";

const Home = lazy(() => import("@/pages/Home"));
const Codex = lazy(() => import("@/pages/Codex"));
const Tables = lazy(() => import("@/pages/Tables"));
const Calendrier = lazy(() => import("@/pages/Calendrier"));
const Notes = lazy(() => import("@/pages/Notes"));
const Settings = lazy(() => import("@/pages/Settings"));
const Game = lazy(() => import("@/pages/Jeu"));

function App() {
  return (
    <ThemeProvider attribute="class" defaultTheme="dark" enableSystem>
      <AuthProvider>
        <QueryClientProvider client={queryClientInstance}>
          <Router>
            <ScrollToTop />
            <Suspense fallback={null}>
              <Routes>
                <Route element={<Layout />}>
                  <Route path="/" element={<Home />} />
                  <Route path="/codex" element={<Codex />} />
                  <Route path="/tables" element={<Tables />} />
                  <Route path="/calendrier" element={<Calendrier />} />
                  <Route path="/notes" element={<Notes />} />
                  <Route path="/jeu" element={<Game />} />
                  <Route path="/settings" element={<Settings />} />
                </Route>
                <Route path="*" element={<PageNotFound />} />
              </Routes>
            </Suspense>
          </Router>
          <RestorePrompt />
          <Toaster />
        </QueryClientProvider>
      </AuthProvider>
    </ThemeProvider>
  );
}

export default App;

=== electron\main.cjs ===
const { app, BrowserWindow, Menu, shell, dialog, ipcMain } = require("electron");
const path = require("path");
const fs = require("fs");

const CURRENT = "arcanes-courante.json";
const SNAP_PREFIX = "arcanes-auto-";
const ARCHIVE_PREFIX = "arcanes-archive-";
const MAX_SNAPSHOTS = 10;
const SNAPSHOT_EVERY_MS = 30 * 60 * 1000;

let mainWindow = null;

// ---------- Configuration et dossier de sauvegarde ----------

const configPath = () => path.join(app.getPath("userData"), "config.json");

function readConfig() {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(configPath(), "utf8"));
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

function writeConfig(config) {
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(configPath()), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(configPath(), JSON.stringify(config, null, 2));
}

function backupFolder() {
  const folder =
    readConfig().backupFolder || path.join(app.getPath("documents"), "Arcanes Scenario", "Sauvegardes");
  fs.mkdirSync(folder, { recursive: true });
  return folder;
}

const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
const stamp = (d = new Date()) =>
  `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}_${pad(d.getHours())}h${pad(d.getMinutes())}m${pad(d.getSeconds())}`;

const isBackupName = (name) =>
  typeof name === "string" && name === path.basename(name) && /^arcanes-[\w.\-]+\.json$/.test(name);

function listBackups(folder) {
  return fs
    .readdirSync(folder)
    .filter(isBackupName)
    .map((name) => {
      const stat = fs.statSync(path.join(folder, name));
      const kind =
        name === CURRENT
          ? "current"
          : name.startsWith(SNAP_PREFIX)
            ? "auto"
            : name.startsWith(ARCHIVE_PREFIX)
              ? "archive"
              : "other";
      return { name, kind, size: stat.size, mtime: stat.mtimeMs };
    })
    .sort((a, b) => b.mtime - a.mtime);
}

// Ã‰criture atomique : fichier temporaire, puis renommage.
function atomicWrite(file, content) {
  const tmp = file + ".tmp";
  fs.writeFileSync(tmp, content, "utf8");
  fs.renameSync(tmp, file);
}

// ---------- Ã‰changes avec l'interface ----------

ipcMain.handle("backup:info", () => {
  try {
    const folder = backupFolder();
    return { ok: true, folder, files: listBackups(folder) };
  } catch (error) {
    return { ok: false, error: String(error?.message || error) };
  }
});

ipcMain.handle("backup:write", (_event, json, options = {}) => {
  try {
    if (typeof json !== "string" || json.length < 2) throw new Error("Contenu invalide.");
    const folder = backupFolder();
    const current = path.join(folder, CURRENT);
    atomicWrite(current, json);

    const snapshots = listBackups(folder).filter((f) => f.kind === "auto");
    const lastSnapshot = snapshots[0]?.mtime || 0;
    if (options.force || Date.now() - lastSnapshot >= SNAPSHOT_EVERY_MS) {
      fs.copyFileSync(current, path.join(folder, `${SNAP_PREFIX}${stamp()}.json`));
      listBackups(folder)
        .filter((f) => f.kind === "auto")
        .slice(MAX_SNAPSHOTS)
        .forEach((f) => fs.unlinkSync(path.join(folder, f.name)));
    }
    return { ok: true, savedAt: Date.now(), folder };
  } catch (error) {
    return { ok: false, error: String(error?.message || error) };
  }
});

ipcMain.handle("backup:read", (_event, name) => {
  if (!isBackupName(name)) return null;
  try {
    return fs.readFileSync(path.join(backupFolder(), name), "utf8");
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
});

ipcMain.handle("backup:archive-current", () => {
  try {
    const folder = backupFolder();
    const current = path.join(folder, CURRENT);
    if (!fs.existsSync(current)) return { ok: true };
    fs.copyFileSync(current, path.join(folder, `${ARCHIVE_PREFIX}${stamp()}.json`));
    return { ok: true };
  } catch (error) {
    return { ok: false, error: String(error?.message || error) };
  }
});

ipcMain.handle("backup:choose-folder", async () => {
  const result = await dialog.showOpenDialog(mainWindow, {
    title: "Dossier des sauvegardes automatiques",
    properties: ["openDirectory", "createDirectory"],
  });
  if (result.canceled || !result.filePaths[0]) return null;
  writeConfig({ ...readConfig(), backupFolder: result.filePaths[0] });
  return result.filePaths[0];
});

ipcMain.handle("backup:open-folder", async () => {
  await shell.openPath(backupFolder());
});

ipcMain.handle("pdf:save", async (_event, bytes, filename) => {
  if (!(bytes instanceof Uint8Array) || bytes.length < 5 || Buffer.from(bytes.subarray(0, 5)).toString("ascii") !== "%PDF-") {
    return { ok: false, error: "Le document PDF est invalide." };
  }
  try {
    const safeName = path.basename(String(filename || "Document.pdf")).replace(/[<>:"/\\|?*\x00-\x1F]/g, "_");
    const result = await dialog.showSaveDialog(mainWindow, {
      title: "Enregistrer le PDF Ã©ditable",
      defaultPath: safeName.endsWith(".pdf") ? safeName : `${safeName}.pdf`,
      filters: [{ name: "Document PDF", extensions: ["pdf"] }],
    });
    if (result.canceled || !result.filePath) return { ok: true, canceled: true };
    fs.writeFileSync(result.filePath, Buffer.from(bytes));
    return { ok: true, filePath: result.filePath };
  } catch (error) {
    return { ok: false, error: String(error?.message || error) };
  }
});

// ---------- FenÃªtre ----------

function createWindow() {
  Menu.setApplicationMenu(null);
  mainWindow = new BrowserWindow({
    width: 1400,
    height: 900,
    minWidth: 900,
    minHeight: 600,
    backgroundColor: "#0b0b0f",
    autoHideMenuBar: true,
    icon: path.join(__dirname, "build", "icon.png"),
    webPreferences: {
      contextIsolation: true,
      nodeIntegration: false,
      preload: path.join(__dirname, "preload.cjs"),
    },
  });

  mainWindow.loadFile(path.join(__dirname, "..", "dist", "index.html"));

  // Liens externes dans le navigateur ; PDF et images (blob:) dans une fenÃªtre de l'appli.
  mainWindow.webContents.setWindowOpenHandler(({ url }) => {
    if (url.startsWith("http")) {
      shell.openExternal(url);
      return { action: "deny" };
    }
    return { action: "allow" };
  });

  mainWindow.on("closed", () => {
    mainWindow = null;
  });
}

if (!app.requestSingleInstanceLock()) {
  app.quit();
} else {
  app.on("second-instance", () => {
    if (mainWindow) {
      if (mainWindow.isMinimized()) mainWindow.restore();
      mainWindow.focus();
    }
  });
  app.whenReady().then(createWindow);
  app.on("window-all-closed", () => app.quit());
}
=== electron\preload.cjs ===
const { contextBridge, ipcRenderer } = require("electron");

contextBridge.exposeInMainWorld("arcanesBackup", {
    info: () => ipcRenderer.invoke("backup:info"),
    write: (json, options) => ipcRenderer.invoke("backup:write", json, options),
    read: (name) => ipcRenderer.invoke("backup:read", name),
    archiveCurrent: () => ipcRenderer.invoke("backup:archive-current"),
    chooseFolder: () => ipcRenderer.invoke("backup:choose-folder"),
    openFolder: () => ipcRenderer.invoke("backup:open-folder"),
});

contextBridge.exposeInMainWorld("arcanesPdf", {
    save: (bytes, filename) => ipcRenderer.invoke("pdf:save", bytes, filename),
});
